Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关。摘要未涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测模型、轻量化方法或星上部署约束。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于机器人任务规划领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无交集。

Abstract: This paper presents a comprehensive methodology for implementing knowledge graphs in ROS 2 systems, aiming to enhance the efficiency and intelligence of autonomous robotic missions. The methodology encompasses several key steps: defining initial and target conditions, structuring tasks and subtasks, planning their sequence, representing task-related data in a knowledge graph, and designing the mission using a high-level language. Each step builds on the previous one to ensure a cohesive process from initial setup to final execution. A practical implementation within the Aerostack2 framework is demonstrated through a simulated search and rescue mission in a Gazebo environment, where drones autonomously locate a target. This implementation highlights the effectiveness of the methodology in improving decision-making and mission performance by leveraging knowledge graphs.

</details>

### [115] [Evolutionary AP Switch ON/OFF Techniques for Energy-efficient Cell-free Massive MIMO Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26209)
*Jan García-Morales,Alejandro de la Fuente,David Gualda,Leopoldo Carro-Calvo,Felip Riera-Palou,Guillem Femenias*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出两种基于进化算法的接入点选择策略（CGA和PDGA），用于无蜂窝大规模MIMO网络的能效优化。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是无线通信领域的无蜂窝大规模MIMO网络中的接入点选择与能效优化问题，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。既不属于遥感/地球观测场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及任何模型压缩或边缘部署方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：无线通信中的接入点（AP）激活选择；应用场景：无蜂窝大规模MIMO网络；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 不适用。论文不涉及深度学习模型架构，而是提出两种进化算法（CGA: constrained genetic algorithm, PDGA: Pareto-driven genetic algorithm）用于AP选择策略优化。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等指标。

Datasets and metrics: 仿真实验，非标准遥感/目标检测数据集。评价指标为频谱效率（spectral efficiency）和能量效率（energy efficiency）。

Experiments: 在CB和MMSE两种处理方案下进行仿真，与贪心基准方法对比，CGA和PDGA在能量效率和能谱效率权衡上均有提升。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的局限性信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于无线通信物理层优化问题，与遥感目标检测模型轻量化课题的研究范畴完全不同。

Abstract: Cell-free massive multiple input multiple output (CF-mMIMO) is an emerging technology for next-generation wireless systems, where dynamically adapting the set of active access points (APs) is crucial to balance quality of service (QoS) requirements and network energy consumption under highly time-varying and spatially non-uniform traffic loads. Existing AP ON/OFF mechanisms--typically based on worst-case dimensioning or greedy heuristics--explore the combinatorial activation space inadequately, leading to suboptimal energy-efficiency outcomes. This paper introduces two evolutionary AP-selection strategies tailored to CF-mMIMO networks. The first, a constrained genetic algorithm (CGA), identifies the near-optimal subset of active APs for any fixed activation cardinality, while an outer search determines the globally optimal operating point. The second, a Pareto-driven genetic algorithm (PDGA), jointly optimizes spectral and energy efficiency by evolving a Pareto front over all feasible activation patterns. A detailed computational-complexity analysis is provided for both techniques. Simulations conducted under realistic spatially inhomogeneous traffic and considering both conjugate beamforming (CB) and minimum mean square error (MMSE) processing confirm consistent performance gains. The proposed methods consistently outperform state-of-the-art greedy benchmarks, delivering noticeable improvements in energy efficiency for both CB and MMSE schemes, while simultaneously enhancing the energy-spectral efficiency tradeoff, which is typically difficult to improve without incurring penalties elsewhere. These results highlight the strong potential of evolutionary optimization as a powerful and reliable approach for energy-efficient CF-mMIMO deployments.

</details>


### [116] [SWIPT-Enhanced Cell-Free Massive MIMO Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26303)
*Guillem Femenias,Jan García-Morales,Felip Riera-Palou*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种SWIPT增强的无小区大规模MIMO网络，研究下行能量收集与上行数据传输的联合优化问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文属于无线通信领域，研究SWIPT（无线信息与能量同时传输）在无小区大规模MIMO网络中的频谱效率和能量效率优化，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文讨论的是无线通信网络架构（无小区大规模MIMO），而非深度学习模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等任何模型轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上部署、边缘部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等部署相关内容。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文使用数值仿真结果展示频谱效率、能量效率、收集能量等通信指标，不涉及遥感目标检测数据集。

Limitations: 该论文与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法提供任何相关局限性分析。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于无线通信物理层优化方向，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交叉。

Abstract: Simultaneous wireless information and power transfer (SWIPT) has been advocated as a highly promising technology to provide near-perpetual operation to low-powered wireless devices in Internet-of-Things (IoT)-based wireless networks. In this paper, a SWIPT-enhanced cell-free massive MIMO network is proposed. In such a network, a large set of spatially distributed access points (APs) interconnected via a central processing unit (CPU) can collaboratively serve a large number of both energy harvesting mobile stations (MSs) (requiring wireless energy transfer) and conventional MSs (not requiring wireless energy transfer) on the same time-frequency resources. We consider spatially correlated Rician fading channels and the use of different precoding schemes that are based on different channel estimators differing on the assumed knowledge of the line-of-sight component. Mathematically manageable expressions are derived for the harvested energy during the downlink (DL) energy harvesting phase and the achievable spectral and energy efficiencies during the uplink (UL) payload transmission phase. A coupled UL/DL optimization problem is formulated aiming at finding the power control coefficients that maximize the minimum of the weighted achievable UL signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratios (SINRs) of all MSs. Extensive numerical results are presented that serve to highlight the existing trade-offs among the achievable spectral and energy efficiencies, the harvested energy, the energy dedicated to UL pilot transmission or the system configuration.

</details>


### [117] [Joint Beamforming, Energy Management, and Trajectory Optimization for Figure-Eight Loitering in Solar-Powered HAPS-Enabled ISAC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26325)
*Xue Zhang,Bang Huang,Mohamed-Slim Alouini*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种太阳能高空平台站(HAPS)的通信-感知-能量联合优化框架，用于昼夜持续运行的集成感知与通信(ISAC)，重点优化通信性能与推进能耗。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。虽然涉及SAR成像（遥感相关），但论文核心是通信-感知-能量联合优化的资源分配问题，不涉及目标检测模型，也不涉及模型轻量化或星上部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：集成感知与通信(ISAC)资源优化；应用场景：太阳能高空平台站(HAPS)昼夜持续运行；传感器类型：SAR（合成孔径雷达）；目标类别：未涉及具体目标检测任务。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及深度学习模型或神经网络架构，而是提出优化框架和迭代算法求解非凸优化问题。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标，其"能耗"讨论的是HAPS推进能耗而非计算能耗。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文通过仿真验证方法有效性，未使用标准遥感目标检测数据集。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化方向完全无关。论文属于通信与资源优化领域，不涉及目标检测模型设计、训练或推理优化。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能的弱关联是：该论文讨论的HAPS平台可作为遥感载荷的搭载平台，但其优化目标（通信速率、推进能耗）与在轨目标检测模型轻量化的核心问题（检测精度、推理速度、模型大小）完全不同。

Abstract: Solar-powered high-altitude platform stations (HAPSs) provide a promising platform for integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) owing to their wide-area coverage and long-endurance operation. This paper proposes a solar-powered HAPS-enabled ISAC framework for sustainable day-night operation, where a figure-eight loitering architecture is adopted to provide persistent ISAC services over geographically separated regions while harvesting solar energy. A unified communication-sensing-energy model is developed by jointly characterizing solar energy harvesting, battery dynamics, propulsion power consumption, communication transmission, and synthetic aperture radar (SAR) imaging. Based on this model, coupled optimization problems are formulated for daytime operation (DTO) and nighttime operation (NTO), where the battery state bridges the two operational phases through a long-term energy budget. The proposed framework jointly optimizes communication, sensing, mobility, and energy management to maximize daytime communication performance while minimizing nighttime propulsion energy consumption. Efficient iterative algorithms are developed to solve the resulting non-convex optimization problems. Simulation results verify the effectiveness of the proposed communication-sensing-energy co-design and demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively supports sustainable day-night ISAC operation.

</details>


### [118] [MVLA-GR: A Phase-Free Multipath-Based Geometry Reconstruction Method via Multi-View Likelihood Accumulation for ISAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26421)
*Bowei Xing,Yuxiang Zhang,Jianhua Zhang,Yifeng Xiong,Hongbo Xing,Li Yu,Guangyi Liu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于信道脉冲响应(CIR)的多视图似然累积几何重建方法(MVLA-GR)，用于ISAC系统中无需相位信息的目标几何重建。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文属于无线通信与感知一体化(ISAC)领域，解决的是通信信号中的目标几何重建问题，不涉及遥感图像、卫星平台、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分，与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：ISAC环境中的目标几何重建；场景：无线通信感知；传感器：通信基站/毫米波信道；目标类别：周围物体/车辆几何形状。不涉及遥感卫星、光学/SAR/高光谱图像。

Model architecture: MVLA-GR方法：基于CIR测量，提取主多径分量，对每个候选空间位置跨视图累积匹配传播距离的分量，使用软距离匹配核容忍测距误差，以接收功率作为可靠性权重，联合阈值策略生成二值几何估计。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及轻量化方法。仅提到"low-complexity phase-free solution"，但这是方法本身的特性，并非模型压缩/加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署信息。仅提及"low-complexity"，但未给出量化指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：射线追踪仿真数据（规范目标与复杂目标）+ 36 GHz真实车辆测量数据。评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标名称。

Experiments: 在射线追踪仿真和36 GHz真实车辆测量上验证，表明MVLA-GR能有效恢复目标几何。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 不涉及任何目标检测模型；3. 不涉及任何轻量化技术；4. 不涉及图像/视觉处理，而是无线信道信号处理。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题无直接可借鉴点。弱相关点：多视图信息融合的思想在遥感目标检测中也有应用（如多角度遥感），但方法本质差异较大，借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) enables wireless systems to reuse communication signals for environmental sensing, where reconstructing the geometry of surrounding objects is a representative sensing task. However, many conventional methods rely on coherent processing and require accurate phase information, which is often hard to guarantee in practical communication systems, particularly at high carrier frequencies. To address this problem, this paper proposes a Multi-View Likelihood Accumulation Geometry Reconstruction (MVLA-GR) method based on channel impulse response (CIR) measurements, which uses only delay and power observations without requiring phase information. The method extracts dominant multipath components from each observation, and for each candidate spatial location, accumulates components across views whose propagation distances match the location as supporting evidence. A soft distance-matching kernel is introduced to tolerate range estimation errors and viewpoint-dependent scattering migration, and the received power of each component is used as a reliability weight. A joint thresholding strategy combining response magnitude and angular support continuity then converts the continuous support map into a binary geometry estimate. Ray-tracing simulations on canonical and complex targets, as well as real-world vehicle measurements at 36 GHz, demonstrate that MVLA-GR can effectively recover target geometry, providing a low-complexity phase-free solution for ISAC.

</details>


### [119] [Energy-Efficient Access-Point Sleep-Mode Techniques for Cell-Free mmWave Massive MIMO Networks With Non-Uniform Spatial Traffic Density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26449)
*Jan García-Morales,Guillem Femenias,Felip Riera-Palou*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出面向无小区毫米波大规模MIMO网络的能效AP休眠策略，基于拟合优度检验动态开关接入点以适应非均匀空间流量分布。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文属于无线通信（B5G/6G）领域的无小区大规模MIMO网络能效优化问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：AP开关策略优化；应用场景：无小区毫米波大规模MIMO网络；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体的神经网络模型架构，论文讨论的是通信系统中的AP开关策略（ASO策略），非深度学习模型。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文讨论的是通信网络层面的AP休眠/唤醒能效优化策略，与模型剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法无关。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。论文讨论的是地面通信基站（AP）的开关策略，与遥感卫星在轨部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集。评价指标：能效（energy efficiency）、服务质量（QoS）。

Experiments: 数值结果表明，在非均匀空间流量分布下，基于GoF的ASO策略能显著提升能效。但未与遥感目标检测方法进行对比。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无任何可参考的局限性信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于无线通信物理层/资源分配方向，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Cell-free massive multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) is a novel beyond 5G (B5G) and 6G paradigm that, through the use of a common central processing unit (CPU), coordinates a large number of distributed access points (APs) to coherently serve mobile stations (MSs) on the same time/frequency resource. By exploiting the characteristics of new less-congested millimeter wave (mmWave) frequency bands, these networks can improve the overall system spectral and energy efficiencies by using low-complexity hybrid precoders/decoders. For this purpose, the system must be correctly dimensioned to provide the required quality of service (QoS) to MSs under different traffic load conditions. However, only heavy traffic load conditions are usually taken into account when analysing these networks and, thus, many APs might be underutilized during low traffic load periods, leading to an inefficient use of resources and waste of energy. Aiming at the implementation of energy-efficient AP switch on/off strategies, several approaches have been proposed in the literature that only consider rather unrealistic uniform spatial traffic distribution in the whole coverage area. Unlike prior works, this paper proposes energy efficient AP sleep-mode techniques for cell-free mmWave massive MIMO networks that are able to capture the inhomogeneous nature of spatial traffic distribution in realistic wireless networks. The proposed framework considers, analyzes and compares different AP switch ON-OFF (ASO) strategies that, based on the use of goodness-of-fit (GoF) tests, are specifically designed to dynamically turn on/off APs to adapt to both the number and the statistical distribution of MSs in the network. Numerical results show that the use of properly designed GoF-based ASO strategies under a non-uniform spatial traffic distribution can serve to considerably improve the achievable energy efficiency.

</details>


### [120] [Calibrating the Digital Twin Channel: Statistics-Consistent Sim-to-Lab Adaptation for W-Band Industrial OFDM Links](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26501)
*Pulok Tarafder,Abigail O. Oyekola,Jasni Areepatta Mannil,Imtiaz Ahmed,Zoheb Hassan,Danda B. Rawat,Wenjie Che*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种统计一致性仿真-实验室适配框架(SC-SLA)，利用GAN启发的循环一致性架构校准95 GHz射线追踪信道孪生模型，使其信道频率响应与实测数据统计特性对齐。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于工业无线网络中的数字孪生信道校准问题，涉及毫米波(95 GHz)通信、射线追踪、OFDM测量等通信领域内容，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型压缩或星上部署等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：信道孪生(Channel Twin)校准，使仿真信道频率响应与实测数据统计特性一致。场景：工业无线网络、W-band(92-95 GHz)毫米波通信。传感器/数据类型：射线追踪仿真数据、实验室OFDM测量数据。目标类别：不涉及目标检测。

Model architecture: GAN启发的循环一致性架构，包含ResNet生成器，不使用判别器（非对抗性）。损失函数包括批级别的信道统计损失：PDP、子带PDP、均方根时延扩展(τ_rms)的矩和分位数、归一化均方误差(NMSE)。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未涉及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该工作面向地面工业无线网络的信道校准，不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：95 GHz实验室OFDM测量数据（50 MHz采样带宽），以及Sionna射线追踪仿真数据。评价指标：τ_rms Kolmogorov-Smirnov (KS)统计量、PDP误差、CFR幅度误差、NMSE。对比方法：FCNN、CNN1D、BiLSTM、UNet1D四种监督基线。

Experiments: SC-SLA将τ_rms KS统计量从0.86降至0.050（相对未校准射线追踪输入）；相比最强监督基线(UNet1D)的0.089降低44%至0.050。无需重新训练即可泛化至92-94 GHz载波，在所有基线中取得最低PDP和CFR幅度误差。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴。论文本身局限：仅验证了92-95 GHz频段，未说明对其他频段或更宽带宽的泛化能力；非对抗性GAN架构虽避免了训练不稳定，但统计损失的设计依赖领域知识。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，可借鉴点有限：(1) 循环一致性架构的思想可用于遥感图像域自适应（如仿真→真实遥感图像的风格迁移），但本文是信道数据而非图像；(2) 统计分布对齐的损失设计思路（如对齐PDP、时延扩展分布）可类比为遥感检测中特征分布对齐，但场景差异大；(3) 整体而言，对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接借鉴价值很低。

Abstract: Digital twins (DTs) can reduce over-the-air validation cost in industrial wireless networks, but their utility depends on the fidelity of the underlying channel twin (CT). At W-band, site-specific ray tracing captures deterministic propagation geometry, yet its channel frequency responses (CFRs) do not reproduce the small-scale impairments and capture-to-capture variability observed in laboratory orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM) measurements above 90 GHz. This paper proposes Statistics-Consistent Sim-to-Lab Adaptation (SC-SLA), a calibration framework that improves the fidelity of a 95 GHz Sionna ray-traced CT toward that of the testbed by aligning the mean power delay profile (PDP), the distribution of root-mean-square delay spread ($τ_{\mathrm{rms}}$), and per-subcarrier statistics at 50 MHz sampling bandwidth. SC-SLA uses a generative adversarial network (GAN)-inspired, cycle-consistent architecture with ResNet generators and batch-level channel-statistics losses on the PDP, sub-band PDP, $τ_{\mathrm{rms}}$ moments and quantiles, and normalized mean-square error (NMSE) of the mean CFR-magnitude profile. The framework is non-adversarial and requires neither paired simulated/measured samples nor discriminators. On held-out 95 GHz data, SC-SLA reduces the $τ_{\mathrm{rms}}$ Kolmogorov-Smirnov (KS) statistic from 0.86 to 0.050 relative to the impairment-augmented ray-traced input, and by 44% (from 0.089 to 0.050) relative to the strongest of four supervised baselines (FCNN, CNN1D, BiLSTM, and UNet1D). Without retraining, the same checkpoint also generalizes to 92-94 GHz carriers, where it achieves the lowest PDP and CFR-magnitude errors among all baselines while reducing the $τ_{\mathrm{rms}}$-distribution mismatch relative to the uncalibrated twin.

</details>


### [121] [A Data-Driven Vibration Analysis Framework for Micro-Motor Fault Diagnosis and Quality Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26519)
*Xuan Chen,Xinjun Zuo,Yancheng Bi,Shunli Yu,Yunqi Cao*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于振动信号和RF特征选择+SVM分类的电动牙刷微电机故障检测方法。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是电动牙刷微电机的振动故障检测，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等遥感领域内容；也不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测等视觉检测任务；虽然提到了"降低维度与计算开销"，但方法本质是传统机器学习特征选择+分类，与深度学习模型轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）无关。

Reading priority: 1分，与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：微电机故障检测（二分类：正常/缺陷）；场景：电动牙刷质量控制和状态监测；传感器：高精度加速度计采集振动信号；目标类别：微电机缺陷。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: RF（随机森林）进行特征重要性评估与选择，SVM（支持向量机）作为最终分类器。非深度学习模型，无CNN/Transformer等结构。

Lightweight method: 通过RF特征重要性排序选取Top-3特征构成最优特征子集，以降低维度、减少计算开销、避免过拟合。但这属于传统特征选择，并非深度学习模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。由于是传统机器学习（SVM+RF），模型本身计算量较小，但论文未讨论任何星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：自行采集的电动牙刷微电机振动信号数据（摘要未说明样本量）；评价指标：balanced accuracy 94.44%, defect recall 88.89%, defect F1-score 94.12%, MCC 93.74%, geometric mean 94.28%, AUC 100.00%。

Experiments: 仅报告了所提方法（RF特征选择+SVM）的最终分类性能指标，未与其它方法进行对比，未做消融实验。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. 仅针对特定电动牙刷微电机故障场景，泛化性未知；3. 无对比实验和消融实验；4. 传统机器学习方法，不涉及深度学习模型压缩与加速；5. 未讨论实时性、资源受限部署等关键问题。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无直接借鉴价值。唯一可参考的点是：在资源极度受限场景下，传统特征选择+轻量分类器（如SVM）的思路可作为极端轻量化的对比基线，但遥感在轨检测主流仍是深度学习方案，该论文的方法论不具参考意义。

Abstract: The reliability of the internal micro-motors is crucial for the performance and lifespan of electric toothbrushes. In this paper, a vibration-based fault detection method is proposed to identify micro-motor defects in electric toothbrushes. A dedicated signal acquisition device was designed and developed to capture the vibration signals of micro-motors using a high-precision accelerometer. To effectively characterize the micro-motor conditions, comprehensive features were extracted from the raw vibration data in both the time and frequency domains. A random forest (RF) algorithm was then employed to evaluate the importance of all extracted features. To better interpret the extracted features based on fault mechanisms, and to reduce dimensionality and computational overhead while avoiding overfitting, the top three features with the highest importance scores were selected to form the optimal feature subset. Finally, a support vector machine (SVM) model was utilized to classify the motor states based on the selected features. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method, combining RF-based feature selection and SVM classification, achieves outstanding diagnostic performance. Specifically, the model yields a balanced accuracy of 94.44%, a defect recall of 88.89%, a defect F1-score of 94.12%, a Matthews correlation coefficient of 93.74%, a geometric mean of 94.28%, and an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve of 100.00%. These robust metrics confirm that the proposed approach can accurately and efficiently detect micro-motor faults in electric toothbrushes, providing a practical and reliable solution for quality control and condition monitoring in manufacturing.

</details>


### [122] [Secure Relay Low-Altitude Networks via Hybrid Fixed-Position and Rotatable Antenna Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26531)
*Maolin Li,Qi Zhang,Riqing Chen,Wei Gao,Feng Shu,Liang Yang,Cunhua Pan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种混合固定位置与可旋转天线阵列的中继网络，利用可重构智能表面（RIS）为低空经济应用提供安全通信，通过联合波束赋形、功率分配和天线方向设计最大化最差情况下的保密速率。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是通信安全（物理层安全）中的中继网络天线设计与波束赋形问题，完全不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署。虽然提到了"低空经济"和"可重构智能表面"，但属于通信领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测无直接关联。

Reading priority: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 通信安全（物理层安全）、低空经济应用场景；涉及基站、中继站、地面用户、空中用户；不涉及遥感传感器、目标检测任务。

Model architecture: 混合固定位置天线阵列与可旋转天线阵列的中继网络；可重构智能表面（RIS）作为候选路径；联合波束赋形、功率分配、天线方向优化设计。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明轻量化方法。论文中提到的"低功耗"（71.4% power saving）和"节省天线"（55% antenna saving）是通信硬件层面的资源节省，并非模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明星上/边缘部署相关信息。无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等指标。

Datasets and metrics: 仿真验证（Simulation results）；评价指标为最差情况保密速率（worst-case secrecy rate, SR）。无标准遥感数据集。

Experiments: 单用户场景下提出逐步泄漏方案；多用户场景下提出基于DSAC-T（Distributional Soft Actor-Critic with Three refinements）的强化学习方案；相比各向同性天线，保密速率提升约2倍；系统实现约71.4%功耗节省、55%天线节省，可服务更多用户。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关，无法提供任何目标检测或模型轻量化方面的信息。该论文聚焦通信物理层安全，属于无线通信领域。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于通信领域，不涉及遥感图像处理、目标检测或深度学习模型轻量化。如果强行寻找间接关联，DSAC-T强化学习方法可能对资源分配问题有参考价值，但与在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化的核心问题（模型压缩、加速、边缘部署）无关。

Abstract: In this paper, a relay network with hybrid fixed-position and rotatable antenna arrays is proposed. The deployment of rotatable arrays in conventional relay networks is considered to provide more secure communications for low-altitude economy applications. Specifically, both the base station and the relay station are equipped with fixed-position antenna arrays and rotatable arrays to serve ground users and aerial users, respectively. To address the challenge of multi-user interference, a low-cost reconfigurable intelligent surface is exploited as a candidate path. Accordingly, under constraints on transmit power, user quality of service, rotatable range, and path selection, the objective is to maximize the worst-case secrecy rate (SR) through joint beamforming, power allocation, and rotatable antenna orientation design. First, the SR performance in the single-user scenario is investigated, and a step-by-step leakage-based scheme is proposed. Then, the general multi-user scenario is studied, and a Distributional Soft Actor-Critic with Three refinements (DSAC-T)-based learning scheme, which supports hybrid discrete and continuous actions, is proposed to maximize the worst-case SR. Simulation results validate the effectiveness of the proposed schemes. The proposed schemes achieve approximately a twofold improvement in SR performance compared to isotropic antennas. The proposed system achieves approximately 71.4\% power saving, 55\% antenna saving, and can serve more users.

</details>


### [123] [Stay or Switch: Online Conformal Bayesian Optimization Guided Fluid Antenna Configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26547)
*Gangyong Zhu,Jia Yan,Shijian Gao*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种面向空-地网络中流体天线系统(FAS)的在线共形贝叶斯优化(OCBO)算法，用于在考虑端口切换能耗代价下优化集成感知与通信(ISAC)性能。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是通信领域的流体天线系统(FAS)和集成感知与通信(ISAC)，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感核心内容；不涉及目标检测任务；不涉及模型轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘/星上部署等任何轻量化或部署技术。虽然摘要中出现了"sensing"和"air-ground networks"，但这里的sensing是通信感知一体化中的感知功能，而非遥感成像或目标检测。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：通信感知一体化(ISAC)中的端口切换决策优化；应用场景：空-地网络中的流体天线系统；传感器类型：不涉及遥感传感器；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体的神经网络模型结构。核心方法是OCBO（在线共形贝叶斯优化）算法，属于优化算法而非深度学习模型。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何模型轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Abstract: Fluid antenna systems (FAS) introduce additional spatial degrees of freedom to enable integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) in air-ground networks. However, conventional studies often overlook or simplify the physical overheads and switching costs of FAS. In practice, port switching incurs non-negligible time, during which communication and sensing may continue but with potentially degraded slot-level performance. This leads to two key challenges: (1) the characterization of a slot-level, cost-aware ISAC metric is difficult, and (2) the large port space and accompanying abrupt environmental variations demand more reliable online decision-making. To address these challenges, a cost-aware multi-objective FAS switching problem is formulated, jointly considering slot-level ISAC performance and switching energy. The online conformal Bayesian optimization (OCBO) algorithm is then proposed to learn the unknown gray-box ISAC objectives and calibrate surrogate uncertainty for robust stay-or-switch decisions. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed cost-aware optimization framework achieves substantially improved long-term ISAC performance compared to existing baselines.

</details>

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测方向完全无关，无可借鉴的检测或轻量化技术；2. 实验基于特定信道分布，泛化性未知；3. 摘要未说明计算复杂度、实时性等关键部署指标。

Abstract: This paper investigates multi-user localization in uplink wireless systems assisted by electromagnetically reconfigurable antennas (ERAs). Unlike traditional localization schemes, we formulate an active sensing problem where a base station (BS) exploits historical pilot observations accumulated over previous sensing stages to adapt the shared ERA configuration and progressively refine position estimates. To capture both theoretical flexibility and practical hardware constraints, we establish a unified wideband geometric signal model accommodating two complementary ERA paradigms: a synthesis-based model utilizing spherical-harmonic basis functions, and a finite-state model based on measured radiation codebooks. Because analytically solving the resulting joint design problem is highly intractable due to the high-dimensional observation and the shared-aperture coupling among multiple users, we develop a learning-based active sensing framework. Specifically, pilot-matched wideband observations are compressed into compact user-wise features and sequentially accumulated by a long short-term memory (LSTM) module. These temporal features are then processed by a graph neural network (GNN) to capture multi-user shared-aperture coupling. Model-specific output heads generate either continuous synthesis coefficients or finite-state ERA selections, while a localization head produces stage-wise position estimates. Numerical results under a specific channel distribution show that the proposed ERA-assisted active sensing framework achieves progressive localization refinement across sensing stages and obtains better performance than conventional non-reconfigurable arrays and representative ablation baselines.

</details>

*Luca Barbieri,Gianluca Fontanesi,Lorenzo Galati Giordano,Alfonso Fernandez Duran,Thorsten Wild*

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化均无任何关联。研究场景是Wi-Fi网络流量预测，方法为聚类联邦学习，属于无线通信与分布式机器学习领域，与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文讨论的是通信开销和能耗的优化，但这是联邦学习场景下的分布式通信效率问题，并非模型轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）方法。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集。评价指标：预测性能（具体指标未说明）、通信开销、能耗开销。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。但该工作聚焦Wi-Fi网络流量预测，与遥感在轨目标检测场景完全不相关，其聚类联邦学习方法在遥感卫星场景下的适用性未知。

Abstract: Centrally-managed Wi-Fi solutions are increasingly leveraging Distributed Artificial Intelligence (AI) to predict key operational statistics of Access Points (APs) and proactively optimize network performance. In this context, Clustered Federated Learning (CFL) represents a fitting methodology, enabling the generation of multiple AI models that account for diverse statistical properties of the APs data distribution. However, identifying informative clusters for grouping APs models remains a significant challenge. In this paper, we address this problem by proposing a novel CFL tool integrating a two step clustering procedure. Initially, multiple clustering solutions are generated and filtered based on a minimum set of desired clustering criteria. Subsequently, if no solutions meet sufficient quality metrics, a global model is produced by aggregating all AP models. Otherwise, the final clustering solution is selected as the one that maximizes the informativeness (quantified via differential entropy) for the smallest cluster. Our results, focusing on a Wi-Fi traffic prediction problem, demonstrate that the developed CFL tool achieves the best predictive performance among all evaluated distributed strategies and the lowest communication and energy footprint among the clustered ones, exceeding the cost of single-model FL only in the regimes where it markedly improves accuracy.

</details>

Abstract: This paper presents the analysis, design, fabrication, and experimental validation of a dual-mode frequency/amplitude modulator based on a time-modulated varactor diode. By exploiting the varactor as a time-varying capacitor in combination with an operational amplifier configured as an inverting integrator and a passband filter, the proposed circuit generates frequency-modulated (FM) signals in an efficient manner. Amplitude-modulated (AM) signals can also be obtained with a simple modification. The implementation, realized in microstrip technology, leverages the unique properties of time-modulated electronic components, particularly their inherent frequency-mixing capability. Analytical expressions are derived to predict the characteristics of the generated waveforms, and their accuracy is verified through numerical simulations performed in Keysight ADS. A microstrip PCB prototype is then fabricated and experimentally characterized. The measured results show excellent agreement with both the theoretical predictions and the numerical simulations. The proposed approach demonstrates the potential of time-varying capacitors as an attractive alternative to conventional FM techniques for telecommunications and radar applications.

</details>


### [127] [Efficient Channel Prediction based on Gram-Square-Root Factorization using GMMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26959)

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于无线通信领域的MIMO-OFDM信道状态信息（CSI）预测与参数压缩，完全不涉及遥感图像、卫星观测、目标检测或模型轻量化部署。虽然文中提到了"parameter reduction"（参数约简）和"low complexity"（低复杂度），但这些是针对通信信道矩阵的维度压缩，与深度学习模型剪枝/量化/蒸馏等轻量化技术无关。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标为预测精度（prediction accuracy）和信道子空间捕获能力（correctly capture the underlying channel subspaces）。对比方法包括ZOH、FOH、LMMSE和NN-based predictor。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与本人课题无关，无直接可借鉴点。但可注意一点：通信领域中的"参数约简"思路（利用结构化先验降低维度）在遥感模型轻量化中也有类似思想（如结构化剪枝、低秩分解），不过具体技术路线完全不同，不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Accurate channel state information (CSI) is critical for downlink (DL)-multi-user (MU)-multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems, where feedback delays and mobility can degrade precoding performance. To ensure reliable beamforming and interference mitigation, CSI prediction is required. In practical systems, full CSI feedback is often infeasible due to signaling overhead, so transmitters rely on partial CSI reported by the receivers. In this work, we propose a Gaussian mixture model (GMM)-based prediction framework for MIMO-orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM) channels under partial feedback using Gram-square-root factorization. To address the high dimensionality, we introduce an efficient parameter reduction technique that exploits structured covariance matrices, significantly lowering complexity without noticeable performance degradation. This reduction is based on the Gram-square-root factorization and remains of interest even when full CSI is available. Simulation results demonstrate that GMMs achieve the highest prediction accuracy and correctly capture the underlying channel subspaces, which is essential for effective MU-precoding. The proposed method outperforms classical baselines such as zero-order hold (ZOH), first-order hold (FOH), and linear minimum mean squared error (LMMSE) predictors, and an advanced neural network (NN)-based predictor. Notably, the parameter-reduced partial CSI GMM achieves performance comparable to that of full CSI prediction, highlighting its ability to efficiently model the channel structure under limited feedback.

</details>


### [128] [Probabilistic Denoising-Enhanced ISAC for Stochastic Cluttered Mobile Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26994)
*Nghia Thinh Nguyen,Tri Nhu Do*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出PDISAC框架，利用多比特时隙分割ISAC波形实现通信与感知一体化，并设计轻量级概率去噪网络RDPDNet抑制数据引入的旁瓣噪声，提升雷达感知性能。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于**通信感知一体化（ISAC）**中的雷达波形设计与去噪网络，属于无线通信/雷达信号处理领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱图像，也不涉及目标检测（文中CFAR检测是雷达信号处理层面的检测，非视觉目标检测），更不涉及遥感模型轻量化或星上部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。除非课题涉及ISAC与遥感交叉的极端特殊情况，否则可忽略。

Model architecture: RDPDNet：轻量级概率去噪网络，插入在RD图生成与CFAR检测之间；训练采用对抗性频率混合机制（adversarial frequency-mixup）。摘要未说明具体网络结构细节（层数、卷积类型等）。

Lightweight method: 文中提到RDPDNet是"lightweight probabilistic denoising network"，但摘要未说明具体的轻量化手段（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等），也未给出参数量、FLOPs等量化指标。轻量化的含义仅体现在网络规模较小，而非系统性的模型压缩方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关指标。虽然网络被称为轻量级，但缺乏量化数据支撑星上或边缘部署可行性。且应用场景为地面城市几何环境，非星上场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：基于真实城市几何的仿真数据（realistic urban geometry），非公开遥感数据集；评价指标：RMSE（均方根误差）、BER（误码率）、平均容量（average capacity）、信噪比（SNR）下的性能对比。摘要未说明遥感领域常用指标如mAP、F1、IoU等。

Experiments: 通过解析与数值结果展示：RDPDNet在低SNR下显著降低RMSE，吸收大部分数据嵌入带来的感知性能损失；传统无数据链在高SNR下达到基准性能；增加时隙分配提升数据速率但增加BER，体现感知-通信可调权衡。摘要未说明与SOTA方法的对比实验或消融实验细节。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/在轨目标检测完全无关，属于雷达ISAC领域；2. 轻量化仅停留在"轻量网络"的定性描述，缺乏参数量、FLOPs等量化指标；3. 仅在仿真城市几何数据上验证，无真实遥感数据实验；4. 未涉及任何视觉目标检测任务（如舰船、飞机、车辆检测）；5. 未讨论星上部署、边缘推理等场景。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义非常有限。可能的间接启发：1）概率去噪思路可迁移到遥感图像去噪/增强预处理环节；2）对抗性频率混合机制可能对遥感图像中的频域噪声抑制有参考价值；3）"轻量级去噪网络"的设计理念可类比遥感检测中的轻量级backbone设计。但整体而言，该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题方向匹配度极低。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose Probabilistic Denoising ISAC (PDISAC), a framework built on a multi-bit slot-partitioned ISAC waveform: by partitioning each maximal-length sequence into alternating pilot and data slots, we embed multiple bits per sequence through symbol-level spreading, multiplying the data rate while every chip retains the deterministic radar code. The added throughput, however, injects data-dependent, non-white sidelobes into the range-Doppler (RD) heatmap that degrade matched-filter (MF) sensing. Rather than modifying the MF receiver, we develop RDPDNet, a lightweight probabilistic denoising network inserted between RD-map formation and constant-false-alarm-rate detection; training it with an adversarial frequency-mixup mechanism, we suppress the data-induced sidelobes and thermal noise without knowledge of the embedded symbols. We further characterize the statistics of the geometry-determined channel. The fundamental performance limits of the design are then analyzed through an analytical lower bound, a semi-analytical bit error rate (BER), and an average capacity that tie the slot allocation and sequence length to the sensing-communication trade-off. Through analytical and numerical results over a realistic urban geometry, we show that RDPDNet absorbs most of the data-embedding sensing penalty and markedly lowers the RMSE at low SNR, while the conventional data-free chain attains the bias-adjusted benchmark at high SNR. Moreover, increasing the slot allocation raises the data rate at the expense of a higher BER, exposing a tunable sensing--communication trade-off.

</details>


### [129] [Two-Filter Adaptive Gaussian Mixture Smoothing for Nonlinear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27151)
*Benjamin Schneiderheinze,Andrea De Vittori,Keith A. LeGrand,Jill Bruer*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种面向非线性非高斯系统的递归贝叶斯平滑算法，通过自适应高斯混合滤波器与双滤波器平滑框架改进空间目标跟踪精度，在Molniya轨道和地月halo轨道上验证了效果。

Research relevance: 1分。论文涉及"space object tracking"（空间目标跟踪），但这是轨道力学层面的状态估计问题，与遥感成像目标检测（object detection in remote sensing images）完全不同。不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、轻量化或星上部署。属于航天动力学/导航滤波方向，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：空间目标跟踪（state estimation/tracking of space objects），属于轨道动力学中的滤波与平滑问题。场景：Molniya轨道、地月halo轨道。传感器类型：摘要未说明。目标类别：空间飞行器/轨道物体。不涉及遥感图像目标检测。

Model architecture: 自适应高斯混合滤波器（Adaptive Gaussian Mixture Filter）+ 双滤波器平滑框架（two-filter smoother）。包含分裂（splitting）、合并（merging）、递归测量更新（recursive measurement updating）等技术。属于贝叶斯滤波框架，非深度学习模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明轻量化方法。文中提到的"splitting, merging, recursive measurement updating"是为了提高近似精度和计算效率，但并非模型轻量化技术（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。虽然"space object tracking"本身可能涉及星上计算，但本文未讨论在轨部署可行性或实时推理约束。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Molniya轨道和地月halo轨道仿真场景（非公开遥感图像数据集）。评价指标：估计误差（estimation error）和不确定性（uncertainty）的降低程度。摘要未说明具体数值指标。

Experiments: 在Molniya轨道和地月halo轨道两个空间目标跟踪问题上验证，结果表明所提平滑器相比前向滤波器显著降低了估计误差和不确定性。摘要未提供与其他平滑方法的对比。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感图像目标检测完全无关，不涉及任何视觉检测任务。2. 未讨论计算资源约束或实时性要求。3. 仅在仿真轨道数据上验证，未涉及真实遥感图像或传感器数据。4. 未提供轻量化或边缘部署相关分析。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。该论文属于航天动力学中的状态估计与平滑滤波方向，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题在问题定义、技术路线、评价体系上均无交集。不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Space object tracking poses challenging estimation problems due to the significantly non-Gaussian distributions that can arise, particularly under highly nonlinear dynamics or during periods of measurement unavailability. Adaptive Gaussian mixture filters can dynamically adjust their mixture resolution to systematically approximate these non-Gaussian distributions, but challenging estimation problems can still produce highly uncertain or inaccurate estimates, especially during prolonged observation gaps. Smoothing algorithms can significantly improve filtered estimates by incorporating future measurement information for applications where immediacy is not required, but smoothing in nonlinear, non-Gaussian settings poses additional theoretical and computational challenges. This work develops a new recursive Bayesian smoothing algorithm for nonlinear systems that refines Gaussian mixture posteriors produced by a forward adaptive Gaussian mixture filter. A two-filter smoothing approach approximates the future measurement information by an information-form Gaussian mixture in the state variable. Techniques from nonlinear Gaussian mixture filtering including splitting, merging, and recursive measurement updating are also incorporated to improve the accuracy and computational efficiency of this approximation. The proposed smoother's estimation capabilities are demonstrated on space object tracking problems for Molniya and Earth-Moon halo orbits and shown to significantly reduce estimation error and uncertainty compared to the forward filter.

</details>

### [130] [Probing the Origins of Reasoning Performance: Representational Quality for Mathematical Problem-Solving in RL vs. SFT Fine-Tuned Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26119)
*Antyabha Rahman,Akshaj Gurugubelli,Omar Ankit,Kevin Zhu,Aishwarya Balwani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究RL训练与SFT训练的大推理模型在数学推理任务上的内部表征差异，发现RL模型具有更线性可分的隐藏状态和层次化关键层结构。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化，属于大语言模型推理机制的机理分析，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：数学推理（mathematical reasoning）的内部表征分析。应用场景：大语言模型（LLM）的RL与SFT训练对比。传感器类型：无。目标类别：无。

Model architecture: 大推理模型（large reasoning models），具体架构摘要未说明。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明任何部署相关信息（参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等均未提及）。

Datasets and metrics: 数学推理任务数据集，具体名称摘要未说明。评价指标：线性探针准确率（linear probe accuracy）、均值消融（mean ablation）分析、token计数变异性（token-count variability）。

Experiments: 主要发现：(1) RL模型隐藏状态比SFT模型更线性可分；(2) RL模型深层更关键（层次化架构），SFT模型各层重要性均匀；(3) token分配变异性与训练流程相关，不单纯取决于RL vs SFT。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感、检测或轻量化内容。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文研究的是大语言模型的推理机制，不涉及计算机视觉、遥感图像处理或模型压缩部署。

Abstract: Large reasoning models trained via reinforcement learning (RL) have been increasingly shown to outperform their supervised fine-tuned (SFT) counterparts on mathematical reasoning tasks; Yet the mechanistic basis for this advantage remains unclear. We therefore ask, what internal representational differences enable RL models' superior performance? Our work presents two converging lines of evidence: First, linear probes trained on layer-wise hidden states reveal that RL models tend to achieve higher accuracy in predicting answer correctness compared to SFT models, indicating more linearly separable and structured representations. Second, mean ablation studies show that RL models develop a hierarchical architecture where deeper layers become progressively more critical, whereas SFT models distribute importance uniformly across layers. Together, these findings demonstrate that RL training fundamentally restructures how models represent and process reasoning problems. Finally, we analyze token-count variability under repeated sampling across problems to assess adaptive compute allocation. While we observe higher variability in some RL-tuned models than in their SFT counterparts, we see strong consistency in others, suggesting that token allocation may depend more on the overall training pipeline than on RL versus SFT alone. We believe this token-allocation variability reveals the spread of plausible on-policy reasoning, highlighting which models exhibit stable policies versus those that are under-determined, potentially non-identifiable solution behaviour.

</details>


### [131] [Even More Deception: Objective Misalignment in Mixed-Motive LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26120)
*Marylou Fauchard,Florian Carichon,Margarida Carvalho,Golnoosh Farnadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究LLM驱动的多智能体系统在混合动机环境中的目标错位评估，使用社交推理游戏"狼人杀"作为实验平台，分析智能体内部推理与公开行为的差异。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化，属于LLM多智能体系统与社会推理领域的研究。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明遥感相关任务。任务类型：LLM多智能体系统的目标错位评估；应用场景：社交推理游戏（狼人杀）；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。提及使用来自四个不同模型家族和规模的LLM，以及四个玩家角色和三种目标公式化表达。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 完全不涉及星上/边缘部署相关内容。未提及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明遥感数据集。实验基于狼人杀游戏环境，使用LLM（四个模型家族和规模）、四个玩家角色、三种目标公式化表达。评价指标：内部推理分析、公开廉价谈话行为分析、游戏结果分析。

Experiments: 主要发现：目标错位会损害固有对抗环境中的游戏结果，不对称信息和专业化角色会加剧这种影响；被破坏的智能体发展出目标依赖的推理策略，但这些适应在公开行为中几乎不可见；即使是微妙的目标错位也会深刻影响集体决策。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感/检测/轻量化内容。摘要未说明模型效率、推理速度、资源约束等关键信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于LLM多智能体系统与社会推理领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs)-powered multi-agent systems are increasingly deployed in mixed-motive environments, where agents operate under asymmetric information and strategic deception due to conflicting or hidden objectives. In these settings, misalignment with collective goals becomes a central concern. We propose a novel framework for evaluating objective misalignment using the social deduction game Werewolf, modifying the objective of a single agent while preserving its assigned role. Across LLMs from four different model families and sizes, four player roles, and three objective formulations, we introduce a dual analysis of the agents' internal reasoning and their public cheap-talk behavior (i.e costless, non-binding communication that does not directly affect the agents' utilities), complemented by an analysis of game outcomes. Our results show that objective misalignment undermines outcomes in inherently adversarial environments, an effect exacerbated by asymmetric information and specialized roles. While compromised agents consistently develop distinct objective-dependent reasoning strategies, these adaptations remain largely invisible in their public behavior. More broadly, our findings suggest that even subtle objective misalignment can profoundly affect collective decision-making, highlighting the need for effective mitigation strategies for LLM-based multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [132] [ClinLens: Towards Long-Horizon Coding Agents for Longitudinal Multimodal Clinical Data Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26155)
*Yuan Zhu,Ethan B. Liu,Frank Nie,Jindong Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个面向临床数据分析的基准测试CLINLENS，用于评估AI代理在电子健康记录、心电图、胸片等多模态医疗数据上的分析能力，与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于临床数据科学（clinical data-science）和医疗AI代理基准测试，涉及电子健康记录、心电图、胸片等医疗模态，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感领域内容，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：临床数据分析基准测试；应用场景：医疗健康；传感器/数据类型：结构化电子健康记录、临床笔记、心电图、胸片、超声心动图；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。与遥感场景完全无关。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。提及了24种标准化模型-脚手架配置（model-scaffold configurations）以及基于GPT-4o-mini适配的5个生物医学系统，但未给出网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计、实时推理或边缘部署等轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何与星上/边缘部署相关的指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：CLINLENS基准测试，基于5个MIMIC资源（MIMIC resources）；评价指标：STRICTPASS（严格通过率，最强配置56.3%）、EXECSUCCESS（执行成功率，最强配置100%）、scope-macro（范围宏平均）；任务规模：200个可执行任务，固定126任务子集用于标准化评估。

Experiments: 在126个固定任务上评估了24种模型-脚手架配置，最强配置scope-macro STRICTPASS为56.3%（EXECSUCCESS为100%）；单独配置的编码代理解决了126个任务中的83个；5个基于GPT-4o-mini的生物医学系统最多达到2.9%的scope-macro STRICTPASS。结果显示可运行提交与正确临床分析之间存在显著差距。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供该方向的局限性分析。从论文自身看，摘要未说明基准测试的泛化性、计算开销、对真实临床工作流的适配程度等。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文属于临床医疗AI领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题在任务场景、数据模态、技术方法上均无交集，无法提供可借鉴的想法、可复现方向或可改进点。

Abstract: Clinical data-science agents must transform heterogeneous longitudinal records into auditable analyses, yet existing benchmarks largely isolate medical question answering, structured-table reasoning, or generic scientific repositories. We introduce CLINLENS, a benchmark of 200 executable tasks over five linked MIMIC resources spanning structured electronic health records, notes, electrocardiograms, chest radiographs, and echocardiograms. A 4 x 5 taxonomy crosses four patient-time scopes with five analysis capabilities. Program-first reverse synthesis pairs each bounded semi-raw package with an evaluator-private reference workflow and checks required artifacts, cohort and temporal semantics, and the final answer. On a fixed 126-task suite, the strongest of 24 standardized model-scaffold configurations achieves 56.3% scope-macro STRICTPASS despite 100% EXECSUCCESS. For reference, a separately configured coding agent solves 83 of 126 tasks, while five biomedical systems adapted to GPT-4o-mini reach at most 2.9% scope-macro STRICTPASS. These results expose a substantial gap between runnable submissions and correct clinical analyses.

</details>


### [133] [When benchmark inferences do not compose: Projectibility in AI evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26159)
*Brett Reynolds*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这篇论文讨论AI基准测试结果的可推广性（projectibility）和证据链的有效性问题，属于AI评估方法论，与遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分——与本研究方向基本无关。论文讨论的是AI基准测试结果如何推广到实际部署场景的认知论和方法论问题（projectibility, validity, evidence chain），不涉及遥感图像、卫星平台、目标检测模型或任何轻量化技术。虽然提到了"deployment study"，但这是泛指AI系统部署的评估逻辑，而非星上/边缘部署。

Reading priority: 1分——完全不建议阅读。该论文属于AI评估哲学/方法论方向，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化的技术路线、实验方法、部署优化无任何直接或间接关联。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何具体的视觉任务、应用场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型结构或网络设计。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文未提及任何具体数据集或评价指标。仅提到"legal-research case"作为案例研究，以及"reanalysis and simulation"作为分析方法。

Experiments: 摘要未说明。论文未报告任何模型实验或对比结果。仅提到对法律研究案例的再分析和仿真实验，用于论证aggregate stability会抹消后续投影所需的区分度。

Limitations: 该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何技术层面的借鉴。它讨论的是AI评估的认识论问题，属于科学哲学/评估方法论范畴。

Ideas for my research: 尽管论文本身与研究方向无关，但其核心论点——"benchmark-to-deployment的证据链不能自动组合"——对在轨部署有一定启发意义：在轨目标检测模型在公开遥感数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016）上的高精度，并不能自动保证其在星上真实场景（不同轨道高度、光照条件、传感器噪声、计算资源限制）中的表现。这提示我们在做轻量化模型评估时，需要设计更贴近真实在轨条件的验证协议，而非仅依赖标准benchmark。但这一启发属于方法论层面的泛化思考，非技术性借鉴。

Abstract: An AI benchmark result rarely reaches a consequential claim in one step. Evaluators generalize it to further cases, interpret it as evidence of capability, extrapolate it to new tasks, transport it to another system or site, and combine it with assumptions about human review and downstream consequences. Validity-centred approaches require evidence for each claim. This paper identifies a further epistemic problem: warranted links don't automatically make a warranted chain. The target of one study may not be the source of the next; system, population, outcome, or conditions may change at the interface; and shared data or model lineage may make apparently independent support dependent. Projectibility concerns whether a bounded extension from observed to unobserved cases is warranted. Goodman supplies the problem of rival extensions; argument-based validity supplies an architecture for testing them. The paper's distinctive claim is a non-composition principle: support for adjacent projections warrants their composition only when endpoints and assumptions align and dependence and uncertainty are carried through. A legal-research case shows how benchmark evidence and a deployment study can each be sound while remaining parallel. A reanalysis and simulation show why aggregate stability can erase distinctions a later projection requires. The resulting projectibility audit diagnoses unsupported joins in benchmark-to-use arguments.

</details>


### [134] [GuideSkill: Evolving Executable LLM Agent Skills for Guideline-Grounded Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26160)
*Lang Cao,Yuhao Shen,Tianyang Luo,Simo Du,Hao Peng,Yue Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GuideSkill，一种将临床指南编译为可执行函数的外部推理层，用于提升LLM在疾病诊断中的准确性和规则遵循能力。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文聚焦于临床诊断领域的LLM推理增强，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署完全无关。不涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测或模型压缩技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：临床诊断辅助推理；场景：医疗健康；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：疾病诊断。

Model architecture: GuideSkill作为外部推理层，将疾病标准编译为可执行函数；GuideSkill-Zero（从指南初始化）、GuideSkill-Evo（通过病例-诊断对优化）；LLM提出鉴别诊断，匹配技能并融合排序分数。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等信息。

Datasets and metrics: 四个基准数据集（摘要未具体命名）；四个backbone（Qwen3.5-9B等）；评价指标：macro-average accuracy、gold-label skill coverage。

Experiments: GuideSkill-Zero相比guideline RAG平均提升13.45% macro-average accuracy；GuideSkill-Evo相比直接推理相对提升18.49%，gold-label skill coverage从56.5%提升至99.5%；在Qwen3.5-9B上超过最强参数更新基线11.16%且不更新backbone。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或轻量化内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文属于医疗NLP领域，不涉及遥感图像处理、目标检测或模型轻量化技术。

Abstract: Clinical practice guidelines (CPGs) encode diagnostic criteria, but LLM systems typically retrieve guideline text or absorb it through training rather than execute its rules. We introduce GuideSkill, an external reasoning layer that compiles disease-specific criteria into executable functions returning ordinal diagnostic-support scores. GuideSkill-Zero is initialized from guidelines, while GuideSkill-Evo uses case--diagnosis pairs to refine covered skills and add missing diagnoses. At inference, an LLM proposes a differential diagnosis, grounds the features required by each matched skill, and fuses its ranking with the executed skill scores. Across four benchmarks and four backbones, GuideSkill-Zero improves macro-average accuracy over guideline RAG by 13.45% on average. GuideSkill-Evo achieves the highest macro-average for every backbone, improves over direct inference by 18.49% relatively, and increases gold-label skill coverage from 56.5% to 99.5%. On Qwen3.5-9B, it also exceeds the strongest parameter-update baseline by 11.16% without updating the backbone. Expert evaluation further indicates that GuideSkill produces clinically sound and broadly acceptable skills, suggesting that its initialized and evolved rules are reliable and practically meaningful. These results support executable skills as a model-agnostic mechanism for combining guideline-derived procedures with case-derived diagnostic patterns.

</details>


### [135] [GoGoTB: Agentic RTL Verification with Specification-Grounded Coverage Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26181)
*Xin Xin,Jincheng Lou,Junhui Li,Jinglin Yan,Panda Xiao,Di Wu,Haixiao Li,Weicong Lu,Weijian Fan,Xinyu Qu,Yuxiang Zhao,Min Yu,Zhixiong Di,Yibo Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个基于LLM Agent的IC前端验证自动化框架GoGoTB，实现端到端验证收敛。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全聚焦于集成电路(IC)前端功能验证，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化均无任何关联。属于电子设计自动化(EDA)领域，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：IC前端功能验证自动化；场景：RTL设计验证；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: GoGoTB框架包含三个子系统：agentic execution control layer（执行控制层）、evolvable knowledge system（可进化知识系统）、specification-grounded coverage closure（规约驱动的覆盖率收敛）。底层依赖LLM。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：8个RTL设计（无公开数据集名称）；评价指标：环境生成成功率100%，line coverage 98.4%，branch coverage 97.2%，toggle coverage 97.0%，functional coverage 83.2%。

Experiments: 在8个RTL设计上无人工干预测试，达到100%环境生成成功率，平均行覆盖率98.4%、分支覆盖率97.2%、翻转覆盖率97.0%、功能覆盖率83.2%。与prior work对比，声称是首个实现完整验证环境生成和有意义覆盖率的方案。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化完全无关；2. 仅在8个RTL设计上测试，泛化性未知；3. 依赖LLM，计算开销和推理延迟未讨论；4. 功能覆盖率仅83.2%，仍有提升空间。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于EDA/芯片验证领域，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向无交叉。如果强行寻找间接启发，Agent框架中"execution control layer分离确定性执行与LLM推理"的思想或许可类比于在轨系统中将确定性处理（如NMS、后处理）与神经网络推理分离的设计思路，但关联性非常弱。

Abstract: Functional verification dominates integrated circuit (IC) front-end engineering effort, and a single missed bug that escapes to silicon can trigger a costly respin. Recent large language models (LLMs) offer new opportunities to automate this process, yet existing LLM-based approaches generate each component through independent single-turn calls with no shared context, leaving interface mismatches undetected and reported coverage disconnected from specification requirements. To address these challenges, we present GoGoTB, an agentic framework that achieves end-to-end verification closure through three subsystems: an agentic execution control layer, an evolvable knowledge system, and specification-grounded coverage closure. The execution control layer separates deterministic enforcement from LLM reasoning at every tool and stage boundary. The knowledge system dispatches methodology and design-specific expertise on demand. The coverage framework anchors every bin to a named specification behavior so that each residual gap has a diagnosable root cause and a targeted remedy. Tested on 8 register transfer level (RTL) designs without any human intervention, GoGoTB achieves 100\% environment generation success and averages 98.4\% line, 97.2\% branch, 97.0\% toggle, and 83.2\% functional coverage. No prior work successfully generates a complete verification environment or achieves meaningful coverage on the same benchmarks.

</details>


### [136] [Position: Evaluation Scores Are Perishable Knowledge Claims](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26191)
*Sankalp Gilda,Shlok Gilda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文讨论大语言模型评估中的"信任膨胀"问题，提出评估结果应附带形式性、范围和有效期等元数据，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。该论文聚焦于大语言模型（LLM）的评估方法论，讨论自动化指标、LLM评判、人工评估等多信号聚合时的信任膨胀问题。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化或边缘部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何视觉或遥感模型架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型压缩或轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 使用了HELM leaderboard上的54个前沿模型在10个场景上的数据，但这是LLM评估基准，与遥感数据集无关。

Experiments: 在HELM leaderboard上展示了均值聚合与最弱链聚合的排名差异：top-5模型完全不相交。

Limitations: 该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化研究方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文讨论的是LLM评估中的统计聚合问题，与遥感目标检测模型轻量化课题在技术路线、应用场景、方法论上均无交集。

Abstract: Evaluation methodologies for language models increasingly combine multiple signals, from automated metrics and LLM-as-judge ratings to human assessments and benchmark suite results. When these signals are aggregated via averaging, evaluation confidence can then substantially exceed the reliability of the weakest signal: a phenomenon we call trust inflation in evaluation. We argue that evaluation scores should be treated as epistemic claims with three properties: formality (human evaluation provides stronger evidence than an automated metric), scope (a benchmark result applies to the tested distribution, not universally), and validity windows (benchmark results expire as contamination accumulates and distributions shift). Several converging research traditions (chain-of-thought analysis, possibilistic logic, and algebraic theory) establish weakest-link aggregation as the conservative endpoint of a parameterized operator family controlled by a single pessimism parameter. Drawing on those traditions, and on concrete lessons from building an evaluation harness for agentic AI, we propose that evaluation results carry explicit metadata (formality tier, scope declaration, and expiration date) to make their epistemic status transparent. We illustrate the cost of mean aggregation on the public HELM leaderboard: across 54 frontier models on ten scenarios, the top-five models ranked by mean score and by weakest-link are completely disjoint.

</details>


### [137] [TraceCoder: Explainable and Auditable Code Generation with Position-Key Snippet Versioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26307)
*Rwaida Alssadi,Muntaser Syed,Balaji Kasula,Lamine Deen,Majed Alotaibi,Mohammed Alghamdi,Tyler Ton,Ali Alqarni,Marius Silaghi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种名为TraceCoder的代码生成可追溯机制，通过关系型代码片段历史模式、浏览器可视化工具和分数位置键索引，使LLM驱动的代码生成过程可审计、可回放。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于LLM代码生成的可解释性和可追溯性，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化等任何相关主题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：代码生成可追溯性（code generation provenance）；应用场景：LLM编程代理的审计与可信度；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体的模型架构。涉及LLM-based coding agents作为黑盒代码生成器，但未指定具体模型。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 完全不涉及星上/边缘部署相关内容。未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：30个算法编程任务（字符串处理、数学计算、数据结构操作）；评价指标：Mean Chg%（平均变更百分比），可追溯修复事件行占比；实验设置：两种provider配置，6次迭代预算。

Experiments: 在30个算法编程任务上评估，其中10个任务用满6次迭代预算；Mean Chg%达到30%；三成代码片段带有可追溯修复事件行（单一provider配置下为21%）；3个详细案例研究。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。该论文属于软件工程/LLM可解释性领域。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文讨论的是代码生成过程的可追溯性，与遥感目标检测模型轻量化无任何交集。

Abstract: Contemporary LLM-based coding agents produce code as black-box outputs: the rationale behind each line is hidden, the evolution of the code through benchmark-driven repair is ephemeral, and post-hoc auditing is impossible. We present a code generation concept that addresses these shortcomings through three complementary mechanisms: (i) a relational snippet-history schema that records, per repair event, the benchmark reference, round number, failure text, and LLM explanation, enabling full provenance queries; (ii) a browser-based visualisation tool that renders this history as heat-mapped, hover-annotated source code; and (iii) a competitive fractional position-key indexing scheme with tree-node delimiters that assigns stable, lexicographically-ordered identifiers to each code snippet, enabling fine-grained tracking without disrupting surrounding lines. We evaluate TraceCoder on 30 algorithmic programming tasks spanning string processing, mathematical computation, and data-structure manipulation, across two provider configurations. Of these, 10 exhaust the 6-iteration budget on tasks with subtle edge-case behaviour. Mean Chg% reaches 30%, three in ten code snippets carry a traceable repair-event row, compared to 21% when using Gemini 2.0 Flash as sole provider on a 20-task subset. Three detailed case studies demonstrate how the system explains which specific benchmark failures shaped each line of the final program. The proposed mechanism makes the internal "narrative" of automated code generation auditable and replayable, a property essential for trust and accountability in production deployments.

</details>


### [138] [Exploring Structures in Physics Problems: Can AI Agents Discover Statistical Mechanical Mappings?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26367)
*Wanyu Zhao,Wanbing Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM智能体在理论物理中从配分函数到可解模型的统计力学映射发现能力，提出了StatMechBench-v0基准测试，评估propose-verify-revise智能体在Ising类问题上的表现。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文主题是理论物理中的统计力学映射和LLM智能体推理能力，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

<details>

Task and scene: 任务类型：AI智能体在理论物理中的结构发现；应用场景：统计力学映射；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: propose-verify-revise智能体框架，基于LLM（大语言模型）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论星上/边缘部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: StatMechBench-v0基准测试，包含6个Ising类问题；评价指标为数值正确性检查。

Experiments: 在多个LLM和问题表述下评估propose-verify-revise智能体，发现数值反馈有助于修复代码和恢复正确配分函数，但智能体可能通过数值检查却错误识别可解类或低估计算复杂度。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感、检测或轻量化内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于理论物理+AI推理交叉领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何关联。

Abstract: An important skill in theoretical physics is to recognize when a new problem can be transformed into a known model. We study this skill as an AI-agent task: can LLM-based agents discover statistical mechanical mappings from a raw partition function to a tractable representation? To probe this question, we introduce StatMechBench-v0, a benchmark of six Ising-type problems covering transfer-matrix methods, gauge-removable disorder, and planar/Pfaffian structure. We evaluate a simple propose-verify-revise agent across multiple LLMs and problem phrasings. The results show that numerical feedback often helps agents repair code and recover correct partition functions. However, agents can also pass the numerical checks while misidentifying the underlying tractable class or understating computational complexity. This both reveals limitations in current LLM reasoning and calls for a verification stack that goes beyond numerical agreement, incorporating, for example, symbolic checks and structural invariants. Our study provides an early evaluation and design directions for AI agents aimed at structural discovery in theoretical physics.

</details>


### [139] [CaM-Wolf: Causal-Aware Multimodal Agents for Social Deduction Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26393)
*Zheng Zhang,Nanjie Yao,Jiarui He,Deheng Ye,Peilin Zhao,Hao Wang*

Abstract: Social deduction games (SDGs) such as Werewolf have become challenging testbeds for AI agents. These games require complex social skills such as reasoning, deception, and collaboration. While recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have driven significant progress in SDG agents, current approaches are predominantly text-based, overlooking the multimodal nature that is fundamental to human social interaction. To bridge this gap, we introduce CaM-Wolf, the first SDG agent that integrates multimodal perception and generation. CaM-Wolf processes video inputs from other players, employs a causal-aware Reasoner trained via reinforcement learning to establish logical chains between observable behaviors and hidden roles, and presents itself through an animated avatar. Our experiments and user study show that CaM-Wolf achieves superior agent gameplay performance and enhances the quality of human-AI interaction. This work represents a significant advancement towards creating more human-like AI agents capable of participating in nuanced social dynamics. Our code is available at https://3dagentworld.github.io/avatar_wolf.

</details>


### [140] [CG-World: A Large-Scale World-State Dataset and Protocol for World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26452)
*Yiming Cai,Fangjie Yu,Meiqing Yu,Ziyue Shi,Pengfei Yuan,Yong Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个从工业CG管线导出的世界状态数据集CG-World，用于世界模型、物理AI和具身智能研究，不涉及遥感或目标检测。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于世界模型(world models)、具身智能(embodied intelligence)、物理AI(Physical AI)领域的数据集构建，不涉及遥感图像、卫星观测、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一项。
