<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：世界状态建模、视频生成、动作预测、闭环视觉-语言-动作策略迁移。应用场景：计算机图形学、仿真、具身智能。传感器类型：CG渲染多通道数据。目标类别：无遥感目标。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。论文主要贡献是数据集构建，而非模型设计。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：CG-World v1，约85万个1-5秒的时间对齐片段，包含多模态语义、空间结构、骨骼/控制器状态、运动曲线、相机/光照参数、物理缓存、接触事件、多通道渲染等。评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标，评估任务包括几何条件视频生成、动作预测、闭环VLA策略迁移。

Experiments: 在几何条件视频生成、动作预测、闭环视觉-语言-动作策略迁移三个任务上进行了评估，结果表明CG-World可为受控生成、动作建模和具身策略迁移提供可复用的结构化监督。未提供与遥感相关方法的对比。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。论文本身是数据集工作，未讨论模型效率或部署问题。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于世界模型/具身智能领域，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向无交集。

Abstract: World models must learn the joint dynamics of states, actions, events, and observations, yet existing video, robotics, and simulation datasets usually capture only part of this structure. We introduce CG-World, a large-scale world-state dataset and protocol derived from industrial computer graphics production pipelines. CG-World explicitly records intermediate states, including multimodal semantics, spatial structure, skeletal and controller states, motion curves, camera and lighting parameters, physics caches, contact events, and multi-pass renderings. CG-World v1 contains approximately 850,000 temporally aligned segments of 1-5 seconds. It separates latent states, observations, relations, events, and branch metadata, and organizes them into unified spatiotemporal samples. To support intervention learning and counterfactual reasoning, CG-World defines a branch lineage covering factual trajectories, observation interventions, action interventions, mechanism interventions, and strict counterfactual branches, with intervention targets, invariants, and alternative outcomes explicitly recorded. We evaluate the dataset on geometry-conditioned video generation, action prediction, and closed-loop vision-language-action policy transfer. Results show that CG-World provides reusable structured supervision for controlled generation, action modeling, and embodied policy transfer. We plan to expand CG-World through continued data collection and community collaboration toward a shared data infrastructure for world models, Physical AI, and embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [141] [MultivationBench: A Benchmark for Multimodal Sequential Motivation Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26465)
*Kawai Chung,Chunkit Chan,Yauwai Yim,Yuxuan Liu,Haochen Shi,Weiqi Wang,Qing Zong,Tianshi Zheng,Yixuan Fu,Kai Chung Wong,Hao Liang,Yifan Gao,Xi Yang,Janet Hui-wen Hsiao,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MultivationBench基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型在故事驱动的视觉叙事中的序列动机推理能力，发现现有模型在动态推理方面存在严重不足。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感领域内容，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化/部署。其核心是多模态大语言模型的动机推理能力评估，属于社会智能与心理学交叉的NLP/CV基础研究。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多模态动机推理（motivation reasoning）评估；应用场景：故事驱动的视觉叙事理解；传感器类型：不涉及遥感传感器；目标类别：不涉及遥感目标。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。测试了"all tested models"（多种多模态大语言模型），但未列出具体模型名称。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 完全不涉及星上/边缘部署相关内容。未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 提出新基准MultivationBench，基于Maslow需求层次理论和Reiss基本欲望理论构建。评价指标：动机推理一致性（motivation reasoning consistency）。

Experiments: 所有测试模型在MultivationBench上均表现困难，无法在序列上下文中保持一致的动机推理，揭示了静态识别能力与动态推理之间的差距。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。但明显局限包括：仅关注动机推理评估，未涉及遥感场景、目标检测或模型效率问题，与在轨遥感部署方向完全无关。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于多模态大语言模型的社会智能评估方向，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化课题在问题定义、技术路线、评价体系上均无交集。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models have sparked significant interest due to their potential for social intelligence; however, their ability to perform sequential motivation reasoning remains insufficiently studied. Existing evaluations predominantly examine static text or isolated visual snapshots, which do not reflect the cumulative nature of real-world behavioral drivers. To address this gap, we introduce MultivationBench, a benchmark designed to rigorously evaluate multimodal motivation reasoning within story-driven visual narratives. The benchmark builds upon established psychological frameworks - Maslow's hierarchy and Reiss's basic desires - and requires models to integrate accumulated multimodal context to infer evolving motivations. Results indicate that MultivationBench presents a significant challenge: all tested models struggle to maintain consistent motivation reasoning across sequential contexts, revealing a critical disconnect between static recognition capabilities and the dynamic reasoning essential for human-like social understanding.

</details>


### [142] [EvoPINN: Agentic Discovery of Executable Algorithms for Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26490)
*Peng Yin,Kai Li,Yifan Zhang,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EvoPINN框架，利用LLM智能体自动搜索和发现适用于物理信息神经网络(PINN)的最优网络表示与训练算法，用于求解偏微分方程。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于科学计算中的PINN自动设计，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一个核心要素。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：PINN自动设计与算法发现；应用场景：科学计算（求解PDE）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: SLRC-PINN（自主发现的新型架构），LLM agent驱动的模块化搜索空间，将神经表示与训练程序解耦。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS（虽然涉及搜索，但面向的是PINN架构和训练算法，而非轻量化NAS）、轻量网络等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该工作面向科学计算精度而非边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：多种PDE场景（振荡、椭圆、耗散、非线性输运）；评价指标：相对L2误差。

Experiments: 在多种PDE场景下，EvoPINN发现的算法相比基线显著降低了相对L2误差；SLRC-PINN在参数匹配对比下性能优势保持。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未涉及任何模型轻量化或部署优化；3. 计算开销未知（LLM agent迭代搜索本身可能非常昂贵）。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可参考的是其"自动化搜索"的思想，但搜索对象（PINN架构与训练算法）与遥感目标检测轻量化（网络剪枝、量化、蒸馏等）完全不同。不建议投入时间阅读。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for solving partial differential equations (PDEs), yet their performance heavily relies on the manual, trial-and-error engineering of neural representations, loss formulations, and optimization dynamics. While Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a promising avenue for automated design, unconstrained code generation often yields mathematically invalid or numerically unstable solutions under strict scientific computing constraints. To bridge this gap, we propose \textbf{EvoPINN}, an agentic framework that reformulates PINN development from labor-intensive manual design into a rigorous, execution-grounded algorithm discovery problem. EvoPINN navigates a modular search space by decoupling neural representations from training programs, utilizing an LLM agent to iteratively propose memory-conditioned programmatic modifications. To ensure scientific validity, all candidates undergo strict structural verification and budget-matched PDE evaluation. Extensive experiments across diverse PDE regimes (oscillatory, elliptic, dissipative, and nonlinear transport) demonstrate that EvoPINN discovers PDE-specialized learning algorithms that significantly reduce relative $L_{2}$ error compared to baselines. Crucially, EvoPINN autonomously invented SLRC-PINN, a novel architecture whose performance gains persist under rigorous parameter-matched comparisons, establishing the viability of execution-grounded agents for discovering genuinely new scientific computing mechanisms.

</details>


### [143] [Evidence-Ledger Adjudication for Claim-Evidence Traceability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26512)
*Gengyu Chen,Yongjie Yu,Weiling Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究AI写作中的证据溯源与声明验证（evidence-ledger adjudication），与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。论文主题是自然语言处理（NLP）领域的AI写作可信度验证，涉及声明-证据溯源、支持关系分类等任务。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、轻量化部署等任何相关方向。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：evidence-ledger adjudication（证据账本裁决），即声明-证据溯源与支持关系分类。场景：AI辅助写作中的可信度验证。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构，仅提及"agent evidence-ledger condition"和"non-agent baseline"。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：AVerTeC, CLIMATE-FEVER, SciFact 三个基准数据集，构建了2,335行的盲测基准。评价指标：relation accuracy（关系准确率）、macro-F1（宏平均F1）、路由召回率（routing claims）。

Experiments: Agent evidence-ledger条件：relation accuracy 0.676, macro-F1 0.601；最佳非agent基线：accuracy 0.383, macro-F1 0.303。Agent方法路由了1270/1435个应被路由的声明，但错误路由了295/900个本应支持的声明。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法或实验设置。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于NLP/AI可信度领域，与遥感目标检测轻量化无任何技术交集。

Abstract: AI agents can draft claims faster than authors can check whether the cited or retrieved evidence supports them. We study evidence-ledger adjudication: a claim-evidence traceability workflow that pairs each claim with an evidence packet, assigns a support relation, and routes unsupported, contradicted, or mixed-evidence claims back to the author. The empirical core is a 2,335-row blind benchmark built from independent external labels in AVeriTeC, CLIMATE-FEVER, and SciFact. Gold relations and source evidence labels are hidden during prediction and joined only for scoring. On this benchmark, the agent evidence-ledger condition achieves 0.676 relation accuracy and 0.601 macro-F1, compared with 0.383 accuracy and 0.303 macro-F1 for the best non-agent baseline. It also routes 1270/1435 claims whose gold labels indicate contradiction, missing evidence, or mixed evidence, while routing 295/900 supported claims. These results show that evidence-ledger adjudication can turn heterogeneous evidence packets into an auditable traceability layer for AI-assisted writing.

</details>


### [144] [Eco3S: Complex Socio-Economic System Simulation via Agent-Based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26588)
*Shaopeng Wei,Yufei Cheng,Wenxi Sun,Yepeng Ding,Yu Zhao,Gang Kou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个基于LLM的社会经济系统仿真框架Eco3S，用于经济研究和政策分析，与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关主题；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、舰船/飞机/车辆检测等任何检测任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘/星上部署等任何模型压缩或部署技术。论文聚焦于LLM驱动的社会经济系统仿真（agent-based modeling），属于社会科学计算领域，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：社会经济系统仿真（agent-based modeling）；应用场景：经济研究、政策分析；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: Eco3S框架包含三个核心机制：(1) Co-evolving Environment Design（共进化环境设计）——智能体与环境双向反馈；(2) Structural Causal Simulation（结构因果仿真）——基于SCM的反事实推理；(3) Simulation-Analysis-Refinement Paradigm（仿真-分析-优化范式）——自纠正迭代优化。不涉及任何视觉/检测网络结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。该框架面向社会科学计算，不涉及星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 实验场景：canal decay（运河衰退）、origins of governance（治理起源）、information propagation（信息传播）等经济研究场景。评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标，提及"effectiveness"、"scalability"、"generalizability"。不涉及任何遥感数据集或检测指标（如mAP、FPS等）。

Experiments: 在多个经济场景上验证了Eco3S能够复现已有经济研究和现象，并展示了可扩展性和泛化能力。不涉及目标检测对比实验或消融实验。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法或实验结论。摘要未讨论计算效率、模型大小、推理速度等对部署关键的问题。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无直接可借鉴点。其提出的因果仿真和自纠正迭代范式属于社会科学计算领域，不适用于遥感视觉检测任务。建议忽略此论文。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models (LLMs) has renewed interest in agent-based modeling (ABM). However, current LLM-based ABM research faces several key challenges: modeling evolving agent-environment interactions, enabling flexible counterfactual reasoning, and automating simulation workflows for scientific research. In this paper, we propose Eco3S, a socio-economic system simulation framework for economic research and policy analysis that addresses these challenges through three key mechanisms: (1) Co-evolving Environment Design, a bidirectional feedback loop where agents and the environment co-evolve, producing realistic emergent behaviors; (2) Structural Causal Simulation, a structural causal model (SCM)-inspired counterfactual mechanism that allows flexible interventions for diverse causal inference tasks; (3) Simulation-Analysis-Refinement Paradigm, a self-corrective mechanism that iteratively refines experimental designs based on prior simulation results. Experiments on diverse economic scenarios confirm \textit{Eco3S}'s effectiveness in replicating multiple established economic studies (canal decay, origins of governance, and information propagation) and phenomena across domains. Additional results further demonstrate its scalability and generalizability, highlighting the framework's potential for rigorous economic research and policy-making.

</details>


### [145] [Fewer Clarifications, Better Code: Benchmarking Cross-Session Personalized Ambiguity Adaptation in Coding Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26611)
*Zijian Xu,Wenshuo Zhang,Zisen Qin,Rui Sheng,Yushi Sun,Huamin Qu,Chuhan Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CAPA基准数据集，研究如何利用同一用户的历史编码会话记忆来解决新会话中重复出现的个性化歧义问题，并提出了轻量级的同用户历史门控方法。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。论文研究的是AI辅助编码中的个性化歧义消解问题，属于软件工程与自然语言处理交叉领域，不涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测或星上部署内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：个性化编码歧义消解（personalized ambiguity adaptation）；场景：AI辅助编码；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。评估了12种近期LLM，并提出same-user history gating作为轻量级推理方法。

Lightweight method: 提出了same-user history gating作为轻量级推理时方法（lightweight inference-time method），但此处的"轻量"是指减少与用户的交互轮次（减少澄清次数），而非模型参数量或计算量的轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：CAPA（600个编码会话，60个用户-歧义单元，含300个保留评估会话）；评价指标：executable success, first-turn success, turns-to-completion。

Experiments: 评估了12种近期LLM，在无历史和有同用户历史条件下进行对比，分析了任务难度、用户身份和基于记忆的历史使用情况。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感、目标检测或模型压缩内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于AI辅助编码领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化研究方向无任何交集。

Abstract: AI-assisted coding increasingly translates informal user intent into executable software, yet coding requests often contain ambiguities that recur in user-specific ways across tasks and sessions. Existing disambiguation methods typically address each ambiguous request in isolation within the current coding session, often through eliciting additional clarification. However, whether resolved session history from the same user can serve as memory for resolving recurring personalized ambiguity in a newly opened session remains underexplored. We formulate personalized ambiguity adaptation as a new task: given a user's previously resolved coding sessions and a new ambiguous request, an assistant should identify the recurring ambiguity pattern, produce the intended executable solution, and minimize clarification. To benchmark this task, we introduce CAPA, which characterizes personalized coding ambiguity through six mechanisms and injects these mechanisms into unambiguous executable tasks using a controlled three-stage generation pipeline. CAPA contains 600 coding sessions across 60 balanced user--ambiguity cells, including 300 held-out evaluation sessions. We evaluate 12 recent LLMs under no-history and same-user-history conditions using executable success, first-turn success, and turns-to-completion. Our analyses examine task difficulty, user identity, and memory-based history use, and we further propose same-user history gating as a lightweight inference-time method. CAPA provides a foundation for developing long-term coding assistants that better align generated code with user intent while reducing repeated clarification.

</details>


### [146] [AlphaSchema: Exploring the Space of Trading Semantics for LLM-Based Alpha Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26642)
*Jingyang Yi,Jian Yang,Yifei Jin,Yuqi Li,Jian Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AlphaSchema框架，利用LLM在结构化语义空间中自动挖掘量化交易因子，与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是金融量化交易中的alpha因子挖掘，利用LLM agent进行语义空间探索。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感相关技术；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测等检测任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、边缘部署等模型压缩或加速方法。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：金融量化交易中的alpha因子自动挖掘。场景：中国股票市场。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: AlphaSchema框架，核心组件包括：结构化语义空间（由Event, Context, Qualities, Direction, Output五个维度定义schema plan）、LLM（用于将schema plan翻译为可执行因子）、surrogate model（用于在语义空间上学习奖励函数）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署，不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：中国股票市场数据。评价指标：因子预测性能、投资组合表现（具体指标名称摘要未详细说明）。

Experiments: 在中国股票市场进行实验，AlphaSchema发现的因子池具有强预测能力和投资组合表现。消融实验表明语义搜索过程能探索多样化区域并逐渐聚焦高奖励区域；同一schema plan由不同LLM实现具有可比的预测质量。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感/检测/轻量化技术内容。该论文聚焦金融领域，其语义空间构建和探索策略对遥感领域无直接参考价值。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与本人研究方向基本无关，无直接可借鉴点。如果强行寻找间接联系，其"结构化语义空间+迭代搜索"的思想在NAS（神经架构搜索）领域有概念上的相似性，但论文本身并未涉及任何轻量化NAS方法，且场景差异巨大，不建议作为参考。

Abstract: Automated alpha mining has increasingly adopted large language model (LLM) agents for factor generation and iterative discovery. However, existing LLM-based systems often delegate both factor construction and search decisions to the agent itself, without an explicit exploration space or a principled mechanism for navigating that space. As a result, exploration remains largely implicit and difficult to control or optimize systematically. We introduce AlphaSchema, which constructs and explores a structured space of trading semantics for alpha mining. Each point in this space is a schema plan composed of Event, Context, Qualities, Direction, and Output, specifying the semantics of a candidate factor before implementation. AlphaSchema decouples exploration from implementation: an LLM translates selected schema plans into executable factors, while evaluated rewards are accumulated to learn a surrogate model over the semantic space. An iterative selection mechanism uses this model to balance global exploration, surrogate-guided exploitation, and local mutation. Experiments on the Chinese stock market show that AlphaSchema discovers factor pools with strong predictive and portfolio performance. Further analyses show that the semantic search process navigates diverse regions while increasingly allocating evaluations toward high-reward regions, and that implementations of the same schema plans by different LLMs exhibit comparable predictive quality, suggesting that alpha mining quality is largely robust to the choice of LLM within our framework.

</details>


### [147] [Rethinking Self-Evolution: A Constrained Exploration-Exploitation Process for Mitigating Skill Overfitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26643)
*Hongqiang Lin,Chao Liu,Xiaofan Bai,Xuan Jin,Yuhong Li,Nenggan Zheng,Xipeng Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SkillBoost框架，通过结构化利用、先验引导探索和验证接受三阶段机制，优化LLM智能体的技能积累与复用，缓解过拟合问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于LLM智能体的技能自进化与经验复用，属于自然语言处理/智能体领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM智能体技能优化与经验复用；应用场景：通用AI智能体交互；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: SkillBoost三阶段框架：结构化利用（structured exploitation）、先验引导探索（prior-guided exploration）、验证接受（verified acceptance）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩、加速或轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 23个模型-基准配置（model–benchmark configurations），具体数据集名称摘要未说明；评价指标为任务性能（performance）和过拟合缓解程度。

Experiments: 在23个模型-基准配置上达到SOTA性能，优于人工编写和LLM生成的技能；迁移实验表明优化后的技能可被其他智能体在类似任务上复用。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术细节。论文仅针对LLM智能体技能优化场景。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。论文不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署等任何相关技术。

Abstract: Enabling large language model (LLM) agents to accumulate and reuse experience from past interactions remains a central challenge in real-world applications. A promising solution is to treat skills as trainable states and optimize them in the same way as model parameters in neural network training. However, data-driven skill optimization is prone to overfitting to the limited trajectories collected from real environments. Overexploiting these trajectories overfits the current batch, while unconstrained exploration causes regression on previously solved cases. This tension motivates a constrained search view of skill self-evolution, governed by an exploration--exploitation trade-off. We propose SkillBoost, a three-stage framework that mitigates both risks: structured exploitation localizes observed failures to editable skill components, prior-guided exploration draws on prior knowledge in the LLM to generate diverse repair candidates, and verified acceptance commits a candidate only when it improves performance within a regression bound. Experiments across 23 model--benchmark configurations show that SkillBoost achieves state-of-the-art performance while mitigating overfitting, outperforming both human-crafted and LLM-generated skills. Transfer experiments further show that optimized skills can be reused by other agents on similar tasks.

</details>


### [148] [AgenticCANN: Automated Ascend C Operator Generation via Knowledge-Augmented Agentic Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26661)
*Junhao Qiu,Zidong Wang,Yansong Sun,Zhitong Ma,Ping Guo,Qingfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AgenticCANN框架，利用大语言模型和知识增强的进化策略，自动生成Ascend C算子代码以优化NPU推理性能。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于Ascend C算子的自动生成与NPU推理优化，属于底层算子编译优化领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型轻量化或卫星在轨部署等核心内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：Ascend C算子自动合成与优化；场景：NPU推理加速；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: AgenticCANN框架，包含知识编排生成系统和阶段自适应进化策略，基于LLM驱动。

Lightweight method: 不涉及模型轻量化。论文关注的是算子级别的代码生成优化，而非模型压缩（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅提及在华为Ascend 910B NPU上实现最高6.65×加速比，但这是算子级别的加速，非模型部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 实验在华为Ascend 910B上进行，覆盖6个算子（5种模式类别）。评价指标：算子生成可行性（feasibility，90-100%）、加速比（最高6.65×）。

Experiments: 在elementwise和normalization算子达到90-100%可行性，fusion算子56%可行性；在1B Pangu模型推理核上实现最高6.65×加速；知识注入使elementwise算子可行性从57%提升至86%。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 不涉及任何目标检测模型或轻量化方法；3. 仅针对Ascend C算子，不具通用性；4. 未讨论模型级部署指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可能的间接启发：若未来在华为昇腾NPU上部署遥感目标检测模型，可关注其算子优化思路，但当前工作不涉及检测模型本身。

Abstract: Ascend C operator optimization is critical for NPU (Neural Processing Unit) inference performance but requires deep hardware expertise.While large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in automated CUDA kernel generation, the fundamentally different programming model of Ascend C introduces unique challenges that remain unexplored. In this paper, we propose AgenticCANN, a knowledge-augmented agentic evolution framework specifically tailored for automated Ascend C operator synthesis in low-corpus NPU environments.To overcome the severe platform knowledge deficit on unfamiliar hardware, AgenticCANN incorporates a knowledge-orchestrated generation system that delivers structured, multi-level domain insights across the development lifecycle to resolve the upstream feasibility bottleneck.Building on this foundation, it features a stage-adaptive agentic evolution strategy that dynamically aligns LLM interaction modes with specific generation and evolution phases, balancing high-exploration candidate discovery with high-convergence performance tuning.Extensive experiments on Huawei Ascend 910B across six operators spanning five pattern categories demonstrate that our method achieves 90 to 100 percent feasibility on elementwise and normalization operators, 56% on fusion operators, and up to 6.65$\times$ speedup on 1B Pangu model inference kernels. Further analysis reveals that knowledge injection monotonically improves feasibility from 57% to 86% on elementwise operators, demonstrating its general rather than operator-specific benefit.

</details>


### [149] [UrbanDS: A Graph-Guided LLM Multi-Agent System for Data-Intensive Urban Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26724)
*Zhilun Zhou,Jianghao Yu,Yuming Lin,yongjun yang,Sun Yongquan,Depeng Jin,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出UrbanDS，一个基于图引导的LLM多智能体系统，用于数据密集型城市任务中的自动化数据科学，不涉及遥感、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于城市数据科学中的LLM多智能体系统，不涉及遥感卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署等任何核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：数据科学自动化（data science automation）；应用场景：城市数据分析（urban data analysis）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: LLM多智能体系统，包含Data Profiling Agent、Relation Agent、Planner Agent、Execution Agent、Report Agent，以及统一的dataset graph结构。不涉及视觉模型或检测网络。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提及部署于武汉市东西湖区城市运营平台，但这是云端/边缘城市应用，非星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 提出UrbanDS-Bench基准，涵盖代表性城市数据分析和建模任务。实验在通用基准和城市基准上进行。具体指标名称摘要未说明。

Experiments: UrbanDS在数据密集型任务上持续优于现有数据科学智能体。具体对比方法和消融实验细节摘要未说明。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的视觉检测或模型压缩内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于LLM智能体与数据科学自动化领域，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无交叉。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have been widely applied in automating data science tasks. However, existing methods typically rely on a limited set of provided datasets, and they face challenges in data-intensive scenarios that require discovering and leveraging relevant information from large-scale and heterogeneous data repositories. Urban tasks are representative examples of such scenarios, as urban data are not only large-scale and multi-sourced, but also exhibit complex spatial, temporal, and semantic relationships. To address these challenges, we propose UrbanDS, a graph-guided LLM multi-agent system for data-intensive urban tasks. We first construct a unified dataset graph to organize reusable dataset skills and the relationships among datasets. Specifically, we develop a Data Profiling Agent that constructs a skill for each dataset. Moreover, a Relation Agent identifies relationships among datasets and integrates these relationships into the dataset graph. At runtime, a Planner Agent retrieves task-relevant datasets from the graph and generates execution plans. Multiple Execution Agents then perform data processing and analysis, while their execution progress and intermediate results are shared through a common memory. Finally, a Report Agent synthesizes the experimental logs into a report, which can be further refined based on user feedback. To systematically evaluate the capability of agents in handling data-intensive urban scenarios, we further construct UrbanDS-Bench, an urban data science benchmark covering representative data analysis and modeling tasks. Experiments on both general and urban benchmarks demonstrate that UrbanDS consistently outperforms existing data science agents on data-intensive tasks. Furthermore, UrbanDS has been deployed on the urban operations platform of Dongxihu District, Wuhan, demonstrating its effectiveness in real-world urban applications.

</details>


### [150] [Do Latent Channels Actually Communicate? A Causal Audit of Latent Multi-Agent LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26773)
*Huixiang Zhang,Mahzabeen Emu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种因果审计方法，用于分析基于LLM的多智能体系统中潜在通信（latent communication）的信息利用情况，通过受控消息替换来分解接收端对发送端信息的依赖程度。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是LLM多智能体系统中的潜在通信因果审计，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化或边缘部署中的任何一项。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM多智能体系统潜在通信的因果分析；应用场景：自然语言推理（GSM8K、ARC-C、MATH-500）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B的潜在通信中继（latent relay）架构，发送端产生连续表示传递给接收端。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署，未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：GSM8K、ARC-C、MATH-500；评价指标：准确率（accuracy）及因果分解后的各分量（percentage point）。

Experiments: 在Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B上进行了因果审计实验，分解了潜在通信中不同信息成分对端到端性能的贡献，发现聚合准确率无法反映消息的具体影响机制。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。本文属于LLM多智能体通信分析领域，与遥感目标检测轻量化课题无交叉。

Abstract: Latent communication in large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems (MAS) transmits continuous internal representations instead of text, but greater representational capacity does not establish that the receiver uses task-relevant information. End-task performance alone also cannot reveal whether an observed effect depends on message presence, content generated for the evaluated example, or information supplied by a separate agent. We introduce a causal audit that applies controlled message replacements at the boundary where the sender-produced representation enters the receiver. Four message settings support five measurements of encoded sender information, receiver sensitivity to message presence and identity, the task value of example-specific content, and the additional value supplied by a separate agent. We apply the audit to latent relay with Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B on GSM8K, ARC-C, and MATH-500. On GSM8K, the Qwen3-4B overall performance effect of -1.00 percentage point decomposes into a -6.17-point effect retained by an other-example message and a +5.17-point effect attributable to example-specific content; both component directions reverse at 8B. On MATH-500, the Qwen3-4B gain of 15.00 points comprises 8.33 points retained by an other-example message and 6.67 points attributable to example-specific content, while the 8B gain is dominated by the former component. Self-substitution comparisons further show that example-specific content and other-agent value are distinct. These results show that aggregate accuracy does not identify how a latent message affects the receiver and motivate controlled message comparisons as a standard evaluation for latent communication.

</details>


### [151] [Property-driven Causal Abstractions for Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26787)
*Jule Schmidt,Maximilian Weininger,Clemens Dubslaff,David Parker,Nils Jansen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于因果关系的因子化MDP抽象技术，通过状态变量谓词的因果依赖关系来缩减MDP状态空间，从而在保持模型关键特性的前提下实现高效策略求解。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是马尔可夫决策过程(MDP)的因果抽象技术，属于决策理论与规划领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文讨论的是MDP决策模型的状态空间抽象，不涉及任何遥感或目标检测任务。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文提出的是基于因果关系的MDP抽象方法，非深度学习模型架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。虽然"抽象"在广义上可视为一种模型简化手段，但本文的抽象是针对MDP状态空间的，与神经网络剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及任何部署相关的参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文使用"standard benchmarks"进行实验，但未提及具体数据集名称或评价指标。

Experiments: 论文在多个标准benchmark上进行了实验，对比了MDP、interval MDP、stochastic games等不同模型类型的因果抽象效果，结果表明该方法能获得小规模抽象并计算近优策略，且能泛化到大规模MDP模型。但实验内容与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Limitations: 该论文与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。其因果抽象方法虽在MDP领域有理论价值，但对遥感目标检测模型的轻量化部署没有参考意义。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于决策规划领域，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化方向无交叉。

Abstract: Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) are widely used as decision-making models, commonly specified over factored state spaces through state variables and their valuations. The exponential blowup in the number of states renders many reasoning tasks in MDPs challenging. Abstractions are promising techniques to reduce MDPs and thus mitigate scalability issues. In this work, we introduce a notion of causality on factored MDPs and a novel property-driven causal abstraction technique that retains many characteristics of the original MDP model. For this, we rely on causal relations over state variable predicates and identify those states that share the same reasons for fulfilling or violating a given abstraction property. We theoretically and empirically compare various causal MDP abstractions using different model types such as MDPs, interval MDPs, or stochastic games. Our evaluation demonstrates the potential of our approach: For several standard benchmarks, we obtain small abstractions that allow us to compute near-optimal policies for the original MDP. Furthermore, our causal abstractions often generalize to related large-scale MDP models.

</details>


### [152] [From Passive Video to Editable Experience: Physically Grounded Experience Synthesis for Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26903)
*Jia Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Pegasus是一个将人类操作视频通过图结构知识迁移转化为机器人可学习数据的框架，核心解决具身AI中的人机形态差异问题。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于具身AI（embodied AI）中的机器人学习数据生成问题，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感核心概念，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：跨具身形态的人类操作视频到机器人可学习数据的知识迁移；应用场景：机器人学习、具身AI；传感器类型：第一人称视频（egocentric video）；目标类别：操作物体、手部动作、物体状态。不涉及遥感场景。

Model architecture: 基于图的中间表示（Task Graph → Affordance & Constraint Graphs → Robot Planning Graph），层次化affordance潜空间模型，闭环物理验证器（kinematic feasibility, collision constraints, joint limits）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等均未涉及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。该工作面向机器人数据生成而非边缘/星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：GTEA Gaze+, EPIC-KITCHENS-100（第一人称操作视频数据集）；评价指标：Task Correctness, Executability, State Consistency, Learnability。

Experiments: 在多个egocentric manipulation benchmark和多种机器人形态上评估，展示了跨形态翻译的可靠性。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供该方向上的技术参考。摘要未说明模型效率、推理速度、资源开销等关键信息。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。如果强行寻找借鉴点：论文中的"低资源框架"（low-resource framework）思路——即通过知识迁移减少对大规模标注数据的依赖——在遥感领域的数据稀缺场景（如SAR舰船检测标注困难）可能有启发，但方法本身（图结构知识迁移、视频生成）与遥感目标检测轻量化差异极大，借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: The key bottleneck in embodied AI is not model architecture but data. Although billions of human manipulation videos exist online, robots cannot directly learn from them due to the embodiment gap between human morphology and robot hardware. We introduce Pegasus, a low-resource framework that bridges this gap by translating human demonstrations into robot-learnable data through structured knowledge transfer. Instead of relying on raw video prompts, Pegasus constructs a graph-based intermediate representation: a Task Graph extracted from human videos is transformed through Affordance and Constraint Graphs into a Robot Planning Graph for robot-conditioned video generation. A hierarchical affordance latent space models the relationship between object states, affordances, and tasks, enabling generalization beyond object identities. A closed-loop physics verifier further filters invalid generations using kinematic feasibility, collision constraints, and joint limits. We evaluate Pegasus across a range of egocentric manipulation benchmarks, including GTEA Gaze+ and EPIC-KITCHENS-100, and diverse robot embodiments, assessing Task Correctness, Executability, State Consistency, and Learnability. Results demonstrate reliable cross-embodiment translation and show that robot data generation can be reframed from a hardware collection problem into a scalable, low-resource knowledge transfer problem.

</details>


### [153] [What Does It Take to Detect an AI Agent? Minimal Feature Sets for Behavioral Detection under Browser Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26935)
*Vishisht Choudhary,Lukas Schmidt,Anne Zoë Kenntner,Feras Skhab,Michel Osswald,Jens Ernstberger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出三分类检测框架区分人类、机器人和AI代理流量，发现二元分类器无法正确识别AI代理流量，通过增加显式代理类实现完美检测。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是Web流量中的AI代理检测（bot detection），属于网络安全/流量分类领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱图像、空间在轨智能等遥感相关主题，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：Web流量三分类（人类/机器人/AI代理）；场景：Web安全、bot检测；传感器类型：无（基于鼠标轨迹等行为特征）；目标类别：人类、机器人、AI代理。

Model architecture: MLP二分类器、SAINT transformer、三分类MLP/SAINT/GBM（梯度提升机）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明轻量化方法。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：自建基准测试（含2299个逃避会话）；评价指标：F1、召回率（recall）、精确率（precision）、macro-F1。

Experiments: MLP二分类器将39.1%的AI代理误分类为人类，SAINT transformer二分类器将34.5%误分类为人类；增加显式代理类后，3个模型族×10个种子共30次运行中agent F1=1.000；两个行为特征（mouse_event_rate, teleport_click_ratio）在所有逃避级别上实现100% agent召回率，agent精确率0.994；五个特征将macro-F1提升至0.991。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或轻量化内容。该论文的检测信号（浏览器自动化伪影）与遥感图像特征完全不同。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于Web流量分类领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无交叉。

Abstract: Bot detectors deployed at scale treat traffic as binary: human or bot. This assumption breaks when AI agents browse the web through browser automation, a traffic class that is neither and that binary classifiers structurally cannot represent. We present a three-class detection framework distinguishing humans, bots, and AI agents, and show that the binary-vs-agent confusion is architectural: a binary human-vs-bot detector misroutes agent sessions because its label space lacks an agent class. On our controlled benchmark, an MLP binary classifier misclassifies 39.1% of real AI agents as human and a SAINT binary transformer misclassifies 34.5%; adding an explicit agent class yields per-class agent F1 = 1.000 in all 30 runs (3 model families $\times$ 10 seeds). To measure evasion resistance, we construct a five-level evasion ladder spanning passive observation, GAN-generated trajectories, and replay of real human cursor data ($n = 2299$ evasion sessions). Across 10 seeds and 3 model families we observe zero agent misses in 22990 per-seed predictions. The discriminative signal is a browser-automation artifact, not evidence of agent reasoning: Playwright does not emit the raw pointer-move and wheel-delta streams a physical input device produces, and this absence signature survives trajectory manipulation. Exhaustive search over all feature subsets of size 1-5 (9401 GBMs) shows that two behavioral features (mouse_event_rate, teleport_click_ratio) give 100% observed agent recall at every evasion level with agent precision 0.994; five features lift macro-F1 to 0.991. The signal is redundantly encoded: removing teleport_click_ratio leaves agent detection at 100%. The single-feature regime is degenerate, flagging every agent only by collapsing the classifier to always predict "agent". Two features robustly isolate agents; five separate all three traffic classes at macro-F1 $\geq 0.99$.

</details>


### [154] [Belief-Guided Decision Making with Uncertainty Gating in the Game of Go](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26946)
*Mehrad Yaghoubi,Azam Bastanfard,Abbas Jalilvand,Ashkan Rezaei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种Belief-Guided架构，将策略头与信念头解耦，使围棋AI在不依赖大规模MCTS搜索的情况下实现专业级对弈，降低对计算资源的依赖。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是Computer Go（计算机围棋），属于游戏AI/强化学习领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或轻量化部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：围棋对弈（Computer Go）；场景：游戏AI；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: Belief-Guided架构，包含Policy head和独立的Belief head，集成Transformer/GRU作为记忆机制，使用门控机制过滤过度自信的策略错误。

Lightweight method: 论文的核心动机是减少对MCTS搜索的依赖，通过将"智能"从运行时搜索转移到参数化"直觉"中，从而降低推理时的计算开销。但这并非传统意义上的模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等），而是架构设计层面的推理效率优化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要提到"professional-level play on limited hardware where massive MCTS is infeasible"，说明该方法在消费级硬件上可行，但未给出参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等具体部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要提到实验结果表明该方法显著提升了无搜索胜率（search-free win rates）并减少了幻觉（hallucination），但未给出具体数值和对比方法。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未提供量化指标（参数量、FLOPs、延迟等）；3. 方法针对围棋博弈的特定问题设计（如Ko rule），难以直接迁移到遥感目标检测任务。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。唯一可能的启发是：将"运行时搜索"的负担转移到"参数化直觉"的思路，在遥感在轨场景中可类比为——将部分后处理或复杂推理逻辑编码到网络参数中，减少星上推理时的计算开销。但这一思路较为抽象，且论文未提供可复现的轻量化技术细节。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Computer Go, driven by AlphaZero and MuZero, rely heavily on Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) to correct the errors of the neural network policy. While effective on massive computational clusters, this dependence creates a critical bottleneck on consumer-grade hardware, where the computational cost of tree management severely limits inference rates. Furthermore, without deep search, these models suffer from hallucination, proposing moves with high confidence that are strategically fatal. This paper introduces a novel Belief-Guided architecture that disentangles the Policy head from a distinct Belief head. Unlike traditional value functions, the Belief head acts as an internal simulator and independent critic, modeling epistemic uncertainty and strategic stability. By integrating memory mechanisms (Transformer/GRU) to handle long-term dependencies and the Ko rule, and utilizing a gating mechanism to filter overconfident policy errors, our model shifts the burden of intelligence from runtime search to parametric "intuition." Experimental results demonstrate that this approach significantly improves search-free win rates and reduces hallucination, enabling professional-level play on limited hardware where massive MCTS is infeasible.

</details>


### [155] [Setoka: A Benchmark for Hierarchical User Understanding in Personalized Agents over Heterogeneous Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27056)
*Lingyang Zeng,Guangze Chen,Kaichen Yu,Zhicheng Pan,Siyang Weng,Zirui Hu,Xiangyun Du,Hailin He,Rong Zhang,Chengcheng Yang,Kai Huang,Xuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个面向个性化智能体的分层用户理解基准测试Setoka，评估模型从语义记忆、情景记忆、行为模式到人格特质的理解能力。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。论文研究的是大语言模型驱动的个性化智能体（personalized agents）及其用户理解能力评估，属于NLP/人机交互领域，不涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测或模型压缩技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：个性化智能体的用户理解能力评估；应用场景：对话系统、人机交互；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。评估对象为3种语言模型（language models）结合5种记忆系统（memory systems）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Setoka（自建基准），包含10个合成用户（synthetic users）的异构数据；评价指标：按四个理解层级（语义记忆、情景记忆、行为模式、人格特质）分别评估性能。

Experiments: 评估了3种语言模型+5种记忆系统。结果表明：现有系统在语义记忆检索上表现良好，情景记忆任务性能下降，行为模式和人格特质理解任务（需跨时间整合异构碎片化信息）性能进一步下降。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无直接可参考的局限性。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于NLP/认知智能领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Personalized agents are increasingly applied to assist users across a wide range of tasks. Effective personalized assistance requires not only retrieving explicit facts from past interactions stored in agent memory, but also inferring abstract personal characteristics. However, existing memory benchmarks primarily evaluate whether an agent can retrieve information explicitly stated in conversational histories, failing to provide an effective assessment of deeper user understanding. In this work, we propose Setoka, a benchmark for evaluating memory-augmented personalized agents with hierarchical user understanding from heterogeneous data. Grounded in theories from cognitive and personality psychology, Setoka defines four levels of user understanding, i.e., semantic memory, episodic memory, behavior pattern, and personality trait. Moreover, to enable realistic yet privacy-preserving evaluation, we design a psychometrics-based pipeline that synthesizes diverse, coherent heterogeneous user data and queries at scale. Finally, we leverage Setoka to evaluate 3 language models combined with 5 memory systems for 10 synthetic users. Our comprehensive evaluation reveals that while existing systems perform well on semantic memory retrieval, their performance declines on episodic memory. Moreover, when dealing with behavior pattern and personality trait understanding tasks that require integrating heterogeneous and fragmented information dispersed over time, performance declines even further. These findings demonstrate that user understanding cannot be handled by simple fact retrieval, motivating the design of memory mechanisms for cross-source integration and abstraction over long-term user behavior.

</details>


### [156] [On-Policy Distillation for LLM Safety: A Routing Approach to Template-Robust Realignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27081)
*Yongjian Guo,Wanlun Ma,Lingyu Shen,Xi Xiao,Sheng Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于路由的在线策略蒸馏（ROPD）框架，用于解决大语言模型微调过程中的安全后门问题，通过建模对齐与受损输出概率分布之间的差异来提升鲁棒性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦大语言模型（LLM）的安全对齐与防御，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向完全无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：LLM安全对齐与防御；场景：大语言模型微调安全；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。摘要未说明遥感、卫星、目标检测相关内容。

Model architecture: 提出ROPD（Routing-based On-Policy Distillation）框架，核心思想是建模对齐模型与受损模型输出概率分布之间的差异，而非拟合特定提示模板。摘要未说明具体网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文中的"distillation"（蒸馏）是指安全对齐场景下的知识蒸馏，与模型轻量化/压缩无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明任何与星上/边缘部署相关的信息，包括参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等。

Datasets and metrics: 三个数据集、三个基础模型、四种基线方法。摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标名称。

Experiments: 与四种SOTA基线对比，ROPD在模板不匹配场景下显著优于基线方法，在防御有效性和能力保持方面均表现更好。但ROPD并非完全免疫模板偏移。

Limitations: ROPD对模板偏移并非完全免疫；论文完全未涉及遥感、目标检测、模型轻量化等本课题关注的核心问题。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，可借鉴点极少。唯一可能的间接启发是：蒸馏（distillation）作为一种知识迁移手段，在遥感目标检测轻量化中可用于知识蒸馏压缩模型，但本文的蒸馏目标（安全对齐）与本课题（检测精度与速度权衡）完全不同。

Abstract: Fine-tuning is the dominant paradigm for specializing large language models (LLMs), yet it exposes a critical vulnerability: malicious data providers can embed harmful behaviors into downstream corpora, creating models that retain professional skills while violating human values on demand. Existing safety-realignment defenses often fail in practice due to three key limitations: they frequently cause catastrophic forgetting of specialized skills; their effectiveness collapses when the defender cannot observe the attacker's prompt template; and successfully realigned models remain susceptible to re-jailbreaking via simple system prompt switches. To address these challenges, we propose Routing-based On-Policy Distillation (ROPD), a novel realignment framework that models the divergence between aligned and compromised output probability distributions rather than fitting specific prompt templates. We conduct extensive experiments comparing ROPD against four state-of-the-art baselines across three datasets and three base models with varying alignment strengths. Our results demonstrate that when baseline defenses face template mismatches, often accompanied by severe degradation in downstream task performance. In contrast, ROPD substantially mitigates template-mismatch risks, maintaining superior robustness in both defense effectiveness and capability preservation. While our analysis indicates ROPD is not entirely immune to template shifts, its performance degradation is negligible compared to existing methods, establishing a new standard for robust LLM realignment.

</details>


### [157] [AgentMap: Joint Equivalence and Subsumption Discovery for Ontology Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27130)
*Yiping Song,Jiaoyan Chen,Renate Schmidt,Hui Yang,Wen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于LLM多智能体框架的混合本体匹配方法AgentMap，统一了等价发现和包含匹配两种任务。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究本体匹配（Ontology Matching），属于知识图谱/语义Web领域，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。不涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测或模型压缩技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>

Model architecture: 基于LLM的多智能体框架AgentMap，包含语义检索、层次搜索、协作多智能体LLM推理等模块。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署，不报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 扩展了4个本体匹配数据集用于HOM基准测试；评价指标未在摘要中明确说明。

Experiments: 在混合设置、仅等价设置、仅包含设置三种场景下评估，AgentMap在混合设置上表现良好，且在仅等价和仅包含设置上分别优于对应基线。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的视觉检测或模型压缩技术。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于知识图谱/语义匹配领域，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Ontology matching (OM) has traditionally been formulated as either equivalence discovery or subsumption matching. The existing OM systems identify only one type of semantic correspondence and cannot simultaneously discover equivalence and subsumption mappings. In this paper, we introduce Hybrid Ontology Matching (HOM), a new OM task that unifies equivalence and subsumption discovery, and accordingly propose a Large Language Model (LLM)-based multi-agent OM framework AgentMap that is implemented by a series of interdependent semantic decisions. Given a concept in the source ontology, AgentMap integrates semantic retrieval, hierarchical search, and collaborative multi-agent LLM reasoning to progressively explore the target ontology, identifying either the equivalent concept, if one exists, or the most fine-grained subsumer. We further extend four OM datasets for a HOM benchmark and evaluate AgentMap under hybrid, equivalence-only, and subsumption-only settings. Experimental results show that AgentMap achieves promising performance on the hybrid setting, and at the same time outperforms equivalence matching and subsumption matching baselines on the equivalence-only and subsumption-only settings, respectively.

</details>


### [158] [Linguistic Monoculture in LLM-Assisted Language Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27134)
*Suhas Thejaswi,Juhi Kulshreshta,Lutz Oettershagen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这篇论文研究大语言模型（LLMs）对写作语言多样性的影响，提出"语言单一文化"概念，用数学框架分析共享模型、递归反馈和个性化模型对语言演化的作用。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化，而是研究LLM辅助写作对语言多样性的社会语言学影响。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 不适用。论文任务为语言演化建模，应用场景为写作辅助，不涉及遥感传感器、目标类别或视觉任务。

Model architecture: 不适用。论文使用数学框架（分布建模、均衡分析）而非深度学习模型架构，不涉及CNN/Transformer/检测头等视觉模型设计。

Lightweight method: 不适用。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不适用。论文完全不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署信息。

Datasets and metrics: 不适用。论文使用合成仿真（synthetic simulations）而非遥感数据集，不涉及mAP、FPS等检测指标。

Experiments: 论文通过合成仿真验证三种交互机制（固定共享模型、递归反馈、个性化模型）对语言多样性的长期影响，但无遥感目标检测实验。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法或实验结论。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于计算社会科学/自然语言处理领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何技术交集。

Abstract: Writing and communication are increasingly mediated by large language models (LLMs) that are being used to draft, revise and polish text. Although such assistance can improve clarity and help authors meet institutional expectations, widespread reliance on shared models may reduce population-level variation in linguistic form, a phenomenon we refer to as linguistic monoculture. We develop a mathematical framework in which authors and LLMs are represented as distributions over linguistic features and coevolve through repeated interaction. We analyze three interaction mechanisms: a shared model with a fixed linguistic distribution, a shared model recursively updated from author outputs, and personalized models updated through author-specific and population-level feedback. We characterize the resulting equilibria and convergence rates, showing that, shared models can drive authors toward a common norm, recursive feedback relocates the shared norm without altering pairwise spread under common conformity, and personalization can preserve a family of distinct author-model equilibria with nonzero linguistic diversity. We then endogenize conformity as a strategic choice trading off private benefits from clarity, legibility, and perceived fluency against distinctive style. Within this utility model, individually rational authors may conform more than is socially optimal because they do not internalize the value their distinctiveness provides to others, creating a negative externality and a price of monoculture that is finite for each fixed instance but can grow without bound when distinctiveness dominates authenticity. Synthetic simulations illustrate how fixed shared assistance, recursive feedback, and personalization produce different long-run diversity outcomes.

</details>


### [159] [OmegaUse-OfficeVal: Benchmarking LLM Agents on Long-Horizon Office-Suite Tasks with Economic Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27155)
*Jingbo Zhou,Yusai Zhao,Qi Bao,Jingjia Cao,Zhenghai Chen,Chang Gao,Kaiqi Guo,Muxin Guo,Mingxuan Li,Xinjiang Lu,Yanru Ma,Yixiong Xiao,Zenghui Zhang,Le Zhang,Hua Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个用于评估LLM智能体在办公套件长时任务上表现的基准OmegaUse-OfficeVal，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向无关。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。论文主题是LLM智能体在办公套件任务（office-suite workflows）上的基准评估，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关主题，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM智能体办公套件任务评估；应用场景：办公自动化；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构，仅提及评估了若干frontier LLMs。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文讨论的是LLM推理成本与人力成本的比较，但未涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。论文讨论了LLM推理成本（inference costs），但未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等具体部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：OmegaUse-OfficeVal（100个办公套件任务）；评价指标：human labor time、task price proxy、deliverable quality（通过code-based verifiers评估）。

Experiments: 评估了若干frontier LLMs并设置了人类基线。结果显示所有被评估的LLM在成本和速度上优于人类，但在交付质量上尚未达到人类水平。

Limitations: 该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供该方向上的局限性分析。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文聚焦LLM办公任务评估，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题无任何技术交叉或可迁移方法。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly expected to assist users in completing tasks. However, existing benchmarks provide limited support for evaluating whether agents can carry out office-suite workflows at a reasonable cost. We introduce OmegaUse-OfficeVal, a benchmark for evaluating LLM agents on long-horizon office-suite tasks with task-level economic grounding. The benchmark comprises 100 tasks derived from office-suite requests proposed by practitioners and adapted through a privacy-preserving process. On average, these tasks require 2.32 hours of human labor to complete. An important feature of the benchmark is that each task is paired with two economic signals: human labor time and task price proxy. These signals enable direct comparisons between human costs and LLM inference costs, as well as value-weighted evaluation. To support stable evaluation, we develop code-based verifiers from fine-grained rubrics. We evaluate several frontier LLMs together with a human baseline. Although all evaluated LLMs are substantially cheaper and faster than human workers, they have not yet approached human-level deliverable quality. The code and dataset are fully open-sourced, and more information is available on our project website: https://omegause-officeval.github.io.

</details>


### [160] [Partner Capability Estimation for Task-Agnostic Adaptation in Ad-Hoc Teamwork](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27177)
*Peter Tisnikar,Maja Swieczkowska,Benteng Ma,Gerard Canal,Matteo Leonetti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于贝叶斯推断的上下文模型（CE-CM），用于多智能体临时团队协作中推断隐藏的伙伴能力，并扩展至多任务场景。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是多智能体临时团队协作（ad-hoc teamwork）与能力推断问题，属于多智能体系统（Multi-Agent Systems）和人类-AI协作领域，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明遥感/卫星/目标检测相关任务。任务类型为多智能体临时团队协作（ad-hoc teamwork）与能力估计，应用场景为人类-AI协作，传感器类型和目标类别均不涉及。

Model architecture: CE-CM（Capability Estimation via Contextual Models）：基于近似贝叶斯方法推断任务不变的能力向量；CE-CM-Div：扩展版本，通过多样化规划轨迹评估能力假设。涉及上下文多智能体马尔可夫决策过程（contextual Multi-agent MDP）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。未讨论剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 模拟实验 + 离线人类研究（225条轨迹，15名参与者）。评价指标：能力估计准确性、不可行动作分配减少量、随时间变化的适应能力。

Experiments: 模拟实验表明CE-CM能快速恢复隐藏能力、减少不可行动作分配、适应时变能力；人类研究中CE-CM-Div相比基线CE-CM显著提升了能力估计质量。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感/检测/轻量化内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于多智能体协作与贝叶斯能力推断方向，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Effective collaboration with novel and diverse partners is a crucial skill for autonomous agents. Most current ad-hoc teamwork (AHT) approaches assume that agents will collaborate on a single, fixed task and that the partner's capabilities, their ability to successfully execute the desired action, are already known. In reality, a partner's true capabilities are often hidden, and human collaborators may act sub-optimally on tasks with multiple valid strategies. To address these limitations, we extend ad-hoc teamwork into a multi-task setting by re-framing it as a problem of joint planning with decentralised execution under hidden partner capabilities. We introduce CE-CM (Capability Estimation via Contextual Models), an approximate Bayesian method that infers task-invariant capability vectors. By using simulation-based sampling, the agent estimates capabilities and induces a contextual Multi-agent Markov Decision Processes for planning. This approach requires no population pre-training and refines its beliefs online from just a few tasks. To account for human unpredictability, we propose CE-CM-Div, an extension that evaluates capability hypotheses against diverse planner rollouts rather than a single optimal trajectory. Simulated experiments demonstrate that CE-CM rapidly recovers hidden capabilities, reduces infeasible action assignments, and adapts to changes over time. Furthermore, in an offline human study of 225 trajectories from 15 participants, CE-CM-Div substantially improved capability estimates over the baseline CE-CM method. Our results suggest capability-based modelling is a promising interpretable, task-agnostic representation in the studied settings, demonstrating that accounting for behavioural diversity is essential for robust human-AI teaming.

</details>


### [161] [Can AI agents conduct open-ended AI research? Early evidence from two case studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27191)
*Peter Kirgis,Sayash Kapoor,Andrew Schwartz,Stephan Rabanser,David Africa,Konstantinos Voudouris,Viet Nguyen,Toby Pilditch,Magda Dubois,Harry Coppock,Cozmin Ududec,Nitya Nadgir,Matilda Orona,Tilman Bayer,Derrick Chan-Sew,Yue Ling,Abhishek Shetty,Helen Toner,Gillian Hadfield,Seth Lazar,Steve Newman,Shoshannah Tekofsky,Rishi Bommasani,Arvind Narayanan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"影子评估"方法，测试AI智能体能否自主完成开放式的AI研究，实验表明当前智能体可完成工程部分但无法做出实质性研究进展。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关，属于AI智能体自动化研究能力的评估方法论文。

Reading priority: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何遥感/卫星/目标检测任务或场景。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何目标检测或轻量化模型架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文使用两份未发表的NeurIPS 2026投稿论文进行影子评估，不涉及遥感数据集或目标检测评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明。实验内容为AI智能体完成研究任务的能力评估，与遥感目标检测模型轻量化无关。

Limitations: 该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法或实验结论。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文讨论的是AI智能体自主研究能力评估，与遥感目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Forecasts of explosive AI progress hinge on AI agents automating AI research. But evidence on whether agents can carry out open-ended AI research is thin. Current evaluations either test agents on narrow, verifiable tasks, which excludes open-ended research, or submit AI-generated papers to blind peer review, which is overstretched, stochastic, and suffers from poor review quality. We introduce a third way to measure progress towards AI R\&D automation. An agent takes on the central, open-ended research question of a high-quality unpublished paper, and the paper's original authors grade its output. We call these shadow evaluations. We ran shadow evaluations on two unpublished NeurIPS 2026 submissions, giving frontier agents six days and thousands of dollars of compute. The agents completed all of the engineering without human help, yet could not make substantial progress towards answering the research questions. As a result, both papers were unambiguously rejected by the authors. We identify five recurring failure modes: poor judgment about the bar for publishable research, uncreative responses to shortcomings in the research design, ineffective backtracking from dead ends, poor resource awareness, and instruction drift. A robustness check with a second model and scaffold reproduced these failures. We release the expert reviews, survey responses, agent repositories, and logs. Our results provide early evidence that today's agents can do the engineering of AI research, but struggle with critical parts of the research lifecycle.

</details>

### [162] [Shape-Based Inductive Bias for Glioma Grading from Tumor Contours](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26090)
*Puneet Velidi,Michelle F. Miranda,Farouk Nathoo,Ashery Mbilinyi,Cédric Beaulac*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出基于功能形状对齐的频域轮廓分析方法，将脑胶质瘤分级任务从像素级建模转为形状级建模，使用紧凑MLP实现高精度分级，参数量仅为像素基线模型的1/46。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。该论文处理的是医学影像（脑胶质瘤分级），任务场景为BraTS肿瘤轮廓分析，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感核心要素；不涉及目标检测（object detection）任务；虽然使用了轻量MLP（2.9k-117.3k参数），但轻量化并非论文核心动机，而是形状表示带来的自然降维结果，且未涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、边缘部署等轻量化技术。因此与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。除非对形状表示+频域降维的通用方法论感兴趣，可作为跨领域启发参考。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：脑胶质瘤分级（glioma grading）；场景：医学影像分析；传感器类型：MRI（BraTS数据集）；目标类别：低级别胶质瘤（LGG）vs 高级别胶质瘤（HGG）。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 紧凑多层感知机（compact MLP），以频域排序的token作为输入。对比基线：ResNet-18、ViT-Tiny。摘要未说明具体MLP结构细节。

Lightweight method: 非传统轻量化方法。模型轻量是形状表示（shape-based representation）带来的副产品——将轮廓对齐到频域后，维度大幅降低，自然使用小MLP即可。未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等标准轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 参数量：2.9k-117.3k（跨折），至少比像素基线少46倍。摘要未说明FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性、边缘/星上部署相关信息。该模型面向医学诊断，非在轨部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：BraTS 2020肿瘤轮廓数据；评价指标：balanced accuracy（平衡准确率）、low-grade glioma F1（低级别胶质瘤F1分数）；实验设置：五折患者独立交叉验证，组内验证进行模型选择。

Experiments: 紧凑MLP平均平衡准确率71.5%，高于ResNet-18（65.9%）和ViT-Tiny（63.3%）；低级别胶质瘤F1为54.9%（最高）；池化折外平衡准确率72.4%（患者bootstrap 95% CI: 66.4-77.8%）；无噪声仿真中形状模型达56.3-71.5%，像素模型仅50.0-52.5%。

Limitations: 1. 仅针对脑胶质瘤分级，任务和领域与遥感完全不相关；2. 依赖肿瘤轮廓的精确提取，实际应用中轮廓获取本身是挑战；3. 未讨论在轨部署、实时推理、资源受限环境下的可行性；4. 未涉及遥感目标检测中的旋转框、小目标、多尺度等核心问题。

Ideas for my research: 1. 形状归纳偏置（shape-based inductive bias）的思想可借鉴：遥感目标（如舰船、飞机）也具有强几何形状特征，可尝试将遥感目标的轮廓/边界进行频域表示，降低输入维度；2. 频域token化方法可能适用于遥感旋转框检测中的角度建模；3. 紧凑MLP在极低参数量下的表现提示：遥感在轨场景中，若能将输入特征有效降维，简单的轻量网络可能就足够。但需注意该论文是分类任务而非检测任务，且领域差异大，直接迁移需谨慎。

Abstract: Glioma grading from tumor contours is often treated as a pixel problem even when the signal of interest is shape. We align closed contours with a functional shape-alignment framework, separate global deformation from residual Fourier shape, and organize these quantities as frequency-ordered tokens. In five-fold patient-disjoint cross-validation on BraTS~2020 tumor contours, with model selection performed using grouped inner validation, a compact multilayer perceptron (MLP) achieves the highest mean balanced accuracy at 71.5\%, compared with 65.9\% for ResNet-18 and 63.3\% for ViT-Tiny. It also gives the highest mean low-grade glioma F1 at 54.9\%. Its pooled out-of-fold balanced accuracy is 72.4\% (patient-bootstrap 95\% CI: 66.4--77.8\%). The selected MLPs use 2.9k--117.3k parameters across folds, at least 46 times fewer than the pixel baselines. In a controlled noise-free simulation, shape-based models reach 56.3--71.5\% balanced accuracy while the pixel models remain at 50.0--52.5\%. This work demonstrates how incorporating a shape-based inductive bias at the representation level can improve interpretability and scalability while enabling substantial dimensionality reduction.

</details>


### [163] [ScalablePromptus: Scalable and High-Fidelity Prompt-Based Video Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26106)
*Zehao Cao,Bowei Xu,Xun Cao,Zhan Ma,Hao Chen*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出ScalablePromptus，通过dropout训练策略使基于prompt的视频流在丢包/截断条件下仍能鲁棒重建，解决Promptus框架在网络波动时的质量崩溃问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于基于prompt的视频流传输与生成式重建，属于视频压缩与通信领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一项核心内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：基于prompt的视频流传输与生成式重建；场景：视频通信（lossy network）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于Promptus框架的改进，包含语义与颜色感知的prompt反演、球面线性插值（spherical linear interpolation）处理中间帧、dropout训练策略生成rank-ordered prompt表示。

Lightweight method: 不涉及轻量化方法。论文中的"dropout training"是用于提升鲁棒性的训练策略，而非模型压缩或加速手段。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文提及"real-world deployment"但指的是视频通信场景下的部署，非星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标：性能退化程度（performance degradation reduction 82%-95%）。

Experiments: 稳定网络下：质量略有提升；丢包/截断条件下：相比基线减少82%-95%的性能退化。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无直接借鉴价值。论文本身未讨论遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型压缩的任何方面。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，但可抽象借鉴的思路：dropout训练策略使模型输出具有排序特性（rank-ordered），从而在部分信息缺失时仍能产生合理结果。这种"部分输入→合理输出"的鲁棒性思想，或许可启发在轨模型应对通信中断或传感器部分失效时的容错设计。但整体关联度极低。

Abstract: Prompt-based video streaming transmits compact semantic prompts instead of pixel-level content for generative reconstruction, enabling ultra-low-bitrate communication. However, the state-of-the-art Promptus framework is vulnerable to network fluctuation, where partially received prompts lead to catastrophic quality collapse. We propose ScalablePromptus, which enhances Promptus with semantic and color-aware prompt inversion, spherical linear interpolation for intermediate frames, and--most critically--a dropout training strategy that produces rank-ordered prompt representations. This allows the receiver to reconstruct meaningful video from arbitrarily truncated prompts without any adaptation. Under stable networks, ScalablePromptus achieves modest quality gains. Under lossy conditions, it reduces the performance degradation caused by truncation by 82%-95% compared to the baseline, making prompt-based streaming robust enough for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [164] [A Hierarchical Framework for Graph Structure Learning in Histopathology Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26153)
*Sudipta Paul,Amanda W. Lund,Bülent Yener*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出G2*-Net，一种面向组织病理学全切片图像的两级图学习框架，通过双层优化学习图像级图结构，用于组织病理图像分类。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文面向的是组织病理学图像（histopathology images）分类任务，属于医学图像分析领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及轻量化/在轨部署相关内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：组织病理图像分类（whole-slide image classification）；应用场景：医学病理诊断；传感器类型：组织病理学显微成像；目标类别：细胞/组织结构。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: 两级图学习框架（two-level hierarchical graph representation）：第一级为patch内的细胞级图（cell-level graphs），第二级为patch间的图像级图（image-level graph），图像级图结构通过双层优化（bilevel optimization）学习得到，采用DARTS风格的一步展开近似（one-step unrolled approximation）进行超梯度估计。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS（DARTS仅作为优化策略被借用，非用于轻量化架构搜索）、实时推理、边缘部署等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。从方法复杂度看，双层优化+图学习+全切片图像处理的计算开销较大，不适合星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 三个不同的组织病理学数据集（three distinct histopathology datasets），具体数据集名称和评价指标摘要未说明。

Experiments: 在三个组织病理学数据集上验证了有效性，但未给出具体数值结果、对比方法或消融实验细节。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关；2. 方法计算复杂度高（双层优化+图学习），缺乏轻量化设计；3. 仅在医学病理图像上验证，泛化到遥感场景的可能性极低。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能的技术交叉点是：如果未来在遥感场景中需要建模目标间的空间拓扑关系（如舰船编队、机场飞机排列），其两级图表示+可学习图结构的思想或许有参考价值，但与本课题"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"的核心方向（检测+轻量化+星上部署）几乎无直接关联。

Abstract: The spatial organization of cells and tissues provides important diagnostic cues in histopathology images. Although graph-based approaches can model these relationships, many rely on fixed or heuristic graph structures that may not accurately represent tissue connectivity. In this work, we propose $G_2^*$-Net, an optimized two-level graph learning framework for classifying large-scale histopathology images, such as whole-slide images (WSIs) or large regions of interest (ROIs). Here, $G_2$ denotes the two-level hierarchical graph representation, and the superscript $*$ indicates the optimized image-level graph structure learned from the proposed framework. The method first divides each WSI or large ROI into image patches, constructs cell-level graphs within each patch to capture local tissue architecture, and then represents each patch as a node in a learnable image-level graph. $G_2^*$-Net formulates image-level graph structure learning as a second-order bilevel optimization problem, separating graph connectivity learning from classifier optimization while coupling them through validation-driven feedback. To make this formulation computationally practical, we adopt a DARTS-inspired one-step unrolled approximation for efficient hypergradient estimation. Experimental validation on three distinct histopathology datasets demonstrates the effectiveness of our proposed method.

</details>


### [165] [Rethinking Clinical Relevance in Chest X-ray Machine Learning: How Evaluation References Define Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26333)
*Panagiotis Fytas,Ian Selby,Clemens Karner,Judith Babar,Simon Baker,Jake Beckford,Timothy J. Sadler,Shahab Shahipasand,Arthikkaa Thavakumar,John Li Chen,Alex Sawer,Michael Roberts,Jonathan Weir-McCall,J. H. F. Rudd,Carola-Bibiane Schönlieb,Anna Korhonen,Anna Breger*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了胸部X光片（CXR）机器学习中评估参考标准（报告标签 vs 专家标注）对病理分类和图像质量评估模型排名的影响，发现评估选择会显著改变模型表现排序。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于医学影像（胸部X光片）的病理分类和图像质量评估，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：病理分类（thoracic findings classification）和图像质量评估（IQA）；场景：医学影像（Chest X-ray）；传感器：X光机；目标类别：胸部病变。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: ResNet, DenseNet（监督分类器）；MedKLIP, GLoRIA, ConVIRT（视觉-语言模型）。均为通用医学影像模型，非遥感专用。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Cambridge University Hospitals (CUH) 临床队列 + MIMIC-CXR 公开子集；评价指标：病理分类性能指标、IQA指标（SSIM, PSNR等）。与遥感数据集无关。

Experiments: 对比了不同标签来源（报告标签 vs 专家标注）对模型排名的影响；对比了监督模型与视觉-语言模型的表现；分析了IQA指标与专家判断的对齐程度。无遥感相关实验。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的轻量化或遥感检测方法。局限性在于研究领域完全不同。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。唯一可能的间接启发是：在遥感目标检测中，评估指标的选择（如mAP的不同变体、旋转框IoU计算方式等）也可能影响模型排名，但本文未涉及遥感场景。

Abstract: Chest X-ray (CXR) machine learning relies heavily on automated evaluation using reference standards that aim to approximate clinical judgment. However, commonly used report-derived labels for pathology classification or generic image quality metrics for reconstruction may not reliably reflect clinical judgment. We systematically investigate how evaluation-reference choices affect model performance and ranking in both pathology classification and image quality assessment (IQA). To enable controlled comparison across evaluation references, we collected paired expert image- and report-derived labels for thoracic findings from a clinical cohort at Cambridge University Hospitals (CUH) and curated a subset of the public MIMIC-CXR dataset, along with expert ratings of diagnostic image quality. We show that for supervised image classifiers (ResNet, DenseNet), several zero-shot and fine-tuned vision-language models (e.g., MedKLIP, GLoRIA, and ConVIRT), changing the label source leads to substantial differences not only in performance estimates but also in model rankings. In parallel, alignment of IQA measures with expert judgment depends heavily on the choice of measure, and commonly used IQA metrics such as SSIM and PSNR often fail to align with expert assessments of diagnostic usability. Our results demonstrate that evaluation choices are crucial: they can determine which models and methods appear best and are therefore selected for further development or deployment. The selection of evaluation references should therefore be treated as a central component of clinical validity in CXR machine learning, and justified with respect to the pathology, imaging task, and intended downstream clinical use.

</details>


### [166] [Decoder-Guided Lossy Contour Coding Via Anchor Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26426)
*Ruoyu Yang,Yichi Zhang,Haohong Wang,Fengqing Zhu*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种粗到精的轮廓编码框架，利用解码端已有的粗轮廓作为先验，实现有损轮廓压缩，在保持高几何精度的同时大幅降低码率。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于图像/视频传输场景下的**轮廓编码压缩**问题，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然提到了"machine vision"和"receiver-side vision tasks"，但核心是通信中的轮廓压缩编码，而非遥感目标检测或深度学习模型压缩。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：轮廓编码（contour coding/compression）；场景：带宽受限信道下的图像传输（如超分辨率、边缘条件生成等接收端视觉任务）；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：物体轮廓（object contour），非遥感特定目标。

Model architecture: 提出粗到精轮廓编码框架（coarse-to-fine contour coding framework）：编码器提取精细轮廓上的有序锚点（ordered anchors），在失真约束下进行自适应锚点跳过（adaptive anchor skipping）；解码器利用粗轮廓先验指导锚点连接（anchor connectivity）。非深度学习模型，而是基于几何结构的编码方法。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文讨论的是轮廓压缩编码的码率-失真优化，与模型剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署信息。论文关注的是码率（bitrate）而非计算效率。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称；评价指标：码率（bitrate reduction 54.5%-66.9%）、几何精度（high geometric accuracy），对比方法包括JBIG2和链编码（chain coding）。

Experiments: 实验结果显示：相比无解码端引导的方法，码率降低54.5%-66.9%；相比JBIG2，码率节省高达5倍，同时保持高几何精度。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 非深度学习模型，不涉及模型轻量化；3. 仅针对轮廓编码，不涉及目标检测任务；4. 未讨论实时性、计算资源等部署约束。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义非常有限。唯一可能的启发是：如果未来研究涉及**遥感图像中目标轮廓的星地传输压缩**，其"粗到精"的编码思路（利用解码端已有信息作为先验）或许可用于遥感目标检测结果的轮廓压缩传输，但这属于通信编码方向，而非目标检测模型轻量化。

Abstract: Object contours serve as compact structural priors for many receiver-side vision tasks such as image super-resolution, edge-conditioned generation, and machine vision. When such tasks are deployed over a bandwidth-limited channel, the sender transmits the high-quality object contour as structural side information to guide reconstruction at the receiver, while-to save bandwidth-only a low-quality reference such as a downsampled image or base-layer reconstruction is delivered. As a result, the decoder can already extract a coarse contour from this reference at no transmission cost, creating an encoder-decoder asymmetry: the fine contour must be coded and sent, yet a free coarse version is available at the decoder. This asymmetry is ignored by existing contour codecs such as JBIG2 and chain coding, which are lossless, symmetric, and offer no rate-distortion control, leading to high bitrates. In this paper, we propose a coarse-to-fine contour coding framework that models a high-quality contour as a structured geometric refinement of the decoder-available coarse contour. The encoder extracts ordered anchors along the fine contour and performs adaptive anchor skipping under a distortion constraint. The decoder then reconstructs the contour by using the coarse prior to guide anchor connectivity. This formulation enables lossy contour compression with an explicit rate-distortion trade-off. Experiments show 54.5%-66.9% bitrate reduction over methods without decoder-side guidance, and up to 5 times savings over JBIG2, while preserving high geometric accuracy.

</details>


### [167] [Multi-scale radiomics in pelvic MRI for endometriosis subtyping: highlighting data heterogeneity constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26692)
*Eliot Leguy,Chloe Mallet,Nicolas Normand,Elodie Germani*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文研究基于MRI影像组学的子宫内膜异位症分型，使用UT-EndoMRI数据集，提取子宫和卵巢区域的影像组学特征，训练分类器进行患者级分型。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是医学影像（盆腔MRI）中的子宫内膜异位症分型，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感领域内容；不涉及目标检测任务；不涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。属于医学影像分析领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化方向无直接关联。

Reading priority: 1分，完全不相关。该论文属于医学影像分析领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化研究方向无任何交集，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：医学影像分型（endometriosis subtyping）；应用场景：盆腔MRI分析；传感器类型：MRI（磁共振成像）；目标类别：子宫内膜异位症患者分型（有/无子宫内膜瘤）。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 基于影像组学（radiomics）的pipeline：提取手工设计的radiomics特征（包括Wavelet-derived特征），采用多尺度特征表示和特征选择策略，使用Gradient Boosting分类器进行监督分类，以及无监督聚类分析。不涉及深度学习目标检测模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何模型轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。该研究面向医学影像诊断，不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：UT-EndoMRI（公开数据集）；评价指标：AUC（最佳0.80），特异性（low specificity）。不涉及遥感领域常用数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR、NWPU VHR-10等）和指标（mAP、FPS等）。

Experiments: 最佳监督性能：使用原始Wavelet特征+Gradient Boosting分类器，AUC=0.80；ComBat harmonization未能一致提升性能；无监督聚类分析发现可复现但分离度差的分组，与采集变量相关。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法；2. 该研究本身存在小样本、多中心数据异质性、假阳性高、特异性低等问题。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与本人课题无直接可借鉴点。建议关注遥感目标检测轻量化相关论文，如YOLO系列轻量化变体、MobileNet/ShuffleNet在遥感场景的应用、知识蒸馏用于遥感检测器压缩、结构化剪枝在SAR/光学遥感检测模型中的应用等。

Abstract: Analyzing female pelvic MRIs is challenging, especially for evaluating endometriosis, where visual features are influenced by several factors, including anatomical complexity, technical variability, and inter-reader variability. Here, we evaluate a radiomics-based pipeline for patient-level endometriosis subtyping using the publicly available UT-EndoMRI dataset. We extract radiomics features from manually segmented uterine and ovarian regions and compare several multi-scale feature representations and feature-selection strategies. We train supervised classifiers to distinguish patients with at least one endometrioma from those without, and perform an unsupervised perturbation analysis to assess whether radiomics profiles reveal reproducible patient subgroups. The best supervised performance is achieved using raw Wavelet-derived features and a Gradient Boosting classifier, yielding an AUC of 0.80. However, this model produces several false positives, resulting in low specificity. ComBat harmonization does not consistently improve performance, suggesting that post hoc harmonization is insufficient in a small, multi-site cohort in which acquisition groups contained very few patients. Using an unsupervised clustering analysis, we identify reproducible but poorly separated partitions that remain associated with acquisition variables. Overall, these results suggest that pelvic MRI radiomics contain a preliminary signal for endometriosis subtyping, while highlighting the fragility of radiomics-based subtyping in small, multi-site datasets.

</details>


### [168] [An Attention-Based Framework for Alzheimers Disease Classification Using Resting-State fMRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26746)
*Harshiddhi Pathak,Gowtham Reddy N,Mrinal Acharya,Manjunatha Mahadevappa*
