Datasets and metrics: 数据集：自采多阶段压力协议数据（n=10）和PulseDB公开数据集（subject-disjoint验证）；评价指标：MAE（SBP 4.02±0.21 mmHg, DBP 1.79±0.05 mmHg），30次独立运行取均值。

Experiments: 30次独立运行，与基线模型对比，联合MAE降低28.2%。摘要未说明具体基线模型名称和消融实验细节。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测方向完全无关，无可借鉴性；2. 样本量较小（n=10自采数据）；3. 未给出模型参数量、FLOPs等量化轻量级指标；4. 未涉及任何遥感场景下的目标检测或轻量化部署问题。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，无直接可借鉴点。唯一可泛化参考的是：混合架构思路（轻量特征提取器+高效回归器）在资源受限场景下的设计哲学，但具体技术（1D-CNN+LightGBM用于PPG信号）无法迁移到遥感目标检测任务。

Abstract: Continuous cuffless blood pressure (BP) monitoring remains challenging due to motion artifacts, physiological variability, and the limited robustness of conventional pulse transit time (PTT) models under dynamic conditions. Many prior approaches rely on multi-second windows to stabilize estimation, an assumption that is frequently violated during real-world monitoring with intermittent signal corruption. Here, we show that discriminative BP-related information is preserved at the single-beat level and present a lightweight multi-modal wearable framework for continuous BP estimation. The system integrates synchronized chest electrocardiography (ECG) and ear-clip reflectance photoplethysmography, each co-located with a 6-axis inertial measurement unit to provide motion context. We introduce a hybrid learning architecture in which a one-dimensional convolutional neural network extracts a 64-dimensional embedding from individual PPG beats and fuses it with 30 physiology-grounded features, including PTT statistics and heart rate variability, followed by LightGBM regression. The method was evaluated using a multi-phase stress protocol ($n=10$) and the PulseDB public dataset with subject-disjoint validation. Across 30 independent runs, the model achieved mean absolute errors of $4.02 \pm 0.21$~mmHg for systolic BP and $1.79 \pm 0.05$~mmHg for diastolic BP, corresponding to a 28.2\% reduction in combined MAE relative to baseline models. By enabling beat-wise estimation without long temporal context, this framework supports computationally efficient cuffless BP monitoring suitable for wearable deployment under practical resource constraints. The source code for this work is available at https://github.com/SYMBIOX-Lab/BP-wireless.

</details>


### [200] [MetaKoopman: Bayesian Meta-Learning of Koopman Operators for Modeling Structured Dynamics under Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26345)
*Mahmoud Selim,Sriharsha Bhat,Karl H. Johansson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MetaKoopman，一种基于贝叶斯元学习的非线性动力学建模与预测框架，用于自动驾驶卡车在恶劣天气下的运动规划。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于自动驾驶场景下的非线性动力学建模与预测，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感核心内容；不涉及目标检测任务；不涉及模型轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、边缘/星上部署等任何轻量化或部署技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：非线性动力学建模与多步预测、运动规划；应用场景：自动驾驶卡车在雪、冰、混合摩擦等冬季恶劣条件下的控制；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。

Model architecture: MetaKoopman框架：基于Koopman算子的贝叶斯元学习，学习Matrix Normal-Inverse Wishart (MNIW)先验，支持闭式贝叶斯更新和后验预测分布。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法。摘要未提及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计、实时推理加速、边缘部署等内容。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。虽然涉及实时运动规划，但未讨论模型在资源受限平台上的部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：全尺寸自动驾驶卡车与拖车系统的实地实验数据（冬季场景：雪、冰、混合摩擦），以及模拟控制任务；评价指标：多步预测精度、不确定性校准、分布偏移鲁棒性。

Experiments: 在真实卡车实验和模拟任务中，MetaKoopman在多步预测精度、不确定性校准和分布偏移鲁棒性上优于先前方法；在动态可行运动规划（尤其是规避机动和牵引极限操作）中验证了有效性。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感、目标检测或模型压缩内容。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于动力学建模与控制领域，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题在问题定义、技术路线、评估体系上均无交集。

Abstract: Modeling and forecasting nonlinear dynamics under distribution shifts is essential for robust decision-making in real-world systems. In this work, we propose MetaKoopman, a Bayesian meta-learning framework for modeling nonlinear dynamics through linear latent representations. MetaKoopman learns a Matrix Normal-Inverse Wishart (MNIW) prior over the Koopman operator, enabling closed-form Bayesian updates conditioned on recent trajectory segments. Moreover, it provides a closed-form posterior predictive distribution over future state trajectories, capturing both epistemic and aleatoric uncertainty in the learned dynamics. We evaluate MetaKoopman on a full-scale autonomous truck and trailer system across a wide range of adverse winter scenarios, including snow, ice, and mixed-friction conditions, as well as in simulated control tasks with diverse distribution shifts. MetaKoopman consistently outperforms prior approaches in multi-step prediction accuracy, uncertainty calibration, and robustness to distributional shifts. Field experiments further demonstrate its effectiveness in dynamically feasible motion planning, particularly during evasive maneuvers and operation at the limits of traction. Project website: https://mahmoud-selim.github.io/MetaKoopman/

</details>


### [201] [Post-Training at the Edge of Detectability: A Game-Theoretic Approach to Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26358)
*Keegan Harris,Brian W. Lee,Ian Waudby-Smith,Philip Amortila,Nika Haghtalab,Michael I. Jordan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于博弈论的强化学习微调框架，通过凸凹分数规划自适应确定KL正则化系数，以优化奖励-保留权衡。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于大语言模型（LLM）的RL微调中的KL正则化系数选择问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然涉及强化学习，但应用场景和问题设定与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化无任何交集。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：大语言模型（LLM）的强化学习微调；应用场景：语言模型训练、API审计；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 基础模型：Qwen3-8B、Llama-3.2-1B（均为大语言模型）；核心方法：基于博弈论的KL正则化RL微调框架，通过凸凹分数规划（concave-convex fractional programming）自适应学习正则化系数。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 实验模型：Qwen3-8B、Llama-3.2-1B；评价指标：reward-retention trade-offs（奖励-保留权衡）；具体数据集摘要未说明。

Experiments: 在Qwen3-8B和Llama-3.2-1B上验证了所提方法在持续学习（continual learning）设定下能实现有竞争力的奖励-保留权衡。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感/检测/轻量化内容。该论文属于NLP/LLM训练领域的方法论研究。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值极低。唯一可能的间接启发是：如果在遥感在轨目标检测中涉及强化学习（如在线自适应学习或模型更新策略），其博弈论框架中自适应调节正则化系数的思路或许有参考价值，但需要大幅改造且场景差异过大。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) fine-tuning is widely used in language model training to improve model performance on a target task while limiting drift from a reference policy. A standard way to balance this trade-off is via a KL-regularized RL objective, although this formulation does not by itself provide a principled way to set the regularization coefficient. In practice, the coefficient is typically chosen heuristically or via hyperparameter search, which can lead to unnecessary overhead in training cost or undesirable reward-retention trade-offs. We instead propose a game-theoretic framework that gives this trade-off an explicit statistical interpretation. Specifically, we study a sequential game in which an agent chooses a policy to maximize cumulative reward while a monitor observes policy outputs over time and tests for deviations from the reference policy. Although not originating from the same perspective, we show that the resulting equilibrium policy can nonetheless be expressed as the solution to a KL-regularized RL problem for an optimal regularization parameter that can be viewed as maximizing reward per unit of statistical distinguishability. Drawing on classical results from concave-convex fractional programming, we provide a principled method for learning this equilibrium coefficient via reduction to the KL-regularized RL objective, thus allowing for flexible integration into standard fine-tuning pipelines. In experiments with Qwen3-8B and Llama-3.2-1B, we demonstrate that our methods result in competitive reward-retention trade-offs in a continual learning setting, and illustrate how our framework may be used to audit API providers serving open-source models.

</details>


### [202] [ClockRoPE: Random Fourier Rotations for Temporal Routine Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26369)
*Yiwen Chen,Joshua Ainslie,Krzysztof Choromanski,Xiang Gao,Su-Lin Wu,Yiping Yuan,Qian Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ClockRoPE，通过从周期性注意力调制函数推导旋转频率替代RoPE的log-linear频率调度，用于序列推荐中的周期性建模，已在视频平台生产环境部署。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦序列推荐（sequential recommendation）和生成式检索（generative retrieval），属于推荐系统/NLP领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：序列推荐（sequential recommendation）、生成式检索（generative retrieval）；应用场景：视频分享平台；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于Transformer架构，核心改进是ClockRoPE（替代RoPE的旋转位置编码），旋转频率由周期性注意力调制函数推导。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。虽然提到"successfully deployed in production-scale"，但未涉及模型压缩或加速技术细节。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提及在线A/B测试和生产部署，但无具体硬件或效率指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称；评价指标为"valued engagement metrics"（用户参与度指标），非遥感领域标准指标。

Experiments: 在线A/B测试显示ClockRoPE在用户参与度指标上有一致改进，已在大型视频平台生产级生成式检索系统中部署。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化完全无关；2. 仅针对序列推荐中的周期性模式，泛化性有限；3. 无量化效率指标；4. 理论贡献（Random Fourier Rotations）虽有一定数学深度，但应用场景狭窄。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但以下思路可抽象参考：1) 位置编码的频域设计思想——在遥感目标检测中，如果存在周期性空间模式（如特定地物重复出现），可借鉴类似思路设计更适配遥感图像空间分布的位置编码；2) 从注意力调制函数推导频率的方法论，可能启发遥感检测中针对不同尺度目标的频率自适应设计。但整体而言，直接借鉴意义不大。

Abstract: Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE) has been widely adopted in transformer-based large language models. However, its log-linear frequency schedule, originally designed to produce long-term attention decay, limits its adoption in domains with more complex distance-correlation patterns, such as temporal periodicity in sequential recommendation. We investigate the expressiveness of general query/key rotations and find that any normalized continuous positive-definite attention modulation function can be approximated by random rotations induced by its own Fourier transform, which we term Random Fourier Rotations. Building on this theory, we propose ClockRoPE for routine modeling in sequential recommendation, where rotation frequencies are derived from periodic attention modulation functions. In online A/B tests, ClockRoPE demonstrates consistent improvements in valued engagement metrics, and has been successfully deployed in production-scale generative retrieval system at a major video-sharing platform.

</details>


### [203] [Q-Steer: Action-Value Guidance for Molecular Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26391)
*Xinyu Wang,Jinbo Bi,Minghu Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Q-Steer，一种在分子语言模型生成过程中通过离线训练的PAVS-Q价值评分器对每个token进行动作价值引导的采样增强方法，在不改变在线oracle预算的前提下提升分子优化奖励。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文研究的是分子优化（molecular optimization）问题，属于计算化学/药物发现领域，使用分子语言模型和SMILES序列生成，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、卫星部署或任何视觉任务。

Reading priority: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。该论文属于计算化学/分子生成领域，不涉及遥感、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一个核心要素。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：分子策略优化（molecular policy optimization）；应用场景：药物发现/分子生成；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：分子结构（SMILES序列）。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: 基于分子语言模型（molecular language model）作为策略网络（policy backbone），引入PAVS-Q（prefix-action value scorer）作为离线训练并冻结的动作价值评分器，在采样时对logits添加归一化价值奖励（normalized value bonus）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未涉及星上/边缘部署相关信息，未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：PMO23（分子优化基准）；评价指标：mean valid-unique score、macro mean-score gains、task wins per cell。

Experiments: 在PMO23上使用固定10,000次在线oracle调用预算，跨2种分子语言模型backbone和4种优化器进行全因子实验，Q-Steer在所有8个backbone-optimizer组合中均提升了mean valid-unique score，macro mean-score gains在+0.033到+0.049之间，每个组合有18-20个任务胜出。消融实验表明动作身份（action identity）至关重要，前缀广播值（prefix-broadcast values）几乎中性，而打乱的动作值（shuffled action values）会损害性能。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化方向完全无关；2. 仅适用于分子SMILES序列生成场景，方法无法直接迁移到遥感图像目标检测任务；3. 未讨论计算效率或部署可行性。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无直接可借鉴点。其核心思想（在生成过程中引入离线价值引导）属于强化学习中的credit assignment问题，与遥感目标检测的模型压缩/加速/部署问题不在同一技术范畴。

Abstract: Oracle-limited molecular optimization gives reward only after a complete molecule is generated, while each rollout requires many local next-token decisions. This delayed-feedback interface makes molecular policy optimization myopic: an optimizer can learn that a molecule was good without knowing which intermediate actions made it good. We introduce Q-Steer, a rollout-time action-value steering primitive for molecular language models. Q-Steer uses an offline-trained and frozen prefix-action value scorer, PAVS-Q, that estimates the downstream reward of taking a candidate next token under a partial SMILES prefix, then adds a normalized value bonus to sampling logits. The optimizer update rule and online oracle budget are unchanged; the claim is fixed-online-oracle performance, not equal total compute. On PMO23 with a fixed 10,000-call online budget, complete factorial studies across two molecular language-model backbones and four optimizers show that Q-Steer improves mean valid-unique score in all eight backbone-optimizer cells, with positive macro mean-score gains between +0.033 and +0.049 and 18-20 task wins per cell. Mechanism controls show that action identity matters: prefix-broadcast values are nearly neutral, while shuffled action values harm performance. These results support Q-Steer as a reusable rollout-time action-value wrapper that improves average molecular optimization reward across optimizer families and policy backbones without changing the online oracle budget.

</details>


### [204] [Flow Map Learning via Nongradient Vector Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26398)
*Mark Goldstein,Anshuk Uppal,Raghav Singhal,Aahlad Puli,Rajesh Ranganath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SGFlow方法，通过非保守动力学和stopgrad机制学习ODE流映射，无需显式可逆约束或迭代反向传播，在CIFAR图像生成上实现少步采样竞争性能。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于生成式模型的流映射学习（consistency models / flow matching），属于图像生成领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化部署。虽然其"少步采样"思想与推理加速有关，但应用场景（CIFAR图像生成）和任务类型（生成式建模）与遥感目标检测完全不同。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：图像生成（image generation），具体为学习ODE流映射的consistency model。应用场景：CIFAR图像基准测试。传感器类型：不涉及。目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: SGFlow框架，基于非保守动力学（non-conservative dynamics）和stopgrad机制，学习ODE解和隐含速度场。不涉及目标检测网络结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未明确提及轻量化方法。核心思想是减少生成式模型的采样步数（one-step到many-step之间的权衡），这属于推理加速范畴，但并非针对模型压缩（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅在CIFAR数据集上报告了FID指标和采样步数（10步最优），无任何星上/边缘部署相关讨论。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：CIFAR（图像生成基准）。评价指标：FID（Fréchet Inception Distance），按不同采样步数（step count）比较。

Experiments: 在CIFAR上对比flow matching、Meanflow、Lagrangian map matching等方法。SGFlow在10步采样时取得最佳FID，在其他步数下保持竞争力，且是唯一具有stopgrad动力学驻点保证的方法。

Limitations: 1. 仅在CIFAR小规模数据集上验证，未在遥感图像或高分辨率图像上测试。2. 面向生成任务，而非检测/识别任务。3. 未讨论模型参数量、计算开销等部署关键指标。4. 未涉及遥感场景中的小目标、旋转框、多尺度等核心问题。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。可能的间接启发：SGFlow中"少步推理"的思想可类比于检测模型中的推理加速（如减少检测头中的冗余计算），但方法本身（ODE流映射学习）与目标检测模型轻量化无直接关联。不建议作为主要参考论文。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow-based models benefit from simple regression losses, but inference incurs significant overhead because sampling requires integration. Consistency models address this by directly learning the flow maps along the ODE trajectory, opening a design space between one-step and many-step approaches. However, existing methods face computational challenges such as requiring model inverses or backpropagation through iterated model calls, and do not always prove that the desired ODE flow map is a solution to the loss. We introduce SGFlow, an approach for learning flow maps that bypasses explicit invertibility constraints and expensive differentiation through model iteration. SGFlow trains a model to compute both the ODE solutions and the implied velocity from scratch by following non-conservative dynamics with a stationary point at the desired flow map. On the CIFAR image benchmark, no single method attains the best FID at every step count: SGFlow attains the best FID at 10 sampling steps and remains competitive with flow matching, Meanflow, and Lagrangian map matching at other step counts, while being the only one with a proven stationary-point guarantee for its stopgrad-based dynamics.

</details>


### [205] [Examining the Efficacy of Graph Neural Network Message-Passing in Regression Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26404)
*Keith G. Mills,Aedan J. DeFrates,Joong Ho Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究图神经网络（GNN）在不同回归任务中的有效性，发现深度卷积GNN（尤其是GEN）在回归任务上优于注意力机制GNN。

Research relevance: 1分。论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化部署，属于纯图神经网络回归任务研究，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。任务为图数据上的回归预测（scalar regression），应用场景为分子、媒体网络、神经网络蓝图等，不涉及遥感场景或目标检测。

Model architecture: GNN（Graph Neural Networks），message passing机制，对比了深度卷积GNN（GEN）与注意力机制GNN（attention-based GNN）以及其他经典GNN。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或星上/边缘部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标涉及rank ordering、error minimization、insight extraction等回归指标。

Experiments: 实验表明深度卷积GNN（GEN）在回归任务上优于注意力机制GNN，部分经典理论驱动的GNN在保持竞争力的同时更高效。

Limitations: 论文仅聚焦于图数据回归任务，与遥感图像目标检测及模型轻量化完全无关，无法直接借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值极低。唯一可能的间接联系是：若未来在遥感领域使用图结构建模（如场景图推理或目标关系建模），可参考本文关于不同GNN层在回归任务上的对比结论，但与本课题核心方向（遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化）无直接关联。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNN) facilitate effective prediction on graph data such as molecules, media networks and neural network blueprints. GNNs facilitate prediction through message passing techniques which define how information flows from a node to its neighbors. Due to the ubiquity of the graph data type, the development of newer and better GNNs has garnered much interest in the machine learning community.
  However, GNN evaluation and benchmarking is primarily driven by classification tasks. Thus, prospective GNN message passing layers are evaluated on their ability to outperform prior work in classification contexts. In contrast, GNNs are equally capable of performing scalar regression prediction, yet this class of problem is often overlooked when proposing new GNNs while the best classification GNNs are utilized in an a priori or off-the-shelf manner for regression problems. In response, this paper studies the efficacy of GNN layers in a slew of regression contexts from rank ordering, error minimization and insight extraction. Results show that deep convolutional GNNs, particularly GEN, are more effective at these tasks than attention-based GNNs, while other classical, theoretically-inspired GNNs remain competitive and efficient.

</details>


### [206] [SCOUT: Per-Context Reset Curricula for Sparse-Reward Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26417)
*Siddharth Aphale,Ayushman Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SCOUT，一种在线、与学习器无关的reset控制器，为每个任务上下文(context)提供独立的课程学习调度，解决稀疏奖励强化学习中全局同步调度无法适应不同上下文学习速率差异的问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于强化学习中的reset curriculum设计，属于机器学习/强化学习基础方法研究，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化或在轨部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：稀疏奖励强化学习中的课程学习调度；应用场景：导航和操控任务(navigation and manipulation)；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。SCOUT是一个reset控制器，作用于强化学习训练流程之上，不涉及检测或视觉模型结构设计。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩、加速或轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性或任何边缘/星上部署相关内容。

Datasets and metrics: 实验设置：六个导航和操控任务(navigation and manipulation settings)；评价指标：二进制rollout成功率(binary rollout success)、平均成功率(average success)、最不成功组的表现(least successful group)。不涉及遥感数据集。

Experiments: 在六个导航和操控任务上验证，SCOUT在三个无辅助训练失败的任务中实现了成功学习。在构造的调度冲突场景中，所有全局调度方案均遗留一组未解决，而SCOUT同时解决两组。报告了最不成功组的表现以揭示平均成功率可能掩盖的失败。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关。该论文解决的是强化学习课程调度问题，不涉及视觉检测、遥感图像处理或模型压缩部署的任何方面。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集，不建议投入时间阅读。

Abstract: Sparse-reward reinforcement learning often fails because rollouts from the unassisted evaluation start rarely reach later task stages. Reset curricula address this by starting some training rollouts from easier intermediate states, called scaffolds. Such a curriculum faces two decisions: scaffold access, obtaining informative starts, and scaffold allocation, deciding how quickly that assistance is removed. Most prior curricula pace removal on one shared schedule, which can fail when task instances, or contexts, learn at different rates. We introduce SCOUT, an online, learner-agnostic reset controller that gives every context its own curriculum. Using only binary rollout success, SCOUT removes assistance after sustained success, restores it after failure, and cautiously tests a harder start when progress stalls, without changing the reward, optimizer, or learner. A counting construction shows that synchronized global pacing can be insufficient when contexts need conflicting amounts of assisted practice. Across six navigation and manipulation settings, scaffold access improves learning and enables success in three where unassisted training fails within the reported budget. In a constructed pacing conflict, each tested global schedule leaves one group unsolved, while SCOUT solves both. Average success can conceal this failure, so we also report the least successful group. Group-level pacing works when learning differences follow known groups but can fail when they occur within one group. SCOUT needs no group labels and remains consistently strong in both cases. A reset curriculum should remove assistance at the scale where learning progress differs.

</details>


### [207] [DHRCL:Training Code LLMs with Dense Hierarchical Rewards and Curriculum Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26457)
*Shuhang Wang,Ziming Li,Hui Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种面向代码大语言模型的密集分层奖励强化学习框架DHRCL，通过语法验证、执行成功、单元测试通过率和AST结构相似性四层奖励信号及课程学习策略提升代码生成能力。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关，属于代码生成/大语言模型后训练优化方向，不涉及任何遥感图像处理、目标检测或模型压缩部署内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：代码生成（code generation）后训练优化；应用场景：大语言模型编程能力提升；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于Qwen3-4B/8B/14B大语言模型作为backbone，框架本身不涉及新的网络结构设计，而是提出强化学习训练策略。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何部署相关指标（参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等）。

Datasets and metrics: 基于Qwen3-8B和KodCode协议进行实验；评价指标包括语法有效性、可执行性、单元测试通过率、AST结构相似性等代码质量指标。

Experiments: 与二值奖励、通过率奖励、基于奖励模型、可验证密集奖励等基线方法对比；在Qwen3-4B/8B/14B三个规模上验证了方法的一致性优势。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文聚焦代码生成领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测或模型轻量化任何相关技术。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is a natural post-training paradigm for code-oriented large language models because generated programs can be evaluated through parsing, execution, unit tests, and structural analysis.However, existing methods often rely on sparse outcome rewards or statically combine heterogeneous dense signals, even though syntax validity, executability, functional correctness, and structural organization describe different and progressively dependent programming capabilities. We propose DHRCL, a reinforcement learning framework with Dense Hierarchical Rewards and Curriculum Learning. DHRCL decomposes feedback into syntax validation, execution success, unit-test pass rate, and AST-based structural similarity, and organizes these signals through a three-stage Syntax, Execution, Pass & Structural curriculum. Stage duration is determined automatically from recent validation trends rather than manually specified capability thresholds. We further introduce stage-aware probability-based token credit redistribution. The mechanism follows a consolidation-to-refinement principle: it emphasizes established token patterns during syntax-oriented optimization, applies uniform propagation for non-local execution feedback, and allocates more credit or blame to less-established token decisions during final functional optimization. Under a unified Qwen3-8B and KodCode protocol, the experiments compare DHRCL with binary, pass-rate, reward-model-based, and verifiable dense-reward baselines. We further evaluate DHRCL across Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B, and Qwen3-14B backbones, showing that its advantage remains consistent as model capacity increases.

</details>


### [208] [Neural Architecture Search for Traffic Prediction: A Survey of Methods, Challenges, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26467)
*Truong Giang Vu,Li Yang,Richard W. Pazzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 一篇关于神经架构搜索(NAS)在交通预测中应用的综述论文，不涉及遥感、卫星或目标检测。

Research relevance: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关。论文聚焦于智能交通系统中的交通预测任务，不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测或星上部署。虽然NAS方法本身对轻量化有潜在借鉴意义，但论文讨论的是交通时空预测而非视觉检测任务，场景差异极大。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。除非课题明确涉及NAS方法在遥感检测模型架构搜索中的应用，否则无需深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：交通预测（traffic prediction）；场景：智能交通系统（adaptive signal control, route guidance, ride-hailing dispatch）；传感器类型：摘要未说明（可能为道路传感器/交通流数据）；目标类别：无目标检测相关类别。

Model architecture: 综述对象包括：图卷积网络(GCN)、循环网络(RNN)、Transformer等深度学习架构。NAS搜索空间覆盖空间和时间交通算子(spatial and temporal traffic operators)。

Lightweight method: 涉及NAS（Neural Architecture Search）作为自动化架构搜索方法，包括梯度方法(gradient-based)、进化方法(evolutionary methods)、单次权重共享方法(one-shot weight-sharing methods)。但NAS在此处用于搜索交通预测模型架构，而非用于轻量化目标检测模型。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提及NAS方法的计算可扩展性(computational scalability)作为开放挑战，但未涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。仅提及"standard benchmarks"和"cross-city generalization"。

Experiments: 这是一篇综述论文(survey)，摘要未报告具体实验结果或对比实验。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 讨论的是时空预测而非视觉检测任务；3. 未涉及任何轻量化部署指标；4. 未涉及遥感图像特性（如多尺度、旋转变化、背景复杂等）。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。NAS方法本身（梯度搜索、进化搜索、权重共享）在理论上可迁移到遥感目标检测模型的轻量化架构搜索中，但本论文未涉及视觉检测或遥感场景，直接借鉴价值有限。如果后续研究关注"NAS for lightweight remote sensing object detection"，本综述中关于搜索空间设计和搜索策略权衡的分析思路有一定参考意义。

Abstract: Traffic prediction is a core task in intelligent transportation systems, supporting applications such as adaptive signal control, route guidance, and ride-hailing dispatch. Deep learning models, including graph convolutional networks, recurrent networks, and Transformers, achieve strong results on standard benchmarks, but their architectures are designed by hand, requiring significant expert effort and producing models that often generalize poorly across cities and datasets. Neural Architecture Search (NAS) offers a systematic alternative to manual design. It automates the search over candidate architectures of deep learning models, finding designs that match the spatial-temporal structure of traffic data without manual trial and error. This survey reviews NAS methods applied to traffic prediction, organized by search strategy: gradient-based methods, evolutionary methods, and one-shot weight-sharing methods. For each category, we analyze how the search space is designed to cover spatial and temporal traffic operators, and how the search strategy balances cost against architecture quality. We also discuss open challenges, computational scalability to large road networks, manual search space design, cross-city generalization, dynamic graph structure, and the open question of NAS for spatial-temporal foundation models, and identify directions for future research.

</details>


### [209] [Learning Dynamic User Personas from Implicit Interaction Streams via Iterative Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26473)
*Haifeng Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出IRIS框架，通过隐式交互流学习动态用户画像，用于个性化大语言模型，无需显式偏好监督。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于大语言模型(LLM)的个性化用户建模，属于自然语言处理(NLP)和对话系统领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型压缩或边缘部署等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：个性化用户画像学习、行为预测、决策预测。场景：对话系统、个性化LLM。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: IRIS框架：从隐式交互流中提取行为信号，通过预测驱动的闭环迭代优化用户画像表示。具体网络结构摘要未说明。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：1）基于公开域自传文本合成的交互流（proof-of-concept）；2）Reddit r/AmItheAsshole (AITA) 真实匿名数据（100位作者）。评价指标：行为预测准确率、画像稳定性、决策预测准确率（IRIS最高达61.0%）。

Experiments: 对比方法：静态画像、仅记忆检索、无个性化基线。IRIS在决策预测准确率上达到最高（61.0%），优于所有对比方法。

Limitations: 1）与遥感、目标检测、轻量化完全无关；2）仅在文本数据上验证，未涉及视觉或遥感模态；3）未讨论计算效率或部署约束。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于NLP/个性化LLM方向，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题无技术交集。

Abstract: Personalizing large language models (LLMs) to individual users is essential for improving user experience, yet existing approaches typically rely on explicit preference supervision such as pairwise comparisons or demographic attributes, limiting their applicability in natural interaction settings. We propose IRIS, a framework that learns dynamic user personas directly from implicit interaction streams by extracting behavioral signals from everyday conversations and iteratively refining persona representations through a prediction-driven closed loop without requiring explicit feedback. We introduce an evaluation protocol based on behavior prediction, persona stability, and decision prediction. A proof-of-concept study on a synthetic interaction stream derived from public-domain autobiographical text shows that IRIS produces stable personas and distinguishes individual users while revealing limitations of memory-only approaches on recall-oriented metrics. We then validate IRIS on anonymized real-world Reddit r/AmItheAsshole (AITA) data, with personas built solely from each author's historical interactions. Across 100 authors, IRIS achieves the highest decision prediction accuracy among all evaluated methods (61.0%), outperforming static personas, memory-only retrieval, and no-personalization baselines. These results suggest that implicit behavioral modeling provides a scalable alternative to explicit preference learning for personalized LLMs and offers a practical foundation for adaptive conversational systems and embodied agents that require continuously evolving models of their users.

</details>


### [210] [From Conceptual Hydrologic Models to Conceptually Interpretable Neural Networks: A Snow-Water Mass-Conserving-Perceptron Framework for Discovering Catchment-Scale Precipitation-Storage-Runoff Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26492)
*Yuan-Heng Wang,Hoshin V. Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种质量守恒感知机（MCP）框架，将概念性水文模型重构为物理约束的可解释神经网络，并在美国513个流域评估了雪水MCP网络，核心是水文建模而非遥感或目标检测。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于水文建模（hydrologic modeling）、雪水当量预测、流域径流模拟，完全不涉及遥感图像、卫星目标检测、在轨部署或模型轻量化。虽然使用了神经网络，但应用场景和问题设定与遥感卫星在轨目标检测无任何交集。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与轻量化方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：水文过程建模（streamflow, snow water equivalent预测）；应用场景：流域水文模拟（CAMELS-US basins）；传感器类型：不涉及遥感传感器；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。摘要未说明任何遥感、卫星、目标检测相关内容。

Model architecture: MCP（Mass-Conserving Perceptron）网络框架，包含SOIL-MCP、SNOWMCP、HYDROMCP等变体，本质是将概念水文模型重构为物理约束的神经网络，具有可解释性。网络结构为单层、1-5个状态节点。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。虽然提到MCP网络比LSTM使用更少参数，但这是模型本身设计的结果，并非专门的轻量化技术研究。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：CAMELS-US basins（513个流域）；评价指标：KGEss（Kling-Gupta Efficiency with standard normalization），AIC（Akaike Information Criterion）。不涉及任何遥感图像数据集。

Experiments: 主要结果：单状态网络KGEss中位数0.82，双状态0.89，五状态0.90；MCP与LSTM选择得到相同KGEss中位数0.90，但MCP使用更少参数。AIC和KGE联合选择可识别紧凑的流域有向图表示。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关，无法提供该方向的局限性分析。论文本身局限：仅测试了单层网络，未进行多任务联合训练（未来方向），仅在美国流域验证。

Ideas for my research: 可借鉴点极少。唯一可能相关的是：MCP通过物理约束实现参数效率（更少参数达到与LSTM相当的性能），这种"物理先验+紧凑网络"的思路在遥感轻量化中或许有启发，但需要完全不同的物理先验（如遥感成像物理、目标几何先验等），且本文方法并非为轻量化设计。

Abstract: The Mass-Conserving Perceptron (MCP) establishes a modeling paradigm in which conceptual hydrologic models can be reformulated as physically constrained, conceptually interpretable neural networks. Here, we develop a snow-water MCP network framework and evaluate it across 513 CAMELS-US basins. We first recast a coupled two-state SOIL-MCP and SNOWMCP conceptual model as a mass-conserving neural network and show that the hydrologic-model and neural-network formulations achieve comparable predictive performance. We then examine cross-node state-information sharing within two-state HYDROMCP architectures and evaluate broader single-layer networks constructed from three types of interpretable MCP units with one to five states. Across CONUS, the median KGEss increases from 0.82 for one-state networks to 0.89 for two-state networks and 0.90 for five-state networks, suggesting diminishing aggregate gains beyond two states. Basin-specific MCP and LSTM selection yields the same median KGEss of 0.90, while the selected MCP networks use fewer parameters on average. Complementary AIC- and KGE-based selection identifies compact, basin-specific directed-graph representations that balance predictive accuracy and model complexity. These analyses provide an empirical basis for identifying the numbers, types, and interactions of states needed for hydrologic representation. Future studies should test joint training against multiple hydrologic responses, such as streamflow, snow water equivalent, and groundwater storage.

</details>


### [211] [From Interface to Inference: Eliciting Any-Order Inference from Any-Order Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26504)
*Seunggeun Kim,Jaeyeon Kim,Taekyun Lee,Yuyuan Chen,Yilun Du,Sham Kakade,Sitan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两种掩码扩散方法（插入式掩码扩散和潜在空间掩码扩散）使语言模型原生支持任意顺序推理，应用于代码生成等非因果离散推理任务。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于语言模型的任意顺序推理（any-order inference）能力，应用于代码生成和数学推理（GSM8K），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：离散推理任务（代码生成、数学推理）；应用场景：自然语言处理/代码生成；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: FlexMDM（插入式掩码扩散模型，7B参数）、LatentMDM（潜在空间掩码扩散模型，125M参数），基于掩码扩散（masked diffusion）框架。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明星上/边缘部署相关信息。模型规模为7B和125M参数，但未讨论FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等指标。

Datasets and metrics: Python代码生成数据集、GSM8K数学推理数据集。具体评价指标摘要未说明。

Experiments: 训练了7B FlexMDM用于Python代码生成和125M LatentMDM用于GSM8K，两种方法均展现出不同的任意顺序推理行为并提升了下游性能。具体对比方法和消融实验摘要未说明。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可迁移的技术或思路。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于NLP/代码生成领域，不涉及遥感图像处理、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Abstract: Many discrete reasoning tasks, such as code generation, are inherently non-causal: programmers move between high-level structure and local details, a process we call any-order inference. For autoregressive language models, which lack a native any-order interface, non-causal abilities such as infilling and next-edit prediction require hand-designed mechanisms. Can we instead design models that natively support any-order inference? Masked diffusion models have recently emerged as compelling candidates, as their any-order training objective naturally offers an any-order prediction interface. This interface, however, does not automatically yield any-order inference. We demonstrate that this interface-inference gap stems from positional uncertainty: fixed-canvas, token-level models may know what semantic component should appear without knowing where to place it. In light of this, we propose two complementary approaches: (1) Insertion-based masked diffusion, building on FlexMDM (Kim et al, 2025), relaxes fixed-position commitments via insertions, enabling generation across non-contiguous regions. (2) Latent-space masked diffusion shifts prediction to coarser semantic segments, enabling search over latent generation orders. Empirically, we train a 7B FlexMDM for Python coding and a 125M LatentMDM for GSM8K and show that both approaches induce distinct any-order inference behaviors and improve downstream performance. We release our codebase at https://github.com/SeunggeunKimkr/genuine-any-order.

</details>


### [212] [Collaborative Weighting with Pessimistic Critic for Mitigating Overestimation in Off-Policy Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26509)
*Gong Gao,Xiao Lai,Ziqi Xie,Guojie Chen,Xianhui Liu,Weidong Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种协同加权Actor-Critic（CWAC）框架，通过分布化价值函数和协同加权机制缓解强化学习中值函数过估计和训练不稳定的问题。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文是深度强化学习（DRL）领域的算法改进工作，聚焦于连续控制任务中的值函数过估计偏差问题，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署均无任何直接或间接关联。属于泛机器学习方法，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入阅读时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：连续控制（continuous control）的深度强化学习算法改进。应用场景：模拟环境中的机器人/连续控制任务。传感器类型：不涉及。目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于Actor-Critic架构，包含分布化Critic（distributional critic）用于建模回报不确定性，以及协同加权机制（collaborative weighting mechanism）。可集成到SAC、TD3、DDPG等现有off-policy算法框架中。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标为模拟任务上的性能表现（performance across simulated tasks）。

Experiments: 在多种模拟连续控制任务上进行实验，与SAC、TD3、DDPG等基线对比，CWAC显著提升性能。消融实验和具体数值摘要未说明。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。但该工作面向通用DRL连续控制，未考虑遥感图像处理、目标检测或边缘部署等场景的特殊约束。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。如果研究方向涉及"基于强化学习的遥感目标检测策略优化"或"在轨自主决策"，其分布化价值估计和不确定性加权机制可能有一定参考意义，但与本课题核心（轻量化目标检测模型）无直接关联。

Abstract: Deep off-policy reinforcement learning algorithms for continuous control typically rely on neural value function approximation to guide policy improvement. However, temporal-difference (TD) learning introduces noisy targets, resulting in non-stationary optimization, while greedy policy updates amplify early-stage estimation errors. The recursive propagation of such errors leads to persistent overestimation bias and degraded training stability in actor-critic methods. Existing approaches attempt to alleviate this issue via prioritized sampling or modified value learning objectives, but often overemphasize high-uncertainty transitions caused by limited data coverage or bootstrapping errors, thereby further amplifying bias.In this paper, we propose Collaborative Weighting Actor-Critic (CWAC), a unified framework that explicitly accounts for predictive uncertainty in value estimation. CWAC employs distributional critic to model return uncertainty and introduces a collaborative weighting mechanism that jointly reweights TD-errors and uncertainty, enabling robust learning from reliable samples while suppressing noisy updates. In addition, we incorporate a stochastic pessimistic value estimation scheme via sampling from the return distribution, which effectively mitigates error propagation during policy improvement. CWAC can be seamlessly integrated into existing off-policy algorithm frameworks such as SAC, TD3, and DDPG with minimal overhead. Empirical results demonstrate that our proposed method significantly enhances performance across a diverse range of simulated tasks. Our code is publicly available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/CWAC-348E.

</details>


### [213] [Temporally Centered SIGReg Improves Multi-Task LeWorldModel Learning: From Analysis to Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26924)
*Chang Liu,Fei Suo,Yanzhou Jin,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo,Yaonan Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将SIGReg正则化应用于时间中心化残差而非隐变量边缘分布，以解决多任务世界模型学习中表示混叠问题，提升LIBERO基准上的下游任务成功率。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、在轨目标检测、模型轻量化均无直接或间接关系。论文聚焦于机器人操作领域的多任务世界模型学习（world-model learning），属于机器人学/强化学习/表示学习范畴，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测或任何模型压缩/加速技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多任务世界模型学习（multi-task world-model learning）、行为克隆（behavioral cloning）。应用场景：机器人操作（LIBERO基准）。传感器类型：视觉（pixels）。目标类别：机器人操作任务。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 基于LeWorldModel（LeWM）框架，使用Sketched Isotropic Gaussian Regularizer（SIGReg）进行隐变量边缘分布正则化。改进点：将SIGReg应用于时间中心化残差（temporally centered residuals）而非原始隐变量边缘分布。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：LIBERO基准（包含多个任务套件）。评价指标：下游任务成功率（success rate），具体报告了long-horizon套件提升1.7倍，四个套件平均成功率从53.2%提升至73.6%。

Experiments: 在LIBERO基准上，改进方法在long-horizon套件上提升1.7倍，四个套件平均成功率从53.2%提升至73.6%。无需外部预训练，略优于从头训练的Diffusion Policy，接近大规模预训练策略基线。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关。论文未讨论遥感场景、目标检测任务、模型压缩或边缘部署等任何相关议题。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于机器人世界模型学习领域，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向无交叉。不建议投入时间阅读。

Abstract: Recent work on LeWorldModel (LeWM) has shown that the Sketched Isotropic Gaussian Regularizer (SIGReg) enables stable end-to-end world-model learning from pixels by regularizing the latent marginal distribution toward an isotropic Gaussian, thereby preventing representation collapse. While effective and elegant in single-task settings, this recipe does not extend reliably to multi-task training, leading to substantially worse downstream behavior-cloning performance. In this paper, we show that marginal Gaussianization compresses the separation between task-dependent latent clusters relative to within-cluster variation. This compression introduces representation aliasing across tasks and states, and makes the learned representations highly sensitive to small visual perturbations. To address this problem, we apply SIGReg to temporally centered residuals rather than to the latent marginal distribution. This surrogate target places no direct regularization pressure on the separation among cluster centers, removes the requirement that the full latent follow a single isotropic Gaussian, and retains the anti-collapse effect of SIGReg. On the LIBERO benchmark, our method improves downstream success on the long-horizon suite by 1.7x and raises the average success rate across four suites from 53.2% to 73.6%. Without external pretraining, it slightly outperforms Diffusion Policy trained from scratch and approaches the performance of large-scale pretrained policy baselines. These results reveal a structural incompatibility between marginal Gaussian priors and multi-task latent structure, and provide a simple route toward stable and scalable end-to-end multi-task world-model learning.

</details>


### [214] [HiFloat4 Format for End-To-End Reinforcement Learning Post-Training of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26515)
*Hei Yi Mak,Shadan Golestan,Hoang Le,Mehran Taghian Jazi,Yunke Peng,Yaoyuan Wang,Yao Wang,Junsong Wang,Tianchi Hu,Fengchen He,Guipeng Hu,Tanzila Rahman,Anandharaju Durai Raju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出首个端到端FP4强化学习后训练量化方案，通过Rollout残差量化（Rollout-ResQ）和HiF4数据格式解决4-bit精度下激活值下溢问题，在Qwen2.5系列模型上接近BF16精度。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文聚焦大语言模型的FP4强化学习量化训练，属于自然语言处理/强化学习领域，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署均无直接关系。虽然涉及低精度量化（4-bit），但应用场景（RL训练）和模型类型（LLM）与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化方向差异极大。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化方向基本无关，阅读优先级极低。仅当对低比特量化（4-bit）的激活值异常处理技术有广泛兴趣时可略读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：FP4精度下的强化学习后训练量化（post-training quantization for RL）；应用场景：大语言模型（LLM）的强化学习训练；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: Qwen2.5-3B和Qwen2.5-Math-7B大语言模型；核心模块：Rollout Residual Quantization (Rollout-ResQ) 残差校正模块、HiFloat4 (HiF4) 三级层次缩放数据格式。

Lightweight method: 涉及4-bit量化（FP4），属于模型量化压缩方法。具体包括：端到端FP4后训练量化、Rollout-ResQ残差量化校正、HiF4数据格式设计。但该方法针对LLM的RL训练场景，非推理部署场景。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。虽然涉及4-bit量化可降低存储和计算开销，但论文未讨论边缘/星上部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集；评价指标为与BF16精度的准确率差距（accuracy gap），在Qwen2.5-3B和Qwen2.5-Math-7B上从4.9%降至1.1%（HiF4格式），从13.6%降至5.3%（MXFP4格式）。

Experiments: 在Qwen2.5-3B和Qwen2.5-Math-7B上验证；对比了全FP4基线、高精度训练策略+FP4 rollout等方案；消融实验揭示了rollout-training mismatch是主要失效模式；Rollout-ResQ+HiF4组合将精度差距从4.9%缩小到1.1%。

Limitations: 1. 仅针对LLM的RL训练场景，非遥感目标检测任务；2. 未在CNN或ViT-based检测模型上验证；3. 未讨论推理阶段的量化部署；4. 未涉及遥感图像特有的多光谱/高光谱/大尺寸图像处理；5. 未提供计算效率指标（FLOPs、延迟等）。

Ideas for my research: 1. Rollout-ResQ中提出的激活值异常检测与残差校正思路，可借鉴用于遥感目标检测模型量化时激活值outlier处理；2. HiF4三级层次缩放格式的思想，可用于遥感图像动态范围大的场景（如SAR图像）的低比特量化；3. 论文揭示的"rollout-training mismatch"问题提醒我们在量化感知训练中需关注训练与推理/部署阶段的一致性；4. 但整体借鉴价值有限，因为场景差异大，且遥感检测模型通常更关注推理部署而非RL训练。

Abstract: We present, to our knowledge, the first end-to-end FP4 RL post-training, in which both the rollout and training policies, including their forward and backward passes, operate at 4-bit precision. A systematic study reveals that the dominant source of degradation in FP4 RL is not training-side quantization error but rollout activation quantization: outliers stretch the dynamic range so far that a large number of activation values underflow to zero under FP4. Counterintuitively, restoring the training policy to higher precision while keeping the rollout in FP4 makes accuracy worse than full FP4 baseline, exposing rollout-training mismatch as the principal failure mode and ruling out standard pretraining-style fixes. We address this with Rollout Residual Quantization (Rollout-ResQ): a single residual correction term constrained to a hardware-friendly sparsity pattern, added only to the FP4 rollout matmul -- a lightweight correction that recovers most of the precision lost to outlier-driven underflow without inflating the rollout's compute footprint. On Qwen2.5-3B and Qwen2.5-Math-7B, Rollout-ResQ paired with the HiFloat4 (HiF4) format -- whose three-level hierarchical scaling preserves resolution under FP4's tight 4-bit budget -- closes the accuracy gap to BF16 from 4.9% to 1.1%, bringing fully quantized FP4 RL within striking distance of full precision. Applied to the open-standard MXFP4, the same recipe narrows the gap from 13.6% to 5.3%, revealing that FP4 format choice is a key factor that determines the ceiling on recoverable accuracy. Together, these results establish HiF4 as the enabling format for end-to-end FP4 RL post-training, and Rollout-ResQ as the activation-side mechanism that makes the gap to BF16 closable.

</details>


### [215] [What Can Latent World Models Know? Physical Parameter Identifiability in Multimodal Predictive Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27017)
*Kaizhen Tan,Xin Xu,Siru Tao,Hanzhe Hong,Yang Feng,Heqing Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究潜在世界模型中物理参数的可识别性，通过POKEWORLD环境分析预测目标如何决定潜变量中包含哪些物理量（质量、阻力、接触刚度），发现目标函数结构决定了潜变量获取的物理参数，而非单纯的数据量。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于潜在世界模型（latent world models）的物理参数可识别性理论分析，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一个核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：潜在世界模型的物理参数可识别性分析；应用场景：交互式环境POKEWORLD和机器人操作数据集RH20T；传感器类型：视觉观测；目标类别：物理参数（质量、阻力、接触刚度）。完全不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测任务。

Model architecture: 潜在世界模型（latent world model），包含编码器-预测器结构，使用certificate-gated protocol进行可识别性分析。摘要未说明具体网络架构细节。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 完全不涉及星上/边缘部署相关内容。未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：POKEWORLD（交互式环境）、RH20T（机器人操作数据集，4,258 episodes）；评价指标：R²（决定系数）用于衡量物理参数的可恢复性和潜变量包含程度。

Experiments: 主要发现：1）输入限制决定什么可知，预测目标决定什么被保留；2）接触刚度仅在预测触觉时进入潜变量（R²=0.50 vs -0.02）；3）阻力在所有确定性预测目标下均无法有效获取（R²≈0.13，监督头可达0.45）；4）RH20T上跨两个机器人4,258 episodes验证了相同机制。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。该论文属于机器人学习和表征学习理论方向。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，无直接可借鉴点。但可抽象思考：在遥感在轨目标检测中，预测目标（如检测损失函数）的设计同样决定了模型学到什么特征，这提示在设计轻量化检测模型时，蒸馏目标或剪枝准则的选择会影响模型保留的关键信息。

Abstract: A central premise of latent world models is that predicting the future forces a representation to internalize the physics of its environment. Which physical quantities does a trained latent actually contain, and what decides this? We answer with controlled interventions in POKEWORLD, an interactive environment whose visually identical objects hide mass, drag, and contact stiffness. A certificate-gated protocol first certifies each parameter as recoverable from raw observations, then measures whether it enters the latent, so a null result can be attributed to the objective rather than to the environment. The resulting identifiability map has two organizing mechanisms and one frontier. Inputs limit what can be known, while prediction targets decide what is retained. Stiffness enters the latent only when touch is forecast ($R^2=0.50$, compared with $-0.02$ when the same signal is merely fused into the input), and under single-step prediction a vision-only latent discards even perfectly visible object state. Drag marks the frontier. It carries a recoverability certificate of 0.89 yet plateaus near 0.13 under every deterministic prediction objective we test, while a supervised head on the same trunk reaches 0.45. Parameters whose readout is slow and ratio-type under the sensed coordinates fall outside what these objectives acquire. On RH20T, an input-target factorial across scaling curves reproduces both mechanisms across two robots and 4,258 episodes. Every arm missing information or prediction pressure stays flat over a fivefold data range, and only the full multimodal objective forecasts force beyond a persistence baseline, with held-out gains that grow with scale. Objective structure determines which physical parameters a latent acquires, and additional data improves only the parameters it already acquires.

</details>


### [216] [From Unsupervised Subgroups to Hypothetical State-Intervention Policies: An Evaluation of Selected Subgrouping Methods in Observational Health Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26521)
*Vasundhara Acharya,Bulent Yener*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合因果发现、无监督聚类和双重稳健策略评估的框架，用于在观察性生物医学数据中进行预算约束下的亚组优先排序，与遥感、目标检测或模型轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全属于生物医学/因果推断/政策评估领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能中的任何内容；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、舰船/飞机/车辆检测、遥感场景理解；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：生物医学观察性数据中的亚组分析（subgroup analysis）与政策优先排序（policy prioritization）。应用场景：预算约束下的健康干预策略分配。传感器类型：不涉及。目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 框架包含因果发现驱动的协变量选择、发现-评估样本拆分、归纳式无监督聚类（K-means, Fuzzy C-means, Bayesian Gaussian Mixture Models）、不确定性感知亚组选择、以及双重稳健策略评估。不涉及任何视觉/检测模型架构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何模型轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署相关内容。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：PIMA Indians Diabetes dataset（糖尿病数据集）、NHANES（美国国家健康与营养调查）。评价指标：ungated utility（未门控效用）、policy-risk difference（策略风险差异）、95%置信区间、Holm校正后的统计显著性。不涉及遥感数据集或检测指标。

Experiments: 在三个假设性健康状态转换场景（肥胖→非肥胖、血糖降低、吸烟史对比）下评估了多种聚类方法。最高估计未门控效用分别为0.799（BMI策略，Bayesian GMM）、0.735（血糖策略，FCM）、0.775（吸烟史策略，K-means）。所有策略风险差异的配对95%置信区间包含零，经Holm校正后无统计显著差异。

Limitations: 摘要明确说明结果应被解释为"假设依赖的决策支持证据"而非"干预获益的证明"。所有比较在多重假设校正后均不显著。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可迁移的技术或思路。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于因果推断与生物统计学交叉领域，与遥感目标检测轻量化课题在问题定义、技术方法、评价体系上均无交集。

Abstract: Conventional subgroup analyses can yield unstable and difficult-to-interpret conclusions, especially in observational biomedical data where each individual is observed under only one exposure state, true individual treatment effects are unavailable, and causal structure is uncertain. We investigate whether subgroups constructed from pretreatment characteristics, without using exposure, outcome, or estimated treatment-effect information, can serve as interpretable units for budget-constrained policy prioritization. We propose a framework combining causal-discovery-informed covariate selection, discovery-evaluation sample splitting, inductive unsupervised clustering, uncertainty-aware subgroup selection, and held-out doubly robust policy evaluation. We compare K-means, hard, membership-weighted, and stochastic Fuzzy C-means, Bayesian Gaussian mixture models, and a supervised causal-forest-derived CATE-tree comparator. Policies are evaluated under a 70% budget for hypothetical obesity-to-non-obesity and elevated-to-lower-glucose state shifts in the PIMA Indians Diabetes dataset and for a lifetime-smoking-history contrast in NHANES. The highest estimated ungated utilities were 0.799 for the BMI policy using Bayesian GMM, 0.735 for the glucose policy using hard or membership-weighted FCM, and 0.775 for the smoking-history policy using K-means. All paired 95% confidence intervals for policy-risk differences included zero, and no comparison remained statistically significant after Holm adjustment. Bayesian pooling generally preserved ungated allocations, whereas Empirical Bernstein gating was more conservative. Policies with similar estimated utility could nevertheless prioritize different individuals. The findings should be interpreted as assumption-dependent decision-support evidence for hypothetical state contrasts rather than proof of intervention benefit.

</details>


### [217] [The Art of Not Forgetting A Local Learning Architecture for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26523)
*Ashmith Atmuri,Yashaswini Rao Bhogarajula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CMP（认知记忆基元）持续学习架构，通过稀疏关系编码、双层竞争记忆和局部更新来缓解灾难性遗忘，在字节级语言建模任务上相比基于EWC的Transformer遗忘更低，但单域准确率差距较大且在视觉基准上无效。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦持续学习/灾难性遗忘，任务为字节级语言建模，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化部署。虽然提及了视觉基准（null result），但整体与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化无任何直接或间接关联。

Reading priority: 1分，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：持续学习（continual learning）/ 领域增量学习（domain-incremental learning）；应用场景：字节级语言建模（byte-level language modeling）；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: CMP架构：稀疏关系编码（sparse relational codes）+ 双层竞争记忆（two-tier competitive memory）+ 局部更新（local updates），无需端到端反向传播。对比基线：参数匹配的Transformer + 在线弹性权重巩固（EWC）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明轻量化方法。局部更新（local learning）避免了端到端反向传播，可能隐含计算效率优势，但论文未从轻量化/模型压缩角度讨论。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。无星上/边缘部署可行性分析。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：字节级语言建模协议（15个域，domain-incremental）；视觉基准（未说明具体数据集名称）。评价指标：后向迁移（backward transfer）、遗忘率（forgetting）、单域准确率（single-domain accuracy）。

Experiments: 三种子复现的15域实验，CMP遗忘行为稳定；与Transformer+EWC的逐域对比和域序分析显示CMP遗忘更低；但CMP单域准确率显著低于Transformer；在视觉基准上结果为null；与独立精度提升机制结合失败。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化完全无关；2. 单域准确率显著低于Transformer基线；3. 视觉基准上结果为null，泛化能力有限；4. 无法与独立精度提升机制结合；5. 仅在语言建模任务上验证，遥感视觉任务适用性存疑。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴价值极低。唯一可能值得注意的点：局部更新（local learning）避免端到端反向传播的思路，如果未来有人将其引入遥感目标检测的持续学习场景（如新类别增量检测），可能有一定启发，但论文本身未涉及检测任务，且视觉基准结果为null，风险较高。

Abstract: We introduce CMP (Cognitive Memory Primitive), a continual-learning architecture that repre?sents inputs as sparse relational codes, stores them in a two-tier competitive memory, and learns through local updates without end-to-end backpropagation through its feature-generating system. We investigate whether combining sparse representations, local learning, and persistent memory can reduce catastrophic forgetting relative to conventional backpropagation-based continual?learning approaches. On a controlled domain-incremental byte-level language modeling protocol, CMP demonstrates substantially lower backward transfer than a parameter-matched Trans?former trained with online Elastic Weight Consolidation (EWC). Across a three-seed replicated 15-domain experiment, CMP exhibits stable forgetting behavior, while separate head-to-head comparisons and domain-order analyses show consistently lower forgetting than the evaluated Transformer baseline under the reported experimental settings. We report these findings alongside a substantial single-domain accuracy gap relative to the Transformer, a null result on a vision benchmark, and a documented failure to combine CMP with an independent accuracy-improving mechanism, reflecting our commitment to reporting both positive and negative outcomes. These results suggest that the combination of sparse representations, local learning, and persistent memory is a promising direction for continual learning, while motivating further investigation into the respective roles of learning rules, representations, and architectural design in mitigating catastrophic forgetting.

</details>


### [218] [AgentGFM: A Graph Foundation Model with Node-Agent Information-Flow Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26533)
*Jingbo Cui,Jitao Zhao,Di Jin,Dongxiao He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AgentGFM，将图神经网络中的每个节点视为智能体，通过端到端可训练策略实现自适应信息流控制，解决图基础模型在不同拓扑结构下的迁移问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文是图基础模型(GFM)领域的通用方法，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。摘要中未出现任何与遥感图像、目标检测、轻量化部署相关的关键词。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：图节点级/图级/大规模迁移学习；应用场景：多领域图数据上的知识迁移；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: AgentGFM：将每个节点视为智能体(agent)，所有节点共享一个端到端可训练的决策策略(policy)，通过predict-act-observe-correct循环实现自适应信息传播。act阶段包含三个决策：source reception（源接收）、signal-channel selection（信号通道选择）、gain-aware node-wise halting（增益感知节点级停止）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文面向通用图学习任务，不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。仅提及在node-level、graph-level和large-scale transfer场景下进行了实验。

Experiments: 摘要仅声称在多种图拓扑场景下验证了有效性，未给出具体数值结果或对比方法。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关；2. 摘要未提供任何定量结果；3. 方法复杂度可能较高（每个节点作为agent进行决策），与轻量化目标相悖。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。如果强行关联，其"自适应信息流控制"的思想或许可类比到遥感检测网络中的自适应特征传播机制，但整体方向差异过大，不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) aim to learn transferable knowledge from multi-domain graphs and adapt to unseen scenarios. As a fundamental source of relational semantics in graphs, the transferability of topological patterns has long been central to GFM research. However, local structural patterns may vary across graphs and even among nodes within the same graph. Despite such structural variation, most existing GFMs rely on manually designed propagation schemes and apply them to new graphs largely unchanged. Such fixed schemes may not suit the diverse structural patterns of different nodes. This raises a key question: can each node autonomously determine how information should be propagated through the graph? We refer to this capability as information-flow control. Inspired by recent advances in agent technology, we formulate this problem as agent-based decision making and treat each node as an agent. Accordingly, we propose AgentGFM, in which all node agents follow a shared end-to-end trainable policy rather than using independent models. For adaptive information-flow control, each node interacts with the graph through a predict-act-observe-correct process. During the act stage, the node makes three decisions: source reception, signal-channel selection and gain-aware node-wise halting. The resulting observation is compared with the prediction and their discrepancy is used to correct the node state and guide subsequent interactions. Extensive experiments across node-level, graph-level and large-scale transfer scenarios demonstrate the effectiveness of AgentGFM across diverse graph topologies.

</details>


### [219] [From Tokens to Watt-hours: Analytical Energy Estimation for LLM Inference on Modern GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26571)
*Tina Vartziotis,Rodopi Kosteli,Elli Vartziotis,George Dasoulas,Michael Keckeisen,Konstantinos Skianis,Sotirios Kotsopoulos,Francesca Dominici*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无需直接硬件测量的LLM推理能耗估算方法，基于参数缩放、访存流量和硬件能耗系数对H100 GPU上的推理能耗进行解析建模。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦大语言模型(LLM)在GPU上的推理能耗估算，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM推理能耗估算；应用场景：AI系统可持续性评估、绿色编码分析；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 不涉及目标检测模型架构。论文分析对象为Transformer-based LLM，区分prompt prefill和autoregressive decoding阶段。

Lightweight method: 不涉及轻量化方法。论文是能耗估算方法，而非模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。论文针对NVIDIA H100-class数据中心级GPU，与星上FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等嵌入式部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集。评价指标为推理能耗估计值，分解为compute、parameter-access、KV-cache write、attention-read等组件。

Experiments: 摘要未说明具体实验结果和对比方法。论文声称提供transparent, reproducible, assumption-explicit的能耗近似估计。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星/目标检测领域；2. 不涉及任何模型轻量化方法；3. 仅针对LLM场景，与视觉检测模型无关；4. 估算结果不能替代物理测量；5. 仅适用于H100-class GPU，不具通用性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，可借鉴点有限：(1) 能耗分解思路（compute vs. memory access）可类比到遥感目标检测模型在星上NPU/FPGA的能耗建模；(2) FLOPs与访存流量联合建模的思路可用于评估轻量化检测模型在星上的实际能效。但整体方向差异过大，不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: The operational energy consumption of large language model (LLM) inference is becoming an increasingly important component of the environmental footprint of deployed AI systems. However, direct measurement of inference energy often requires hardware telemetry, power instrumentation, or infrastructure-specific monitoring, limiting its applicability in comparative studies, early-stage system design, and sustainability reporting. This report presents an analytically structured, empirically calibrated, GPU-level methodology for estimating LLM inference energy on NVIDIA H100-class accelerators without direct runtime measurement. The proposed estimator combines parameter-scaled transformer FLOP accounting, calibrated memory-traffic factors, and hardware-specific energy coefficients for FP16/BF16 tensor-core computation and high-bandwidth-memory movement. It explicitly separates prompt prefill from autoregressive decoding, enabling energy estimates for input tokens, output tokens, and complete inference requests. The methodology further decomposes total energy into compute, parameter-access, key-value-cache write, and attention-read components, allowing the scaling behavior with model size, context length, and generated-token count to be analyzed. The resulting estimates are not intended to replace physical power measurements; rather, they provide transparent, reproducible, and assumption-explicit approximations suitable for model comparison, green-coding analysis, and design-time evaluation of LLM inference workloads.

</details>


### [220] [Simultaneous Coverage and Efficiency Guarantee in Online Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26577)
*Rahul Vaze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应共形推断框架，在分布漂移下同时控制绝对覆盖误差和预测集效率，但完全不涉及遥感、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于统计学习中的在线共形预测理论，讨论分布漂移下的覆盖误差控制和预测集效率，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、卫星在轨部署或任何模型轻量化技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文是纯统计/机器学习理论工作，未涉及任何遥感场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文讨论的是共形预测的算法框架（ACI及其变体、滑动窗口分位数追踪器、分区ACI），而非神经网络模型架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文是理论性工作，未提及任何具体数据集或实验评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明。论文仅给出理论推导和保证，未报告实验对比或消融结果。

Limitations: 论文是纯理论工作，未涉及任何实际应用场景验证；与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向无直接关联，无法直接借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。虽然论文的在线自适应推断思想（如滑动窗口分位数追踪、分区ACI）在理论上可能对遥感在轨场景中动态分布漂移下的置信度校准有启发，但距离实际的目标检测模型轻量化部署差距极大，不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: Adaptive conformal inference (ACI) of Gibbs and Cand{è}s and its variants are the standard approach to online conformal prediction under distribution shift, but they suffer from three fundamental limitations. First, their guarantees control only the \emph{signed} long-run coverage error: persistent miscoverage in one direction can be masked by compensating errors later, so a method can satisfy the theoretical guarantee while being badly wrong for extended periods. Second, existing guarantees say nothing about prediction-set size, so validity can be achieved trivially at the cost of unduly wide prediction sets. Third, the efficiency guarantees that do exist compare against a \emph{fixed} predictor chosen in hindsight, a benchmark that becomes increasingly less meaningful once the data-generating distribution shifts, since the very notion of an optimal threshold then changes over time.
  We consider a unified online learning framework that simultaneously controls absolute, non-cancelling coverage violation and prediction-set efficiency against a dynamically evolving benchmark for three important models. In the fully adversarial setting, exploiting the fact that the standard ACI update is exactly projected online gradient descent on the pinball loss, we derive simultaneous coverage and efficiency guarantees for arbitrary monotone Lipschitz efficiency objectives, with no distributional or {\it convexity} assumptions. In the stochastic setting with full-score feedback, we propose a sliding-window quantile tracker and establish a matching minimax lower bound showing our algorithm is rate-optimal. In the covariate-dependent stochastic setting, we develop a partitioned ACI algorithm that tracks a function-valued oracle threshold, and derive simultaneous coverage and efficiency guarantees.

</details>


### [221] [Benchmarking ConvLSTM for One-Day-Ahead IMDAA Rainfall-Field Prediction across Four Indian Cities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26581)
*Tanmay Ghosh,Shaurabh Anand,Rakesh Gomaji Nannewar,Nithin Nagaraj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究ConvLSTM在日尺度降水预报中是否优于简单基线方法，发现ConvLSTM并未一致优于FC-LSTM或持久性预测，质疑了卷积循环结构在小规模日降水网格数据上的必要性。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于气象降水预报（precipitation forecasting），使用IMDAA再分析数据，完全不涉及遥感卫星图像、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署。虽然ConvLSTM是一种时空序列模型，但论文讨论的是气象预报而非遥感目标检测，且未涉及任何轻量化技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读全文。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：降水场预测（precipitation forecasting）；场景：气象预报，针对印度四个城市（Bengaluru, Delhi, Kolkata, Mumbai）；传感器/数据类型：IMDAA再分析网格数据（非遥感卫星图像）；目标类别：无目标检测任务。

Model architecture: ConvLSTM（卷积长短期记忆网络）、FC-LSTM（全连接LSTM）、持久性预测（persistence）、朴素方法、统计方法、树模型、神经网络等十种方法。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Indian Monsoon Data Assimilation and Analysis (IMDAA) 再分析场，1998-2020年6-9月，四个城市；评价指标：域平均降雨误差（domain-mean rainfall error）、空间异常误差（spatial-anomaly error）、高降雨日阈值超越次数（threshold exceedances）、检测性能（detection performance）。

Experiments: ConvLSTM未一致优于简单基线；FC-LSTM在三个城市取得最低域平均降雨误差；持久性预测在德里表现最佳；ConvLSTM仅在孟买取得最低空间异常误差；神经网络模型低估降雨量且高降雨日预测不足；持久性预测在所有城市取得最高检测性能。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. 仅使用小规模日降水网格数据，结论不适用于高分辨率遥感图像；3. 未讨论模型效率、参数量或推理速度；4. 未涉及任何在轨或边缘部署场景。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能的启发是：论文强调了强基准测试（strong benchmarking）的重要性——在遥感目标检测轻量化研究中，应同样对简单基线（如直接剪枝、直接量化）与复杂方法进行公平对比，避免过度设计。

Abstract: Convolutional long short-term memory networks (ConvLSTMs) are widely used for precipitation forecasting, but most evidence for their performance comes from dense, high-frequency radar sequences. This study tests whether convolutional recurrence improves one-day-ahead rainfall-field prediction on small daily reanalysis grids. Indian Monsoon Data Assimilation and Analysis (IMDAA) fields for June-September 1998-2020 were analysed for Bengaluru, Delhi, Kolkata and Mumbai. Ten naive, statistical, tree-based and neural approaches were compared using atmospheric-only and rainfall-history-plus-atmospheric inputs. Performance was assessed for complete fields, domain-mean rainfall, spatial anomalies and high-rainfall days.
  ConvLSTM did not consistently outperform simpler alternatives. FC-LSTM produced the numerically lowest domain-mean rainfall error in Bengaluru, Kolkata and Mumbai, whereas persistence performed best in Delhi. ConvLSTM produced the numerically lowest spatial-anomaly error only in Mumbai, where rainfall fields showed greater short-term spatial continuity and rainfall-history inputs improved all three neural architectures. The difference between ConvLSTM and FC-LSTM was nevertheless small. Neural models underestimated rainfall magnitude and predicted too few threshold exceedances on high-rainfall days, while persistence achieved the highest detection performance in every city. Post-hoc analyses showed that the selected models were most sensitive to the latest input day, with broader recent-lag sensitivity in Mumbai. These findings show that gridded inputs alone do not justify ConvLSTM and that architecture choice should follow strong benchmarking across average, spatial and high-rainfall performance.

</details>


### [222] [Uncertainty-Guided LLM Semantic Augmentation for Heterogeneous Treatment Effect Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26599)
*Jialu Xu,Mengkun Liang,Guannan Liu,Xiaojie Mao,Junjie Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CURL方法，利用预训练大语言模型的不确定性引导表征学习，用于异质处理效应估计（CATE），属于因果推断/个性化干预领域。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化完全无关。论文研究的是因果推断中的条件平均处理效应（CATE）估计问题，属于统计学习/因果机器学习方向，应用场景为个性化推荐和精准医疗，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化无任何交集。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：异质处理效应估计（CATE估计）；场景：个性化促销、精准医疗；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: CURL（Causal Uncertainty-guided Representation Learning）作为插件式适配器，利用冻结的LLM通过两种角色条件提示构建assignment-oriented和heterogeneity-oriented表征，并通过分离通路路由。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。且论文使用冻结LLM，计算开销较大，与星上/边缘部署方向背道而驰。

Datasets and metrics: 四个基准数据集（具体名称摘要未说明）；评价指标摘要未说明具体名称。

Experiments: 在四个基准上，CURL在大多数设置下提升了十种宿主学习器的性能；进行了消融、细化动态、路由重分配和探针分析。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术点。论文依赖冻结LLM，计算资源需求大，与轻量化部署目标相反。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文属于因果推断领域，与遥感目标检测和模型轻量化无任何技术交叉点。

Abstract: Estimating heterogeneous treatment effects is central to targeted interventions, such as personalized promotions and precision medicine. We focus on the conditional average treatment effect (CATE), a standard estimand for characterizing such heterogeneity. Even under standard identification conditions, finite-sample CATE estimation requires learning the nuisance structure for covariate adjustment and treatment-effect heterogeneity, often together with an effective representation of X. Raw numerical and categorical encodings can leave semantic relations and higher-order interactions implicit, making this joint task locally unstable. A motivating study further shows that this instability appears through partially separable assignment- and heterogeneity-side channels. Building on this observation, we propose CURL (Causal Uncertainty-guided Representation Learning), a plug-in adapter that uses estimator uncertainty to allocate pretrained semantic capacity to locally unstable units. CURL queries a frozen LLM through two role-conditioned prompts, constructs assignment- and heterogeneity-oriented representations from the observed covariates, and routes them through separated pathways. On four benchmarks, CURL improves ten host learners in most settings, while ablation, refinement-dynamics, route-reassignment, and probe analyses support the intended design and roles of the two channels.

</details>


### [223] [FedWeave: Rethinking the Unit of Specialization in Heterogeneous Federated MoE-LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26618)
*Donghang Duan,Xu Zheng,Lizong Zhang,Chong Mu,Meng Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedWeave框架，通过非对称聚合（专家聚合与路由器优化分离）解决联邦PEFT中跨任务干扰问题，实现LLM在异构私有数据上的协作微调。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于联邦学习场景下大语言模型（LLM）的参数高效微调（PEFT），涉及MoE-LoRA、专家路由、跨任务干扰等NLP领域问题，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：联邦学习下的LLM参数高效微调（PEFT）；应用场景：分布式私有数据协作训练；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 基于MoE-LoRA架构，包含专家（LoRA experts）和条件路由器（conditional routing），提出非对称聚合策略（asymmetric aggregation），将专家聚合与路由器优化分离。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明轻量化方法。文中提及的稀疏推理（sparse inference with one active expert）属于推理加速手段，但并非面向遥感模型轻量化设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。稀疏推理提及但无具体数值。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。仅提及在异构多任务基准（heterogeneous multi-task benchmark）上使用主流LLM骨干网络进行实验。

Experiments: 在异构多任务基准上，FedWeave一致优于强基线方法（strong baselines），消融实验验证了设计有效性。理论分析解释了非对称聚合的优势，包括控制专家收敛、识别共识误差、界定稀疏推理风险。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关；2. 仅针对LLM的联邦PEFT场景，不涉及视觉任务；3. 未提供任何计算效率指标（FLOPs、延迟等）。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能相关的点是"稀疏推理"（sparse inference with one active expert）的思路，即在推理时仅激活部分子网络以降低计算量，类似于MoE在遥感检测中的稀疏激活策略。但论文本身不涉及遥感或视觉检测，直接借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Federated PEFT enables LLMs to collaboratively adapt to decentralized private data without sharing raw examples. However, task heterogeneity across clients can cause cross-task interference and gradient conflicts during aggregation. Federated MoE-LoRA addresses this challenge through specialized LoRA experts and conditional routing. Yet existing methods typically specialize at client granularity, implicitly assuming task-coherent clients. Our core insight is that experts need purity, namely pattern-coherent updates that preserve specialization, whereas routers need contrast, namely mixed-task observations that support expert comparison. We propose FedWeave, a framework that adopts asymmetric aggregation, separating expert aggregation from router optimization to meet these two requirements. FedWeave uses unsupervised prototype discovery to form local buckets and align them across clients, enabling prototype-level expert aggregation while retaining mixed-task client trajectories for router training. At inference, FedWeave performs sparse inference with one active expert while preserving nearly all soft-routing performance. Our theoretical analysis explains why asymmetric aggregation is advantageous: it controls expert convergence in stationarity through off-pattern contamination, identifies the consensus error induced by fragmented router trajectories, and bounds sparse-inference risk. On a heterogeneous multi-task benchmark with mainstream LLM backbones, FedWeave consistently outperforms strong baselines, while ablations verify the effectiveness of our design.

</details>


### [224] [Enhancing Automated Machine Learning via Homogeneous Train-Test Splitting Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26625)
*Yearn Tan Yin Tze,Charles Grellois*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究数据集划分策略（train-test split）对AutoML模型评估的影响，提出了一种以统计相似性为优化目标的Optimised-Distribution划分方法。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于通用机器学习中的数据集划分策略和AutoML评估问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文使用UCI benchmark数据集，不涉及遥感场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何目标检测或深度学习模型架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或星上/边缘部署相关内容。

Datasets and metrics: 使用15个UCI benchmark数据集。评价指标包括chi-square、Kolmogorov-Smirnov、Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD)等统计相似性度量。不涉及遥感数据集或目标检测指标（如mAP、FPS等）。

Experiments: 比较了五种划分策略（random splitting, stratified sampling, Kennard-Stone, Duplex, SPXY），提出Optimised-Distribution方法，在MMD相似性上达到89.0%的均值。不涉及目标检测实验。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的模型结构、轻量化方法或部署信息。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文讨论的是数据集划分的统计相似性问题，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Accurate model evaluation in machine learning depends critically on how datasets are split into training and testing subsets. Standard random splitting assumes that both partitions share the same underlying distribution, an assumption often violated in datasets with class imbalance, natural clustering, or spatial autocorrelation. This paper investigates the role of statistical similarity in train-test splitting and its consequences for AutoML model evaluation. Five established strategies are compared across fifteen UCI benchmark datasets: random splitting, stratified sampling, Kennard-Stone, Duplex, and SPXY. Similarity is assessed using chi-square, Kolmogorov-Smirnov, and Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD) tests. Geometry-based methods consistently produce near-zero MMD scores, introducing instability into downstream performance estimates. The proposed Optimised-Distribution method treats similarity as an explicit optimisation objective and achieves the highest mean MMD similarity, 89.0%, across all strategies evaluated.

</details>


### [225] [Understanding Context Sampling in TabPFN on Small Tabular Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26628)
*Mohammed Abdullah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究TabPFN在小表格数据上的上下文选择策略（上下文大小、选择方法）对预测稳定性、准确率和选择成本的影响，发现随机采样因提供特征空间覆盖而优于昂贵的K-Means和最远点采样。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究对象是TabPFN在小表格数据上的上下文学习策略，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化或部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：表格数据分类（in-context learning）；应用场景：小样本表格数据；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: TabPFN（基于Transformer的上下文学习分类器），非遥感目标检测模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 15个OpenML小表格数据集；评价指标：AUC、AUC变异系数（coefficient of variation）；选择成本（selection cost）。

Experiments: 对比了不同上下文大小（k=16 vs 更大）、不同上下文选择方法（随机采样、K-Means、最远点采样）对AUC及稳定性的影响。发现大上下文更准确更稳定；随机采样与K-Means/最远点采样准确率相近但选择成本低2-3个数量级。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关，无任何可借鉴的模型结构、轻量化方法或部署策略。研究场景为小表格数据分类，非视觉/遥感任务。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文不涉及遥感、目标检测、轻量化或星上部署的任何方面。

Abstract: TabPFN performs classification through in-context learning: it conditions on a set of labeled training rows (the context, or prototypes) and predicts test labels without gradient updates. On small tabular datasets, practitioners must still choose the context size and which rows constitute the context. We study how these choices affect prediction stability, accuracy, and selection cost using repeated context sampling on 15 OpenML datasets. Specifically, we investigate (i) whether larger contexts reduce prediction variability across random draws, (ii) whether accuracy depends on preserving the training distribution or on feature-space coverage, and (iii) whether expensive selection methods such as K-Means and farthest-point sampling provide benefits over uniform random sampling.
  We find that larger contexts are both more accurate and substantially more stable, with AUC coefficient of variation decreasing from roughly 6 to 18% at k=16 to 1 to 4% at larger context sizes on datasets with room for improvement. Although accuracy correlates with distribution representativeness in random contexts, controlled experiments show that matching feature means alone can reduce accuracy by up to 0.5 AUC because it reduces context diversity. Mixed-effects analysis identifies diversity and coverage, rather than feature-mean matching, as the stronger predictor of accuracy (diversity beta=+0.23, p=3x10^-12; feature-mean shift beta=-0.01, p=0.71). K-Means and farthest-point sampling achieve similar accuracy to random selection while requiring two to three orders of magnitude more selection cost. These results show that random sampling succeeds because it provides feature-space coverage in expectation, not because it reproduces the underlying data distribution.

</details>


### [226] [RAG-HAR+: Towards Cost-Efficient LLM-Based Human Activity Recognition for Edge Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26631)
*Hansi Karunarathna,Nirhoshan Sivaroopan,Chamara Madarasingha,Anura Jayasumana,Kanchana Thilakarathna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RAG-HAR+，一种基于检索增强生成的可穿戴传感器人体活动识别方法，通过检索优先策略减少对LLM推理的依赖，实现训练-free的HAR。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦可穿戴传感器人体活动识别(HAR)，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感相关领域；不涉及目标检测或轻量化模型压缩方法；不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：Human Activity Recognition (HAR)；场景：可穿戴传感器、移动感知、医疗康复、健身追踪；传感器类型：可穿戴传感器（非遥感传感器）；目标类别：人体活动类别（非遥感目标）。

Model architecture: RAG-HAR+架构包含：离线Retrieval Designer Agent（设计数据集特定特征组）、检索模块（多数投票）、LLM-based Ambiguity Resolver Agent（仅处理不确定样本）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等传统轻量化方法。论文通过"检索优先+LLM延迟决策"策略减少LLM推理调用次数和token消耗，属于推理阶段的计算优化，但与遥感模型轻量化无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要提及RAG-HAR移动端原型(mobile prototype)，展示在移动感知场景中的可行性。但未涉及星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT、参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 6个HAR基准数据集；评价指标：性能(competitive or improved performance)、LLM使用率、token消耗、推理时间。具体数据集名称和指标数值摘要未说明。

Experiments: 在6个HAR基准上，RAG-HAR+保持竞争性或更优性能，同时减少LLM使用、token消耗和推理时间。具体对比方法和消融实验细节摘要未说明。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 不涉及任何目标检测任务；3. 不涉及模型轻量化核心技术（剪枝、量化、蒸馏等）；4. 依赖LLM，不适合星上资源受限场景。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发：检索增强思路可类比遥感目标检测中的"检索辅助检测"或"知识库辅助推理"，但与本课题"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"的核心方向（模型压缩、加速、边缘部署）关联度极低。

Abstract: Human Activity Recognition (HAR) from wearable sensors supports applications in healthcare, rehabilitation, fitness tracking, and smart environments. Yet, existing deep learning approaches require dataset-specific training, large labeled corpora, and repeated adaptation to new sensor settings or activity taxonomies.
